snp1.1:0.2	Uragasutta
snp1.1:1.1	Yo uppatitaṁ vineti kodhaṁ,
snp1.1:1.2	Visaṭaṁ sappavisaṁva osadhehi;
snp1.1:1.3	So bhikkhu jahāti orapāraṁ,
snp1.1:1.4	Urago jiṇṇamivattacaṁ purāṇaṁ.
snp1.1:2.1	Yo rāgamudacchidā asesaṁ,
snp1.1:2.2	Bhisapupphaṁva saroruhaṁ vigayha;
snp1.1:2.3	So bhikkhu jahāti orapāraṁ,
snp1.1:2.4	Urago jiṇṇamivattacaṁ purāṇaṁ.
snp1.1:3.1	Yo taṇhamudacchidā asesaṁ,
snp1.1:3.2	Saritaṁ sīghasaraṁ visosayitvā;
snp1.1:3.3	So bhikkhu jahāti orapāraṁ,

snp1.1:4.1	Yo mānamudabbadhī asesaṁ,

snp1.1:5.1	Yo nājjhagamā bhavesu sāraṁ,
snp1.1:5.2	Vicinaṁ pupphamivā udumbaresu;
snp1.1:5.3	So bhikkhu jahāti orapāraṁ,
snp1.1:5.4	Urago jiṇṇamivattacaṁ purāṇaṁ.
snp1.1:6.1	Yassantarato na santi kopā,
snp1.1:6.2	Itibhavābhavatañca vītivatto;
snp1.1:6.3	So bhikkhu jahāti orapāraṁ,
snp1.1:6.4	Urago jiṇṇamivattacaṁ purāṇaṁ.
snp1.1:7.1	Yassa vitakkā vidhūpitā,
snp1.1:7.2	Ajjhattaṁ suvikappitā asesā;
snp1.1:7.3	So bhikkhu jahāti orapāraṁ,
snp1.1:7.4	Urago jiṇṇamivattacaṁ purāṇaṁ.
snp1.1:8.1	Yo nāccasārī na paccasārī,
snp1.1:8.2	Sabbaṁ accagamā imaṁ papañcaṁ;
snp1.1:8.3	So bhikkhu jahāti orapāraṁ,

snp1.1:13.2	Sabbaṁ vitathamidanti vītamoho;

snp1.1:14.1	Yassānusayā na santi keci,
snp1.1:14.2	Mūlā ca akusalā samūhatāse;

snp1.2:0.1	Sutta Nipāta 1.2
snp1.2:0.2	Dhaniyasutta
snp1.2:1.1	“Pakkodano duddhakhīrohamasmi,
snp1.2:1.2	(iti dhaniyo gopo)
snp1.2:1.3	Anutīre mahiyā samānavāso;
snp1.2:1.4	Channā kuṭi āhito gini,
snp1.2:1.5	Atha ce patthayasī pavassa deva”.
snp1.2:2.1	“Akkodhano vigatakhilohamasmi,
snp1.2:2.2	(iti bhagavā)
snp1.2:2.3	Anutīre mahiyekarattivāso;
snp1.2:2.4	Vivaṭā kuṭi nibbuto gini,
snp1.2:2.5	Atha ce patthayasī pavassa deva”.
snp1.2:3.1	“Andhakamakasā na vijjare,
snp1.2:3.2	(iti dhaniyo gopo)
snp1.2:3.3	Kacche rūḷhatiṇe caranti gāvo;
snp1.2:3.4	Vuṭṭhimpi saheyyumāgataṁ,
snp1.2:3.5	Atha ce patthayasī pavassa deva”.
snp1.2:4.1	“Baddhāsi bhisī susaṅkhatā,
snp1.2:4.2	(iti bhagavā)
snp1.2:4.3	Tiṇṇo pāragato vineyya oghaṁ;
snp1.2:4.4	Attho bhisiyā na vijjati,
snp1.2:4.5	Atha ce patthayasī pavassa deva”.
snp1.2:5.1	“Gopī mama assavā alolā,
snp1.2:5.2	(iti dhaniyo gopo)
snp1.2:5.3	Dīgharattaṁ saṁvāsiyā manāpā;
snp1.2:5.4	Tassā na suṇāmi kiñci pāpaṁ,
snp1.2:5.5	Atha ce patthayasī pavassa deva”.
snp1.2:6.1	“Cittaṁ mama assavaṁ vimuttaṁ,
snp1.2:6.2	(iti bhagavā)
snp1.2:6.3	Dīgharattaṁ paribhāvitaṁ sudantaṁ;
snp1.2:6.4	Pāpaṁ pana me na vijjati,
snp1.2:6.5	Atha ce patthayasī pavassa deva”.
snp1.2:7.1	“Attavetanabhatohamasmi,
snp1.2:7.2	(iti dhaniyo gopo)
snp1.2:7.3	Puttā ca me samāniyā arogā;
snp1.2:7.4	Tesaṁ na suṇāmi kiñci pāpaṁ,
snp1.2:7.5	Atha ce patthayasī pavassa deva”.
snp1.2:8.1	“Nāhaṁ bhatakosmi kassaci,
snp1.2:8.2	(iti bhagavā)
snp1.2:8.3	Nibbiṭṭhena carāmi sabbaloke;
snp1.2:8.4	Attho bhatiyā na vijjati,
snp1.2:8.5	Atha ce patthayasī pavassa deva”.
snp1.2:9.1	“Atthi vasā atthi dhenupā,
snp1.2:9.2	(iti dhaniyo gopo)
snp1.2:9.3	Godharaṇiyo paveṇiyopi atthi;
snp1.2:9.4	Usabhopi gavampatīdha atthi,
snp1.2:9.5	Atha ce patthayasī pavassa deva”.
snp1.2:10.1	“Natthi vasā natthi dhenupā,
snp1.2:10.2	(iti bhagavā)
snp1.2:10.3	Godharaṇiyo paveṇiyopi natthi;
snp1.2:10.4	Usabhopi gavampatīdha natthi,
snp1.2:10.5	Atha ce patthayasī pavassa deva”.
snp1.2:11.1	“Khilā nikhātā asampavedhī,
snp1.2:11.2	(iti dhaniyo gopo)
snp1.2:11.3	Dāmā muñjamayā navā susaṇṭhānā;
snp1.2:11.4	Na hi sakkhinti dhenupāpi chettuṁ,
snp1.2:11.5	Atha ce patthayasī pavassa deva”.
snp1.2:12.1	“Usabhoriva chetva bandhanāni,
snp1.2:12.2	(iti bhagavā)
snp1.2:12.3	Nāgo pūtilataṁva dālayitvā;
snp1.2:12.4	Nāhaṁ punupessaṁ gabbhaseyyaṁ,
snp1.2:12.5	Atha ce patthayasī pavassa deva”.
snp1.2:13.1	Ninnañca thalañca pūrayanto,
snp1.2:13.2	Mahāmegho pavassi tāvadeva;
snp1.2:13.3	Sutvā devassa vassato,
snp1.2:13.4	Imamatthaṁ dhaniyo abhāsatha.
snp1.2:14.1	“Lābhā vata no anappakā,
snp1.2:14.2	Ye mayaṁ bhagavantaṁ addasāma;
snp1.2:14.3	Saraṇaṁ taṁ upema cakkhuma,
snp1.2:14.4	Satthā no hohi tuvaṁ mahāmuni.
snp1.2:15.1	Gopī ca ahañca assavā,
snp1.2:15.2	Brahmacariyaṁ sugate carāmase;
snp1.2:15.3	Jātimaraṇassa pāragū,
snp1.2:15.4	Dukkhassantakarā bhavāmase”.
snp1.2:16.1	“Nandati puttehi puttimā,
snp1.2:16.2	(iti māro pāpimā)
snp1.2:16.3	Gomā gohi tatheva nandati;
snp1.2:16.4	Upadhī hi narassa nandanā,
snp1.2:16.5	Na hi so nandati yo nirūpadhi”.
snp1.2:17.1	“Socati puttehi puttimā,
snp1.2:17.2	(iti bhagavā)
snp1.2:17.3	Gomā gohi tatheva socati;
snp1.2:17.4	Upadhī hi narassa socanā,
snp1.2:17.5	Na hi so socati yo nirūpadhī”ti.
snp1.2:17.6	Dhaniyasuttaṁ dutiyaṁ.
snp1.3:0.1	Sutta Nipāta 1.3
snp1.3:0.2	Khaggavisāṇasutta
snp1.3:1.1	“Sabbesu bhūtesu nidhāya daṇḍaṁ,
snp1.3:1.2	Aviheṭhayaṁ aññatarampi tesaṁ;
snp1.3:1.3	Na puttamiccheyya kuto sahāyaṁ,
snp1.3:1.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:2.1	“Saṁsaggajātassa bhavanti snehā,
snp1.3:2.2	Snehanvayaṁ dukkhamidaṁ pahoti;
snp1.3:2.3	Ādīnavaṁ snehajaṁ pekkhamāno,
snp1.3:2.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:3.1	“Mitte suhajje anukampamāno,
snp1.3:3.2	Hāpeti atthaṁ paṭibaddhacitto;
snp1.3:3.3	Etaṁ bhayaṁ santhave pekkhamāno,
snp1.3:3.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:4.1	“Vaṁso visālova yathā visatto,
snp1.3:4.2	Puttesu dāresu ca yā apekkhā;
snp1.3:4.3	Vaṁsakkaḷīrova asajjamāno,
snp1.3:4.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:5.1	“Migo araññamhi yathā abaddho,
snp1.3:5.2	Yenicchakaṁ gacchati gocarāya;
snp1.3:5.3	Viññū naro seritaṁ pekkhamāno,
snp1.3:5.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:6.1	“Āmantanā hoti sahāyamajjhe,
snp1.3:6.2	Vāse ṭhāne gamane cārikāya;
snp1.3:6.3	Anabhijjhitaṁ seritaṁ pekkhamāno,
snp1.3:6.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:7.1	“Khiḍḍā ratī hoti sahāyamajjhe,
snp1.3:7.2	Puttesu ca vipulaṁ hoti pemaṁ;
snp1.3:7.3	Piyavippayogaṁ vijigucchamāno,
snp1.3:7.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:8.1	“Cātuddiso appaṭigho ca hoti,
snp1.3:8.2	Santussamāno itarītarena;
snp1.3:8.3	Parissayānaṁ sahitā achambhī,
snp1.3:8.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:9.1	“Dussaṅgahā pabbajitāpi eke,
snp1.3:9.2	Atho gahaṭṭhā gharamāvasantā;
snp1.3:9.3	Appossukko paraputtesu hutvā,
snp1.3:9.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:10.1	“Oropayitvā gihibyañjanāni,
snp1.3:10.2	Sañchinnapatto yathā koviḷāro;
snp1.3:10.3	Chetvāna vīro gihibandhanāni,
snp1.3:10.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:11.1	“Sace labhetha nipakaṁ sahāyaṁ,
snp1.3:11.2	Saddhiṁ caraṁ sādhuvihāridhīraṁ;
snp1.3:11.3	Abhibhuyya sabbāni parissayāni,
snp1.3:11.4	Careyya tenattamano satīmā”.
snp1.3:12.1	“No ce labhetha nipakaṁ sahāyaṁ,
snp1.3:12.2	Saddhiṁ caraṁ sādhuvihāridhīraṁ;
snp1.3:12.3	Rājāva raṭṭhaṁ vijitaṁ pahāya,
snp1.3:12.4	Eko care mātaṅgaraññeva nāgo”.
snp1.3:13.1	“Addhā pasaṁsāma sahāyasampadaṁ,
snp1.3:13.2	Seṭṭhā samā sevitabbā sahāyā;
snp1.3:13.3	Ete aladdhā anavajjabhojī,
snp1.3:13.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:14.1	“Disvā suvaṇṇassa pabhassarāni,
snp1.3:14.2	Kammāraputtena suniṭṭhitāni;
snp1.3:14.3	Saṅghaṭṭamānāni duve bhujasmiṁ,
snp1.3:14.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:15.1	“Evaṁ dutīyena sahā mamassa,
snp1.3:15.2	Vācābhilāpo abhisajjanā vā;
snp1.3:15.3	Etaṁ bhayaṁ āyatiṁ pekkhamāno,
snp1.3:15.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:16.1	“Kāmā hi citrā madhurā manoramā,
snp1.3:16.2	Virūparūpena mathenti cittaṁ;
snp1.3:16.3	Ādīnavaṁ kāmaguṇesu disvā,
snp1.3:16.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:17.1	“Ītī ca gaṇḍo ca upaddavo ca,
snp1.3:17.2	Rogo ca sallañca bhayañca metaṁ;
snp1.3:17.3	Etaṁ bhayaṁ kāmaguṇesu disvā,
snp1.3:17.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:18.1	“Sītañca uṇhañca khudaṁ pipāsaṁ,
snp1.3:18.2	Vātātape ḍaṁsasarīsape ca;
snp1.3:18.3	Sabbānipetāni abhisambhavitvā,
snp1.3:18.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:19.1	“Nāgova yūthāni vivajjayitvā,
snp1.3:19.2	Sañjātakhandho padumī uḷāro;
snp1.3:19.3	Yathābhirantaṁ viharaṁ araññe,
snp1.3:19.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:20.1	“Aṭṭhānataṁ saṅgaṇikāratassa,
snp1.3:20.2	Yaṁ phassaye sāmayikaṁ vimuttiṁ;
snp1.3:20.3	Ādiccabandhussa vaco nisamma,
snp1.3:20.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:21.1	“Diṭṭhīvisūkāni upātivatto,
snp1.3:21.2	Patto niyāmaṁ paṭiladdhamaggo;
snp1.3:21.3	Uppannañāṇomhi anaññaneyyo,
snp1.3:21.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:22.1	“Nillolupo nikkuho nippipāso,
snp1.3:22.2	Nimmakkho niddhantakasāvamoho;
snp1.3:22.3	Nirāsayo sabbaloke bhavitvā,
snp1.3:22.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:23.1	“Pāpaṁ sahāyaṁ parivajjayetha,
snp1.3:23.2	Anatthadassiṁ visame niviṭṭhaṁ;
snp1.3:23.3	Sayaṁ na seve pasutaṁ pamattaṁ,
snp1.3:23.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:24.1	“Bahussutaṁ dhammadharaṁ bhajetha,
snp1.3:24.2	Mittaṁ uḷāraṁ paṭibhānavantaṁ;
snp1.3:24.3	Aññāya atthāni vineyya kaṅkhaṁ,
snp1.3:24.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:25.1	“Khiḍḍaṁ ratiṁ kāmasukhañca loke,
snp1.3:25.2	Analaṅkaritvā anapekkhamāno;
snp1.3:25.3	Vibhūsanaṭṭhānā virato saccavādī,
snp1.3:25.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:26.1	“Puttañca dāraṁ pitarañca mātaraṁ,
snp1.3:26.2	Dhanāni dhaññāni ca bandhavāni;
snp1.3:26.3	Hitvāna kāmāni yathodhikāni,
snp1.3:26.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:27.1	“Saṅgo eso parittamettha sokhyaṁ,
snp1.3:27.2	Appassādo dukkhamettha bhiyyo;
snp1.3:27.3	Gaḷo eso iti ñatvā matīmā,
snp1.3:27.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:28.1	“Sandālayitvāna saṁyojanāni,
snp1.3:28.2	Jālaṁva bhetvā salilambucārī;
snp1.3:28.3	Aggīva daḍḍhaṁ anivattamāno,
snp1.3:28.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:29.1	“Okkhittacakkhu na ca pādalolo,
snp1.3:29.2	Guttindriyo rakkhitamānasāno;
snp1.3:29.3	Anavassuto apariḍayhamāno,
snp1.3:29.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:30.1	“Ohārayitvā gihibyañjanāni,
snp1.3:30.2	Sañchannapatto yathā pārichatto;
snp1.3:30.3	Kāsāyavattho abhinikkhamitvā,
snp1.3:30.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:31.1	“Rasesu gedhaṁ akaraṁ alolo,
snp1.3:31.2	Anaññaposī sapadānacārī;
snp1.3:31.3	Kule kule appaṭibaddhacitto,
snp1.3:31.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:32.1	“Pahāya pañcāvaraṇāni cetaso,
snp1.3:32.2	Upakkilese byapanujja sabbe;
snp1.3:32.3	Anissito chetva sinehadosaṁ,
snp1.3:32.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:33.1	“Vipiṭṭhikatvāna sukhaṁ dukhañca,
snp1.3:33.2	Pubbeva ca somanassadomanassaṁ;
snp1.3:33.3	Laddhānupekkhaṁ samathaṁ visuddhaṁ,
snp1.3:33.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:34.1	“Āraddhaviriyo paramatthapattiyā,
snp1.3:34.2	Alīnacitto akusītavutti;
snp1.3:34.3	Daḷhanikkamo thāmabalūpapanno,
snp1.3:34.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:35.1	“Paṭisallānaṁ jhānamariñcamāno,
snp1.3:35.2	Dhammesu niccaṁ anudhammacārī;
snp1.3:35.3	Ādīnavaṁ sammasitā bhavesu,
snp1.3:35.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:36.1	“Taṇhakkhayaṁ patthayamappamatto,
snp1.3:36.2	Aneḷamūgo sutavā satīmā;
snp1.3:36.3	Saṅkhātadhammo niyato padhānavā,
snp1.3:36.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:37.1	“Sīhova saddesu asantasanto,
snp1.3:37.2	Vātova jālamhi asajjamāno;
snp1.3:37.3	Padumaṁva toyena alippamāno,
snp1.3:37.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:38.1	“Sīho yathā dāṭhabalī pasayha,
snp1.3:38.2	Rājā migānaṁ abhibhuyya cārī;
snp1.3:38.3	Sevetha pantāni senāsanāni,
snp1.3:38.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:39.1	“Mettaṁ upekkhaṁ karuṇaṁ vimuttiṁ,
snp1.3:39.2	Āsevamāno muditañca kāle;
snp1.3:39.3	Sabbena lokena avirujjhamāno,
snp1.3:39.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:40.1	“Rāgañca dosañca pahāya mohaṁ,
snp1.3:40.2	Sandālayitvāna saṁyojanāni;
snp1.3:40.3	Asantasaṁ jīvitasaṅkhayamhi,
snp1.3:40.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:41.1	“Bhajanti sevanti ca kāraṇatthā,
snp1.3:41.2	Nikkāraṇā dullabhā ajja mittā;
snp1.3:41.3	Attaṭṭhapaññā asucī manussā,
snp1.3:41.4	Eko care khaggavisāṇakappo”.
snp1.3:41.5	Khaggavisāṇasuttaṁ tatiyaṁ.
snp1.4:0.1	Sutta Nipāta 1.4
snp1.4:0.2	Kasibhāradvājasutta
snp1.4:1.1	Evaṁ me sutaṁ—
snp1.4:1.2	ekaṁ samayaṁ bhagavā magadhesu viharati dakkhiṇāgirismiṁ ekanāḷāyaṁ brāhmaṇagāme.
snp1.4:1.3	Tena kho pana samayena kasibhāradvājassa brāhmaṇassa pañcamattāni naṅgalasatāni payuttāni honti vappakāle.
snp1.4:1.4	Atha kho bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṁ nivāsetvā pattacīvaramādāya yena kasibhāradvājassa brāhmaṇassa kammanto tenupasaṅkami.
snp1.4:1.5	Tena kho pana samayena kasibhāradvājassa brāhmaṇassa parivesanā vattati.
snp1.4:1.6	Atha kho bhagavā yena parivesanā tenupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā ekamantaṁ aṭṭhāsi.
snp1.4:2.1	Addasā kho kasibhāradvājo brāhmaṇo bhagavantaṁ piṇḍāya ṭhitaṁ.
snp1.4:2.2	Disvāna bhagavantaṁ etadavoca:
snp1.4:2.3	“ahaṁ kho, samaṇa, kasāmi ca vapāmi ca; kasitvā ca vapitvā ca bhuñjāmi.
snp1.4:2.4	Tvampi, samaṇa, kasassu ca vapassu ca; kasitvā ca vapitvā ca bhuñjassū”ti.
snp1.4:3.1	“Ahampi kho, brāhmaṇa, kasāmi ca vapāmi ca; kasitvā ca vapitvā ca bhuñjāmī”ti.
snp1.4:3.2	“Na kho pana mayaṁ passāma bhoto gotamassa yugaṁ vā naṅgalaṁ vā phālaṁ vā pācanaṁ vā balībadde vā.
snp1.4:3.3	Atha ca pana bhavaṁ gotamo evamāha:
snp1.4:3.4	‘ahampi kho, brāhmaṇa, kasāmi ca vapāmi ca; kasitvā ca vapitvā ca bhuñjāmī’”ti.
snp1.4:4.1	Atha kho kasibhāradvājo brāhmaṇo bhagavantaṁ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi:
snp1.4:5.1	“Kassako paṭijānāsi,
snp1.4:5.2	na ca passāma te kasiṁ;
snp1.4:5.3	Kasiṁ no pucchito brūhi,
snp1.4:5.4	yathā jānemu te kasiṁ”.
snp1.4:6.1	“Saddhā bījaṁ tapo vuṭṭhi,
snp1.4:6.2	paññā me yuganaṅgalaṁ;
snp1.4:6.3	Hirī īsā mano yottaṁ,
snp1.4:6.4	sati me phālapācanaṁ.
snp1.4:7.1	Kāyagutto vacīgutto,
snp1.4:7.2	āhāre udare yato;
snp1.4:7.3	Saccaṁ karomi niddānaṁ,
snp1.4:7.4	soraccaṁ me pamocanaṁ.
snp1.4:8.1	Vīriyaṁ me dhuradhorayhaṁ,
snp1.4:8.2	yogakkhemādhivāhanaṁ;
snp1.4:8.3	Gacchati anivattantaṁ,
snp1.4:8.4	yattha gantvā na socati.
snp1.4:9.1	Evamesā kasī kaṭṭhā,
snp1.4:9.2	Sā hoti amatapphalā;
snp1.4:9.3	Etaṁ kasiṁ kasitvāna,
snp1.4:9.4	Sabbadukkhā pamuccatī”ti.
snp1.4:10.1	Atha kho kasibhāradvājo brāhmaṇo mahatiyā kaṁsapātiyā pāyasaṁ vaḍḍhetvā bhagavato upanāmesi:
snp1.4:10.2	“bhuñjatu bhavaṁ gotamo pāyasaṁ.
snp1.4:10.3	Kassako bhavaṁ;
snp1.4:10.4	yañhi bhavaṁ gotamo amatapphalaṁ kasiṁ kasatī”ti.
snp1.4:11.1	“Gāthābhigītaṁ me abhojaneyyaṁ,
snp1.4:11.2	Sampassataṁ brāhmaṇa nesa dhammo;
snp1.4:11.3	Gāthābhigītaṁ panudanti buddhā,
snp1.4:11.4	Dhamme satī brāhmaṇa vuttiresā.
snp1.4:12.1	Aññena ca kevalinaṁ mahesiṁ,
snp1.4:12.2	Khīṇāsavaṁ kukkuccavūpasantaṁ;
snp1.4:12.3	Annena pānena upaṭṭhahassu,
snp1.4:12.4	Khettaṁ hi taṁ puññapekkhassa hotī”ti.
snp1.4:13.1	“Atha kassa cāhaṁ, bho gotama, imaṁ pāyasaṁ dammī”ti?
snp1.4:13.2	“Na khvāhaṁ taṁ, brāhmaṇa, passāmi sadevake loke samārake sabrahmake sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadevamanussāya, yassa so pāyaso bhutto sammā pariṇāmaṁ gaccheyya, aññatra tathāgatassa vā tathāgatasāvakassa vā.
snp1.4:13.3	Tena hi tvaṁ, brāhmaṇa, taṁ pāyasaṁ appaharite vā chaḍḍehi appāṇake vā udake opilāpehī”ti.
snp1.4:14.1	Atha kho kasibhāradvājo brāhmaṇo taṁ pāyasaṁ appāṇake udake opilāpesi.
snp1.4:14.2	Atha kho so pāyaso udake pakkhitto cicciṭāyati ciṭiciṭāyati sandhūpāyati sampadhūpāyati.
snp1.4:14.3	Seyyathāpi nāma phālo divasaṁ santatto udake pakkhitto cicciṭāyati ciṭiciṭāyati sandhūpāyati sampadhūpāyati;
snp1.4:14.4	evamevaṁ so pāyaso udake pakkhitto cicciṭāyati ciṭiciṭāyati sandhūpāyati sampadhūpāyati.
snp1.4:15.1	Atha kho kasibhāradvājo brāhmaṇo saṁviggo lomahaṭṭhajāto yena bhagavā tenupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavato pādesu sirasā nipatitvā bhagavantaṁ etadavoca:
snp1.4:15.2	“abhikkantaṁ, bho gotama, abhikkantaṁ, bho gotama.
snp1.4:15.3	Seyyathāpi, bho gotama, nikkujjitaṁ vā ukkujjeyya, paṭicchannaṁ vā vivareyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṁ ācikkheyya, andhakāre vā telapajjotaṁ dhāreyya, ‘cakkhumanto rūpāni dakkhantī’ti; evamevaṁ bhotā gotamena anekapariyāyena dhammo pakāsito.
snp1.4:15.4	Esāhaṁ bhavantaṁ gotamaṁ saraṇaṁ gacchāmi dhammañca bhikkhusaṅghañca,
snp1.4:15.5	labheyyāhaṁ bhoto gotamassa santike pabbajjaṁ, labheyyaṁ upasampadan”ti.
snp1.4:16.1	Alattha kho kasibhāradvājo brāhmaṇo bhagavato santike pabbajjaṁ, alattha upasampadaṁ.
snp1.4:16.2	Acirūpasampanno kho panāyasmā bhāradvājo eko vūpakaṭṭho appamatto ātāpī pahitatto viharanto nacirasseva—yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadeva agārasmā anagāriyaṁ pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṁ—brahmacariyapariyosānaṁ diṭṭheva dhamme sayaṁ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja vihāsi.
snp1.4:16.3	“Khīṇā jāti, vusitaṁ brahmacariyaṁ, kataṁ karaṇīyaṁ, nāparaṁ itthattāyā”ti abbhaññāsi.
snp1.4:16.4	Aññataro ca panāyasmā bhāradvājo arahataṁ ahosīti.
snp1.4:16.5	Kasibhāradvājasuttaṁ catutthaṁ.
snp1.5:0.1	Sutta Nipāta 1.5
snp1.5:0.2	Cundasutta
snp1.5:1.1	“Pucchāmi muniṁ pahūtapaññaṁ,
snp1.5:1.2	(iti cundo kammāraputto)
snp1.5:1.3	Buddhaṁ dhammassāmiṁ vītataṇhaṁ;
snp1.5:1.4	Dvipaduttamaṁ sārathīnaṁ pavaraṁ,
snp1.5:1.5	Kati loke samaṇā tadiṅgha brūhi”.
snp1.5:2.1	“Caturo samaṇā na pañcamatthi,
snp1.5:2.2	(cundāti bhagavā)
snp1.5:2.3	Te te āvikaromi sakkhipuṭṭho;
snp1.5:2.4	Maggajino maggadesako ca,
snp1.5:2.5	Magge jīvati yo ca maggadūsī”.
snp1.5:3.1	“Kaṁ maggajinaṁ vadanti buddhā,
snp1.5:3.2	(iti cundo kammāraputto)
snp1.5:3.3	Maggakkhāyī kathaṁ atulyo hoti;
snp1.5:3.4	Magge jīvati me brūhi puṭṭho,
snp1.5:3.5	Atha me āvikarohi maggadūsiṁ”.
snp1.5:4.1	“Yo tiṇṇakathaṅkatho visallo,
snp1.5:4.2	Nibbānābhirato anānugiddho;
snp1.5:4.3	Lokassa sadevakassa netā,
snp1.5:4.4	Tādiṁ maggajinaṁ vadanti buddhā.
snp1.5:5.1	Paramaṁ paramanti yodha ñatvā,
snp1.5:5.2	Akkhāti vibhajate idheva dhammaṁ;
snp1.5:5.3	Taṁ kaṅkhachidaṁ muniṁ anejaṁ,
snp1.5:5.4	Dutiyaṁ bhikkhunamāhu maggadesiṁ.
snp1.5:6.1	Yo dhammapade sudesite,
snp1.5:6.2	Magge jīvati saññato satīmā;
snp1.5:6.3	Anavajjapadāni sevamāno,
snp1.5:6.4	Tatiyaṁ bhikkhunamāhu maggajīviṁ.
snp1.5:7.1	Chadanaṁ katvāna subbatānaṁ,
snp1.5:7.2	Pakkhandī kuladūsako pagabbho;
snp1.5:7.3	Māyāvī asaññato palāpo,
snp1.5:7.4	Patirūpena caraṁ sa maggadūsī.
snp1.5:8.1	Ete ca paṭivijjhi yo gahaṭṭho,
snp1.5:8.2	Sutavā ariyasāvako sapañño;
snp1.5:8.3	Sabbe netādisāti ñatvā,
snp1.5:8.4	Iti disvā na hāpeti tassa saddhā;
snp1.5:8.5	Kathaṁ hi duṭṭhena asampaduṭṭhaṁ,
snp1.5:8.6	Suddhaṁ asuddhena samaṁ kareyyā”ti.
snp1.5:8.7	Cundasuttaṁ pañcamaṁ.
snp1.6:0.1	Sutta Nipāta 1.6
snp1.6:0.2	Parābhavasutta
snp1.6:1.1	Evaṁ me sutaṁ—

snp1.6:1.3	Atha kho aññatarā devatā abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇā kevalakappaṁ jetavanaṁ obhāsetvā yena bhagavā tenupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṁ abhivādetvā ekamantaṁ aṭṭhāsi.
snp1.6:1.4	Ekamantaṁ ṭhitā kho sā devatā bhagavantaṁ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi:

snp1.6:2.2	mayaṁ pucchāma gotama;
snp1.6:2.3	Bhavantaṁ puṭṭhumāgamma,
snp1.6:2.4	kiṁ parābhavato mukhaṁ”.
snp1.6:3.1	“Suvijāno bhavaṁ hoti,
snp1.6:3.2	suvijāno parābhavo;

snp1.6:3.4	dhammadessī parābhavo”.
snp1.6:4.1	“Iti hetaṁ vijānāma,
snp1.6:4.2	paṭhamo so parābhavo;
snp1.6:4.3	Dutiyaṁ bhagavā brūhi,
snp1.6:4.4	kiṁ parābhavato mukhaṁ”.
snp1.6:5.1	“Asantassa piyā honti,
snp1.6:5.2	sante na kurute piyaṁ;

snp1.6:5.4	taṁ parābhavato mukhaṁ”.
snp1.6:6.1	“Iti hetaṁ vijānāma,

snp1.6:6.3	Tatiyaṁ bhagavā brūhi,
snp1.6:6.4	kiṁ parābhavato mukhaṁ”.
snp1.6:7.1	“Niddāsīlī sabhāsīlī,

snp1.6:7.3	Alaso kodhapaññāṇo,
snp1.6:7.4	taṁ parābhavato mukhaṁ”.

snp1.6:13.1	“Pahūtavitto puriso,
snp1.6:13.2	Sahirañño sabhojano;

snp1.6:15.1	“Jātitthaddho dhanatthaddho,

snp1.6:17.2	Akkhadhutto ca yo naro;

snp1.7:0.1	Sutta Nipāta 1.7
snp1.7:0.2	Vasalasutta
snp1.7:1.1	Evaṁ me sutaṁ—
snp1.7:1.2	ekaṁ samayaṁ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṁ viharati jetavane anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme.
snp1.7:1.3	Atha kho bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṁ nivāsetvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṁ piṇḍāya pāvisi.
snp1.7:1.4	Tena kho pana samayena aggikabhāradvājassa brāhmaṇassa nivesane aggi pajjalito hoti āhuti paggahitā.
snp1.7:1.5	Atha kho bhagavā sāvatthiyaṁ sapadānaṁ piṇḍāya caramāno yena aggikabhāradvājassa brāhmaṇassa nivesanaṁ tenupasaṅkami.
snp1.7:2.1	Addasā kho aggikabhāradvājo brāhmaṇo bhagavantaṁ dūratova āgacchantaṁ.
snp1.7:2.2	Disvāna bhagavantaṁ etadavoca:
snp1.7:2.3	“tatreva, muṇḍaka;
snp1.7:2.4	tatreva, samaṇaka;
snp1.7:2.5	tatreva, vasalaka, tiṭṭhāhī”ti.
snp1.7:3.1	Evaṁ vutte, bhagavā aggikabhāradvājaṁ brāhmaṇaṁ etadavoca:
snp1.7:3.2	“jānāsi pana tvaṁ, brāhmaṇa, vasalaṁ vā vasalakaraṇe vā dhamme”ti?
snp1.7:3.3	“Na khvāhaṁ, bho gotama, jānāmi vasalaṁ vā vasalakaraṇe vā dhamme;
snp1.7:3.4	sādhu me bhavaṁ gotamo tathā dhammaṁ desetu, yathāhaṁ jāneyyaṁ vasalaṁ vā vasalakaraṇe vā dhamme”ti.
snp1.7:3.5	“Tena hi, brāhmaṇa, suṇāhi, sādhukaṁ manasi karohi; bhāsissāmī”ti.
snp1.7:3.6	“Evaṁ, bho”ti kho aggikabhāradvājo brāhmaṇo bhagavato paccassosi.
snp1.7:3.7	Bhagavā etadavoca:
snp1.7:4.1	“Kodhano upanāhī ca,
snp1.7:4.2	pāpamakkhī ca yo naro;
snp1.7:4.3	Vipannadiṭṭhi māyāvī,
snp1.7:4.4	taṁ jaññā vasalo iti.
snp1.7:5.1	Ekajaṁ vā dvijaṁ vāpi,
snp1.7:5.2	yodha pāṇaṁ vihiṁsati;
snp1.7:5.3	Yassa pāṇe dayā natthi,
snp1.7:5.4	taṁ jaññā vasalo iti.
snp1.7:6.1	Yo hanti parirundhati,
snp1.7:6.2	gāmāni nigamāni ca;
snp1.7:6.3	Niggāhako samaññāto,
snp1.7:6.4	taṁ jaññā vasalo iti.
snp1.7:7.1	Gāme vā yadi vā raññe,
snp1.7:7.2	yaṁ paresaṁ mamāyitaṁ;
snp1.7:7.3	Theyyā adinnamādeti,
snp1.7:7.4	taṁ jaññā vasalo iti.
snp1.7:8.1	Yo have iṇamādāya,
snp1.7:8.2	cujjamāno palāyati;
snp1.7:8.3	Na hi te iṇamatthīti,
snp1.7:8.4	taṁ jaññā vasalo iti.
snp1.7:9.1	Yo ve kiñcikkhakamyatā,
snp1.7:9.2	panthasmiṁ vajantaṁ janaṁ;
snp1.7:9.3	Hantvā kiñcikkhamādeti,
snp1.7:9.4	taṁ jaññā vasalo iti.
snp1.7:10.1	Attahetu parahetu,
snp1.7:10.2	dhanahetu ca yo naro;
snp1.7:10.3	Sakkhipuṭṭho musā brūti,
snp1.7:10.4	taṁ jaññā vasalo iti.
snp1.7:11.1	Yo ñātīnaṁ sakhīnaṁ vā,
snp1.7:11.2	dāresu paṭidissati;
snp1.7:11.3	Sāhasā sampiyena vā,
snp1.7:11.4	taṁ jaññā vasalo iti.
snp1.7:12.1	Yo mātaraṁ pitaraṁ vā,
snp1.7:12.2	jiṇṇakaṁ gatayobbanaṁ;
snp1.7:12.3	Pahu santo na bharati,
snp1.7:12.4	taṁ jaññā vasalo iti.
snp1.7:13.1	Yo mātaraṁ pitaraṁ vā,
snp1.7:13.2	Bhātaraṁ bhaginiṁ sasuṁ;
snp1.7:13.3	Hanti roseti vācāya,
snp1.7:13.4	Taṁ jaññā vasalo iti.
snp1.7:14.1	Yo atthaṁ pucchito santo,
snp1.7:14.2	Anatthamanusāsati;
snp1.7:14.3	Paṭicchannena manteti,
snp1.7:14.4	Taṁ jaññā vasalo iti.
snp1.7:15.1	Yo katvā pāpakaṁ kammaṁ,
snp1.7:15.2	Mā maṁ jaññāti icchati;
snp1.7:15.3	Yo paṭicchannakammanto,
snp1.7:15.4	Taṁ jaññā vasalo iti.
snp1.7:16.1	Yo ve parakulaṁ gantvā,
snp1.7:16.2	Bhutvāna sucibhojanaṁ;
snp1.7:16.3	Āgataṁ nappaṭipūjeti,
snp1.7:16.4	Taṁ jaññā vasalo iti.

snp1.7:17.2	Aññaṁ vāpi vanibbakaṁ;
snp1.7:17.3	Musāvādena vañceti,
snp1.7:17.4	Taṁ jaññā vasalo iti.
snp1.7:18.1	Yo brāhmaṇaṁ samaṇaṁ vā,
snp1.7:18.2	Bhattakāle upaṭṭhite;

snp1.7:18.4	Taṁ jaññā vasalo iti.

snp1.7:19.2	Mohena paliguṇṭhito;
snp1.7:19.3	Kiñcikkhaṁ nijigīsāno,
snp1.7:19.4	Taṁ jaññā vasalo iti.
snp1.7:20.1	Yo cattānaṁ samukkaṁse,
snp1.7:20.2	Pare ca mavajānāti;
snp1.7:20.3	Nihīno sena mānena,
snp1.7:20.4	Taṁ jaññā vasalo iti.
snp1.7:21.1	Rosako kadariyo ca,
snp1.7:21.2	Pāpiccho maccharī saṭho;
snp1.7:21.3	Ahiriko anottappī,
snp1.7:21.4	Taṁ jaññā vasalo iti.
snp1.7:22.1	Yo buddhaṁ paribhāsati,
snp1.7:22.2	Atha vā tassa sāvakaṁ;
snp1.7:22.3	Paribbājaṁ gahaṭṭhaṁ vā,
snp1.7:22.4	Taṁ jaññā vasalo iti.
snp1.7:23.1	Yo ve anarahaṁ santo,
snp1.7:23.2	Arahaṁ paṭijānāti;
snp1.7:23.3	Coro sabrahmake loke,
snp1.7:23.4	Eso kho vasalādhamo;
snp1.7:23.5	Ete kho vasalā vuttā,
snp1.7:23.6	Mayā yete pakāsitā.

snp1.7:24.4	Kammunā hoti brāhmaṇo.

snp1.7:25.2	Yathāmedaṁ nidassanaṁ;

snp1.7:25.4	Mātaṅgo iti vissuto.
snp1.7:26.1	So yasaṁ paramaṁ patto,

snp1.7:27.2	Virajaṁ so mahāpathaṁ;
snp1.7:27.3	Kāmarāgaṁ virājetvā,
snp1.7:27.4	Brahmalokūpago ahu;
snp1.7:27.5	Na naṁ jāti nivāresi,
snp1.7:27.6	Brahmalokūpapattiyā.
snp1.7:28.1	Ajjhāyakakule jātā,
snp1.7:28.2	Brāhmaṇā mantabandhavā;
snp1.7:28.3	Te ca pāpesu kammesu,
snp1.7:28.4	Abhiṇhamupadissare.
snp1.7:29.1	Diṭṭheva dhamme gārayhā,
snp1.7:29.2	Samparāye ca duggati;
snp1.7:29.3	Na ne jāti nivāreti,
snp1.7:29.4	Duggatyā garahāya vā.

snp1.8:2.2	Appakicco ca sallahukavutti;

snp1.8:2.4	Appagabbho kulesvananugiddho.
snp1.8:3.1	Na ca khuddamācare kiñci,
snp1.8:3.2	Yena viññū pare upavadeyyuṁ;
snp1.8:3.3	Sukhino va khemino hontu,
snp1.8:3.4	Sabbasattā bhavantu sukhitattā.
snp1.8:4.1	Ye keci pāṇabhūtatthi,
snp1.8:4.2	Tasā vā thāvarā vanavasesā;
snp1.8:4.3	Dīghā vā ye va mahantā,
snp1.8:4.4	Majjhimā rassakā aṇukathūlā.
snp1.8:5.1	Diṭṭhā vā ye va adiṭṭhā,
snp1.8:5.2	Ye va dūre vasanti avidūre;
snp1.8:5.3	Bhūtā va sambhavesī va,
snp1.8:5.4	Sabbasattā bhavantu sukhitattā.
snp1.8:6.1	Na paro paraṁ nikubbetha,
snp1.8:6.2	Nātimaññetha katthaci na kañci;
snp1.8:6.3	Byārosanā paṭighasañña,
snp1.8:6.4	Nāññamaññassa dukkhamiccheyya.
snp1.8:7.1	Mātā yathā niyaṁ puttam
snp1.8:7.2	Āyusā ekaputtamanurakkhe;
snp1.8:7.3	Evampi sabbabhūtesu,
snp1.8:7.4	Mānasaṁ bhāvaye aparimāṇaṁ.
snp1.8:8.1	Mettañca sabbalokasmi,
snp1.8:8.2	Mānasaṁ bhāvaye aparimāṇaṁ;
snp1.8:8.3	Uddhaṁ adho ca tiriyañca,
snp1.8:8.4	Asambādhaṁ averamasapattaṁ.
snp1.8:9.1	Tiṭṭhaṁ caraṁ nisinno va,
snp1.8:9.2	Sayāno yāvatāssa vitamiddho;
snp1.8:9.3	Etaṁ satiṁ adhiṭṭheyya,
snp1.8:9.4	Brahmametaṁ vihāramidhamāhu.
snp1.8:10.1	Diṭṭhiñca anupaggamma,
snp1.8:10.2	Sīlavā dassanena sampanno;

snp1.8:10.4	Na hi jātuggabbhaseyya punaretī”ti.
snp1.8:10.5	Mettasuttaṁ aṭṭhamaṁ.

snp1.10:2.6	“Sādhāvuso”ti bhagavā pāvisi.
snp1.10:3.1	Catutthampi kho āḷavako yakkho bhagavantaṁ etadavoca:
snp1.10:3.2	“nikkhama, samaṇā”ti.
snp1.10:3.3	“Na khvāhaṁ taṁ, āvuso, nikkhamissāmi.
snp1.10:3.4	Yaṁ te karaṇīyaṁ, taṁ karohī”ti.
snp1.10:4.1	“Pañhaṁ taṁ, samaṇa, pucchissāmi.
snp1.10:4.2	Sace me na byākarissasi, cittaṁ vā te khipissāmi, hadayaṁ vā te phālessāmi, pādesu vā gahetvā pāragaṅgāya khipissāmī”ti.
snp1.10:5.1	“Na khvāhaṁ taṁ, āvuso, passāmi sadevake loke samārake sabrahmake sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadevamanussāya yo me cittaṁ vā khipeyya hadayaṁ vā phāleyya pādesu vā gahetvā pāragaṅgāya khipeyya.
snp1.10:5.2	Api ca tvaṁ, āvuso, puccha yadākaṅkhasī”ti.
snp1.10:5.3	Atha kho āḷavako yakkho bhagavantaṁ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi:
snp1.10:6.1	“Kiṁ sūdha vittaṁ purisassa seṭṭhaṁ,
snp1.10:6.2	Kiṁ su suciṇṇaṁ sukhamāvahāti;
snp1.10:6.3	Kiṁ su have sādutaraṁ rasānaṁ,
snp1.10:6.4	Kathaṁjīviṁ jīvitamāhu seṭṭhaṁ”.
snp1.10:7.1	“Saddhīdha vittaṁ purisassa seṭṭhaṁ,
snp1.10:7.2	Dhammo suciṇṇo sukhamāvahāti;
snp1.10:7.3	Saccaṁ have sādutaraṁ rasānaṁ,
snp1.10:7.4	Paññājīviṁ jīvitamāhu seṭṭhaṁ”.
snp1.10:8.1	“Kathaṁ su tarati oghaṁ,
snp1.10:8.2	kathaṁ su tarati aṇṇavaṁ;
snp1.10:8.3	Kathaṁ su dukkhamacceti,
snp1.10:8.4	kathaṁ su parisujjhati”.
snp1.10:9.1	“Saddhāya tarati oghaṁ,
snp1.10:9.2	appamādena aṇṇavaṁ;
snp1.10:9.3	Vīriyena dukkhamacceti,
snp1.10:9.4	paññāya parisujjhati”.
snp1.10:10.1	“Kathaṁ su labhate paññaṁ,
snp1.10:10.2	kathaṁ su vindate dhanaṁ;
snp1.10:10.3	Kathaṁ su kittiṁ pappoti,
snp1.10:10.4	kathaṁ mittāni ganthati;
snp1.10:10.5	Asmā lokā paraṁ lokaṁ,
snp1.10:10.6	kathaṁ pecca na socati”.
snp1.10:11.1	“Saddahāno arahataṁ,
snp1.10:11.2	dhammaṁ nibbānapattiyā;
snp1.10:11.3	Sussūsaṁ labhate paññaṁ,
snp1.10:11.4	appamatto vicakkhaṇo.
snp1.10:12.1	Patirūpakārī dhuravā,
snp1.10:12.2	uṭṭhātā vindate dhanaṁ;
snp1.10:12.3	Saccena kittiṁ pappoti,
snp1.10:12.4	dadaṁ mittāni ganthati.
snp1.10:13.1	Yassete caturo dhammā,
snp1.10:13.2	saddhassa gharamesino;
snp1.10:13.3	Saccaṁ dhammo dhiti cāgo,
snp1.10:13.4	sa ve pecca na socati.
snp1.10:14.1	Iṅgha aññepi pucchassu,
snp1.10:14.2	puthū samaṇabrāhmaṇe;
snp1.10:14.3	Yadi saccā damā cāgā,
snp1.10:14.4	khantyā bhiyyodha vijjati”.
snp1.10:15.1	“Kathaṁ nu dāni puccheyyaṁ,
snp1.10:15.2	Puthū samaṇabrāhmaṇe;
snp1.10:15.3	Yohaṁ ajja pajānāmi,
snp1.10:15.4	Yo attho samparāyiko.
snp1.10:16.1	Atthāya vata me buddho,
snp1.10:16.2	Vāsāyāḷavimāgamā;
snp1.10:16.3	Yohaṁ ajja pajānāmi,
snp1.10:16.4	Yattha dinnaṁ mahapphalaṁ.
snp1.10:17.1	So ahaṁ vicarissāmi,
snp1.10:17.2	Gāmā gāmaṁ purā puraṁ;
snp1.10:17.3	Namassamāno sambuddhaṁ,
snp1.10:17.4	Dhammassa ca sudhammatan”ti.
snp1.10:17.5	Āḷavakasuttaṁ dasamaṁ.

snp2.2:1.1	“Sāmākaciṅgūlakacīnakāni ca,

snp2.2:2.4	So bhuñjasī kassapa āmagandhaṁ.
snp2.2:3.1	Na āmagandho mama kappatīti,
snp2.2:3.2	Icceva tvaṁ bhāsasi brahmabandhu;
snp2.2:3.3	Sālīnamannaṁ paribhuñjamāno,
snp2.2:3.4	Sakuntamaṁsehi susaṅkhatehi;
snp2.2:3.5	Pucchāmi taṁ kassapa etamatthaṁ,
snp2.2:3.6	Kathaṁpakāro tava āmagandho”.
snp2.2:4.1	“Pāṇātipāto vadhachedabandhanaṁ,
snp2.2:4.2	Theyyaṁ musāvādo nikativañcanāni ca;
snp2.2:4.3	Ajjhenakuttaṁ paradārasevanā,
snp2.2:4.4	Esāmagandho na hi maṁsabhojanaṁ.
snp2.2:5.1	Ye idha kāmesu asaññatā janā,
snp2.2:5.2	Rasesu giddhā asucibhāvamassitā;
snp2.2:5.3	Natthikadiṭṭhī visamā durannayā,
snp2.2:5.4	Esāmagandho na hi maṁsabhojanaṁ.
snp2.2:6.1	Ye lūkhasā dāruṇā piṭṭhimaṁsikā,
snp2.2:6.2	Mittadduno nikkaruṇātimānino;
snp2.2:6.3	Adānasīlā na ca denti kassaci,
snp2.2:6.4	Esāmagandho na hi maṁsabhojanaṁ.
snp2.2:7.1	Kodho mado thambho paccupaṭṭhāpanā,
snp2.2:7.2	Māyā usūyā bhassasamussayo ca;
snp2.2:7.3	Mānātimāno ca asabbhi santhavo,
snp2.2:7.4	Esāmagandho na hi maṁsabhojanaṁ.
snp2.2:8.1	Ye pāpasīlā iṇaghātasūcakā,
snp2.2:8.2	Vohārakūṭā idha pāṭirūpikā;
snp2.2:8.3	Narādhamā yedha karonti kibbisaṁ,
snp2.2:8.4	Esāmagandho na hi maṁsabhojanaṁ.
snp2.2:9.1	Ye idha pāṇesu asaññatā janā,
snp2.2:9.2	Paresamādāya vihesamuyyutā;
snp2.2:9.3	Dussīlaluddā pharusā anādarā,
snp2.2:9.4	Esāmagandho na hi maṁsabhojanaṁ.
snp2.2:10.1	Etesu giddhā viruddhātipātino,
snp2.2:10.2	Niccuyyutā pecca tamaṁ vajanti ye;
snp2.2:10.3	Patanti sattā nirayaṁ avaṁsirā,
snp2.2:10.4	Esāmagandho na hi maṁsabhojanaṁ.
snp2.2:11.1	Na macchamaṁsānamanāsakattaṁ,
snp2.2:11.2	Na naggiyaṁ na muṇḍiyaṁ jaṭājallaṁ;
snp2.2:11.3	Kharājināni nāggihuttassupasevanā,
snp2.2:11.4	Ye vāpi loke amarā bahū tapā;
snp2.2:11.5	Mantāhutī yaññamutūpasevanā,
snp2.2:11.6	Sodhenti maccaṁ avitiṇṇakaṅkhaṁ.
snp2.2:12.1	Sotesu gutto viditindriyo care,
snp2.2:12.2	Dhamme ṭhito ajjavamaddave rato;
snp2.2:12.3	Saṅgātigo sabbadukkhappahīno,
snp2.2:12.4	Na lippati diṭṭhasutesu dhīro”.
snp2.2:13.1	Iccetamatthaṁ bhagavā punappunaṁ,
snp2.2:13.2	Akkhāsi naṁ vedayi mantapāragū;
snp2.2:13.3	Citrāhi gāthāhi munī pakāsayi,
snp2.2:13.4	Nirāmagandho asito durannayo.
snp2.2:14.1	Sutvāna buddhassa subhāsitaṁ padaṁ,
snp2.2:14.2	Nirāmagandhaṁ sabbadukkhappanūdanaṁ;
snp2.2:14.3	Nīcamano vandi tathāgatassa,
snp2.2:14.4	Tattheva pabbajjamarocayitthāti.

snp2.3:1.4	Neso mamanti iti naṁ vijaññā.
snp2.3:2.1	Ananvayaṁ piyaṁ vācaṁ,
snp2.3:2.2	yo mittesu pakubbati;
snp2.3:2.3	Akarontaṁ bhāsamānaṁ,
snp2.3:2.4	parijānanti paṇḍitā.

snp2.3:3.2	Bhedāsaṅkī randhamevānupassī;
snp2.3:3.3	Yasmiñca seti urasīva putto,
snp2.3:3.4	Sa ve mitto yo parehi abhejjo.

snp2.4:1.1	Evaṁ me sutaṁ—

snp2.4:1.3	Atha kho aññatarā devatā abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇā kevalakappaṁ jetavanaṁ obhāsetvā yena bhagavā tenupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṁ abhivādetvā ekamantaṁ aṭṭhāsi.
snp2.4:1.4	Ekamantaṁ ṭhitā kho sā devatā bhagavantaṁ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi:

snp2.4:4.4	etaṁ maṅgalamuttamaṁ.

snp2.4:5.2	vinayo ca susikkhito;

snp2.4:5.4	etaṁ maṅgalamuttamaṁ.

snp2.4:7.4	etaṁ maṅgalamuttamaṁ.

snp2.4:8.2	majjapānā ca saṁyamo;

snp2.4:8.4	etaṁ maṅgalamuttamaṁ.

snp2.4:9.4	etaṁ maṅgalamuttamaṁ.

snp2.4:12.4	Etaṁ maṅgalamuttamaṁ.
snp2.4:13.1	Etādisāni katvāna,

snp2.5:0.1	Sutta Nipāta 2.5
snp2.5:0.2	Sūcilomasutta
snp2.5:1.1	Evaṁ me sutaṁ—
snp2.5:1.2	ekaṁ samayaṁ bhagavā gayāyaṁ viharati ṭaṅkitamañce sūcilomassa yakkhassa bhavane.
snp2.5:1.3	Tena kho pana samayena kharo ca yakkho sūcilomo ca yakkho bhagavato avidūre atikkamanti.
snp2.5:1.4	Atha kho kharo yakkho sūcilomaṁ yakkhaṁ etadavoca:
snp2.5:1.5	“eso samaṇo”ti.
snp2.5:1.6	“Neso samaṇo, samaṇako eso.
snp2.5:1.7	Yāvāhaṁ jānāmi yadi vā so samaṇo, yadi vā so samaṇako”ti.
snp2.5:2.1	Atha kho sūcilomo yakkho yena bhagavā tenupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavato kāyaṁ upanāmesi.
snp2.5:2.2	Atha kho bhagavā kāyaṁ apanāmesi.
snp2.5:2.3	Atha kho sūcilomo yakkho bhagavantaṁ etadavoca:
snp2.5:2.4	“bhāyasi maṁ, samaṇā”ti?
snp2.5:2.5	“Na khvāhaṁ taṁ, āvuso, bhāyāmi;
snp2.5:2.6	api ca te samphasso pāpako”ti.
snp2.5:3.1	“Pañhaṁ taṁ, samaṇa, pucchissāmi.
snp2.5:3.2	Sace me na byākarissasi, cittaṁ vā te khipissāmi, hadayaṁ vā te phālessāmi, pādesu vā gahetvā pāragaṅgāya khipissāmī”ti.
snp2.5:4.1	“Na khvāhaṁ taṁ, āvuso, passāmi sadevake loke samārake sabrahmake sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadevamanussāya yo me cittaṁ vā khipeyya hadayaṁ vā phāleyya pādesu vā gahetvā pāragaṅgāya khipeyya.
snp2.5:4.2	Api ca tvaṁ, āvuso, puccha yadākaṅkhasī”ti.
snp2.5:4.3	Atha kho sūcilomo yakkho bhagavantaṁ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi:
snp2.5:5.1	“Rāgo ca doso ca kutonidānā,
snp2.5:5.2	Aratī ratī lomahaṁso kutojā;
snp2.5:5.3	Kuto samuṭṭhāya manovitakkā,
snp2.5:5.4	Kumārakā dhaṅkamivossajanti”.
snp2.5:6.1	“Rāgo ca doso ca itonidānā,
snp2.5:6.2	Aratī ratī lomahaṁso itojā;
snp2.5:6.3	Ito samuṭṭhāya manovitakkā,
snp2.5:6.4	Kumārakā dhaṅkamivossajanti.
snp2.5:7.1	Snehajā attasambhūtā,
snp2.5:7.2	Nigrodhasseva khandhajā;
snp2.5:7.3	Puthū visattā kāmesu,
snp2.5:7.4	Māluvāva vitatāvane.
snp2.5:8.1	Ye naṁ pajānanti yatonidānaṁ,
snp2.5:8.2	Te naṁ vinodenti suṇohi yakkha;
snp2.5:8.3	Te duttaraṁ oghamimaṁ taranti,
snp2.5:8.4	Atiṇṇapubbaṁ apunabbhavāyā”ti.
snp2.5:8.5	Sūcilomasuttaṁ pañcamaṁ.
snp2.6:0.1	Sutta Nipāta 2.6
snp2.6:0.2	Kapilasutta (dhammacariyasutta)
snp2.6:1.1	Dhammacariyaṁ brahmacariyaṁ,
snp2.6:1.2	Etadāhu vasuttamaṁ;
snp2.6:1.3	Pabbajitopi ce hoti,
snp2.6:1.4	Agārā anagāriyaṁ.
snp2.6:2.1	So ce mukharajātiko,
snp2.6:2.2	vihesābhirato mago;
snp2.6:2.3	Jīvitaṁ tassa pāpiyo,
snp2.6:2.4	rajaṁ vaḍḍheti attano.
snp2.6:3.1	Kalahābhirato bhikkhu,
snp2.6:3.2	Mohadhammena āvuto;
snp2.6:3.3	Akkhātampi na jānāti,
snp2.6:3.4	Dhammaṁ buddhena desitaṁ.

snp2.6:4.2	avijjāya purakkhato;
snp2.6:4.3	Saṅkilesaṁ na jānāti,
snp2.6:4.4	maggaṁ nirayagāminaṁ.
snp2.6:5.1	Vinipātaṁ samāpanno,
snp2.6:5.2	gabbhā gabbhaṁ tamā tamaṁ;
snp2.6:5.3	Sa ve tādisako bhikkhu,
snp2.6:5.4	pecca dukkhaṁ nigacchati.
snp2.6:6.1	Gūthakūpo yathā assa,
snp2.6:6.2	sampuṇṇo gaṇavassiko;
snp2.6:6.3	Yo ca evarūpo assa,
snp2.6:6.4	dubbisodho hi sāṅgaṇo.
snp2.6:7.1	Yaṁ evarūpaṁ jānātha,

snp2.6:8.2	abhinibbajjiyātha naṁ;
snp2.6:8.3	Kāraṇḍavaṁ niddhamatha,
snp2.6:8.4	kasambuṁ apakassatha.
snp2.6:9.1	Tato palāpe vāhetha,
snp2.6:9.2	Assamaṇe samaṇamānine;

snp2.6:9.4	Pāpaācāragocare.

snp2.6:10.2	Kappayavho patissatā;

snp2.6:10.4	Dukkhassantaṁ karissathāti.
snp2.6:10.5	Dhammacariyasuttaṁ chaṭṭhaṁ.
snp2.7:0.1	Sutta Nipāta 2.7
snp2.7:0.2	Brāhmaṇadhammikasutta
snp2.7:1.1	Evaṁ me sutaṁ—
snp2.7:1.2	ekaṁ samayaṁ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṁ viharati jetavane anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme.
snp2.7:1.3	Atha kho sambahulā kosalakā brāhmaṇamahāsālā jiṇṇā vuḍḍhā mahallakā addhagatā vayoanuppattā yena bhagavā tenupasaṅkamiṁsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṁ sammodiṁsu.
snp2.7:1.4	Sammodanīyaṁ kathaṁ sāraṇīyaṁ vītisāretvā ekamantaṁ nisīdiṁsu. Ekamantaṁ nisinnā kho te brāhmaṇamahāsālā bhagavantaṁ etadavocuṁ:
snp2.7:1.5	“sandissanti nu kho, bho gotama, etarahi brāhmaṇā porāṇānaṁ brāhmaṇānaṁ brāhmaṇadhamme”ti?
snp2.7:1.6	“Na kho, brāhmaṇā, sandissanti etarahi brāhmaṇā porāṇānaṁ brāhmaṇānaṁ brāhmaṇadhamme”ti.
snp2.7:1.7	“Sādhu no bhavaṁ gotamo porāṇānaṁ brāhmaṇānaṁ brāhmaṇadhammaṁ bhāsatu, sace bhoto gotamassa agarū”ti.
snp2.7:1.8	“Tena hi, brāhmaṇā, suṇātha, sādhukaṁ manasi karotha, bhāsissāmī”ti.
snp2.7:1.9	“Evaṁ, bho”ti kho te brāhmaṇamahāsālā bhagavato paccassosuṁ.
snp2.7:1.10	Bhagavā etadavoca:
snp2.7:2.1	“Isayo pubbakā āsuṁ,
snp2.7:2.2	saññatattā tapassino;
snp2.7:2.3	Pañca kāmaguṇe hitvā,

snp2.7:3.1	Na pasū brāhmaṇānāsuṁ,
snp2.7:3.2	na hiraññaṁ na dhāniyaṁ;
snp2.7:3.3	Sajjhāyadhanadhaññāsuṁ,
snp2.7:3.4	brahmaṁ nidhimapālayuṁ.
snp2.7:4.1	Yaṁ nesaṁ pakataṁ āsi,
snp2.7:4.2	dvārabhattaṁ upaṭṭhitaṁ;
snp2.7:4.3	Saddhāpakatamesānaṁ,
snp2.7:4.4	dātave tadamaññisuṁ.
snp2.7:5.1	Nānārattehi vatthehi,
snp2.7:5.2	sayanehāvasathehi ca;
snp2.7:5.3	Phītā janapadā raṭṭhā,
snp2.7:5.4	te namassiṁsu brāhmaṇe.
snp2.7:6.1	Avajjhā brāhmaṇā āsuṁ,
snp2.7:6.2	ajeyyā dhammarakkhitā;
snp2.7:6.3	Na ne koci nivāresi,
snp2.7:6.4	kuladvāresu sabbaso.
snp2.7:7.1	Aṭṭhacattālīsaṁ vassāni,
snp2.7:7.2	(komāra) Brahmacariyaṁ cariṁsu te;
snp2.7:7.3	Vijjācaraṇapariyeṭṭhiṁ,
snp2.7:7.4	Acaruṁ brāhmaṇā pure.
snp2.7:8.1	Na brāhmaṇā aññamagamuṁ,
snp2.7:8.2	napi bhariyaṁ kiṇiṁsu te;
snp2.7:8.3	Sampiyeneva saṁvāsaṁ,
snp2.7:8.4	saṅgantvā samarocayuṁ.
snp2.7:9.1	Aññatra tamhā samayā,
snp2.7:9.2	utuveramaṇiṁ pati;

snp2.7:10.2	ajjavaṁ maddavaṁ tapaṁ;
snp2.7:10.3	Soraccaṁ avihiṁsañca,
snp2.7:10.4	khantiñcāpi avaṇṇayuṁ.
snp2.7:11.1	Yo nesaṁ paramo āsi,
snp2.7:11.2	Brahmā daḷhaparakkamo;
snp2.7:11.3	Sa vāpi methunaṁ dhammaṁ,
snp2.7:11.4	Supinantepi nāgamā.

snp2.7:12.4	Khantiñcāpi avaṇṇayuṁ.
snp2.7:13.1	Taṇḍulaṁ sayanaṁ vatthaṁ,
snp2.7:13.2	Sappitelañca yāciya;
snp2.7:13.3	Dhammena samodhānetvā,
snp2.7:13.4	Tato yaññamakappayuṁ.
snp2.7:14.1	Upaṭṭhitasmiṁ yaññasmiṁ,
snp2.7:14.2	Nāssu gāvo haniṁsu te;
snp2.7:14.3	Yathā mātā pitā bhātā,
snp2.7:14.4	Aññe vāpi ca ñātakā;
snp2.7:14.5	Gāvo no paramā mittā,
snp2.7:14.6	Yāsu jāyanti osadhā.

snp2.7:15.4	Nāssu gāvo haniṁsu te.

snp2.8:4.4	Kiṁ so pare sakkhati tārayetuṁ.
snp2.8:5.1	Tatheva dhammaṁ avibhāvayitvā,
snp2.8:5.2	Bahussutānaṁ anisāmayatthaṁ;

snp2.12:0.1	Sutta Nipāta 2.12
snp2.12:0.2	Nigrodhakappa (vaṅgīsa) sutta
snp2.12:1.1	Evaṁ me sutaṁ—
snp2.12:1.2	ekaṁ samayaṁ bhagavā āḷaviyaṁ viharati aggāḷave cetiye.
snp2.12:1.3	Tena kho pana samayena āyasmato vaṅgīsassa upajjhāyo nigrodhakappo nāma thero aggāḷave cetiye aciraparinibbuto hoti.
snp2.12:1.4	Atha kho āyasmato vaṅgīsassa rahogatassa paṭisallīnassa evaṁ cetaso parivitakko udapādi—
snp2.12:1.5	parinibbuto nu kho me upajjhāyo udāhu no parinibbutoti?
snp2.12:1.6	Atha kho āyasmā vaṅgīso sāyanhasamayaṁ paṭisallānā vuṭṭhito yena bhagavā tenupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṁ abhivādetvā ekamantaṁ nisīdi. Ekamantaṁ nisinno kho āyasmā vaṅgīso bhagavantaṁ etadavoca—
snp2.12:1.7	idha mayhaṁ, bhante, rahogatassa paṭisallīnassa evaṁ cetaso parivitakko udapādi—
snp2.12:1.8	parinibbuto nu kho me upajjhāyo, udāhu no parinibbutoti.
snp2.12:1.9	Atha kho āyasmā vaṅgīso uṭṭhāyāsanā ekaṁsaṁ cīvaraṁ katvā yena bhagavā tenañjaliṁ paṇāmetvā bhagavantaṁ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi:
snp2.12:2.1	“Pucchāmi satthāramanomapaññaṁ,
snp2.12:2.2	Diṭṭheva dhamme yo vicikicchānaṁ chettā;
snp2.12:2.3	Aggāḷave kālamakāsi bhikkhu,
snp2.12:2.4	Ñāto yasassī abhinibbutatto.
snp2.12:3.1	Nigrodhakappo iti tassa nāmaṁ,
snp2.12:3.2	Tayā kataṁ bhagavā brāhmaṇassa;
snp2.12:3.3	So taṁ namassaṁ acari mutyapekkho,
snp2.12:3.4	Āraddhaviriyo daḷhadhammadassī.
snp2.12:4.1	Taṁ sāvakaṁ sakya mayampi sabbe,
snp2.12:4.2	Aññātumicchāma samantacakkhu;
snp2.12:4.3	Samavaṭṭhitā no savanāya sotā,
snp2.12:4.4	Tuvaṁ no satthā tvamanuttarosi.
snp2.12:5.1	Chindeva no vicikicchaṁ brūhi metaṁ,
snp2.12:5.2	Parinibbutaṁ vedaya bhūripañña;
snp2.12:5.3	Majjheva no bhāsa samantacakkhu,
snp2.12:5.4	Sakkova devāna sahassanetto.
snp2.12:6.1	Ye keci ganthā idha mohamaggā,
snp2.12:6.2	Aññāṇapakkhā vicikicchaṭhānā;
snp2.12:6.3	Tathāgataṁ patvā na te bhavanti,
snp2.12:6.4	Cakkhuñhi etaṁ paramaṁ narānaṁ.
snp2.12:7.1	No ce hi jātu puriso kilese,
snp2.12:7.2	Vāto yathā abbhaghanaṁ vihāne;
snp2.12:7.3	Tamovassa nivuto sabbaloko,
snp2.12:7.4	Na jotimantopi narā tapeyyuṁ.
snp2.12:8.1	Dhīrā ca pajjotakarā bhavanti,
snp2.12:8.2	Taṁ taṁ ahaṁ vīra tatheva maññe;
snp2.12:8.3	Vipassinaṁ jānamupāgamumhā,
snp2.12:8.4	Parisāsu no āvikarohi kappaṁ.
snp2.12:9.1	Khippaṁ giraṁ eraya vaggu vagguṁ,
snp2.12:9.2	Haṁsova paggayha saṇikaṁ nikūja;
snp2.12:9.3	Bindussarena suvikappitena,
snp2.12:9.4	Sabbeva te ujjugatā suṇoma.
snp2.12:10.1	Pahīnajātimaraṇaṁ asesaṁ,
snp2.12:10.2	Niggayha dhonaṁ vadessāmi dhammaṁ;
snp2.12:10.3	Na kāmakāro hi puthujjanānaṁ,
snp2.12:10.4	Saṅkheyyakāro ca tathāgatānaṁ.
snp2.12:11.1	Sampannaveyyākaraṇaṁ tavedaṁ,
snp2.12:11.2	Samujjupaññassa samuggahītaṁ;
snp2.12:11.3	Ayamañjalī pacchimo suppaṇāmito,
snp2.12:11.4	Mā mohayī jānamanomapañña.
snp2.12:12.1	Parovaraṁ ariyadhammaṁ viditvā,
snp2.12:12.2	Mā mohayī jānamanomavīra;
snp2.12:12.3	Vāriṁ yathā ghammani ghammatatto,
snp2.12:12.4	Vācābhikaṅkhāmi sutaṁ pavassa.
snp2.12:13.1	Yadatthikaṁ brahmacariyaṁ acarī,
snp2.12:13.2	Kappāyano kaccissa taṁ amoghaṁ;
snp2.12:13.3	Nibbāyi so ādu saupādiseso,
snp2.12:13.4	Yathā vimutto ahu taṁ suṇoma”.
snp2.12:14.1	“Acchecchi taṇhaṁ idha nāmarūpe,
snp2.12:14.2	(iti bhagavā)

snp2.13:2.3	Uppātā supinā ca lakkhaṇā ca;

snp2.13:2.5	Sammā so loke paribbajeyya.
snp2.13:3.1	Rāgaṁ vinayetha mānusesu,
snp2.13:3.2	Dibbesu kāmesu cāpi bhikkhu;
snp2.13:3.3	Atikkamma bhavaṁ samecca dhammaṁ,
snp2.13:3.4	Sammā so loke paribbajeyya.
snp2.13:4.1	Vipiṭṭhikatvāna pesuṇāni,

snp2.13:6.4	Sammā so loke paribbajeyya.

snp2.13:9.2	Virato chedanabandhanā ca bhikkhu;

snp2.13:9.4	Sammā so loke paribbajeyya.

snp2.13:10.2	No ca bhikkhu hiṁseyya kañci loke;

snp2.13:11.1	Yassānusayā na santi keci,

snp2.13:11.4	Sammā so loke paribbajeyya.
snp2.13:12.1	Āsavakhīṇo pahīnamāno,

snp2.13:13.2	Vaggagatesu na vaggasāri dhīro;

snp2.14:0.1	Sutta Nipāta 2.14
snp2.14:0.2	Dhammikasutta
snp2.14:1.1	Evaṁ me sutaṁ—
snp2.14:1.2	ekaṁ samayaṁ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṁ viharati jetavane anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme.
snp2.14:1.3	Atha kho dhammiko upāsako pañcahi upāsakasatehi saddhiṁ yena bhagavā tenupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṁ abhivādetvā ekamantaṁ nisīdi. Ekamantaṁ nisinno kho dhammiko upāsako bhagavantaṁ gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi:
snp2.14:2.1	“Pucchāmi taṁ gotama bhūripañña,
snp2.14:2.2	Kathaṅkaro sāvako sādhu hoti;
snp2.14:2.3	Yo vā agārā anagārameti,
snp2.14:2.4	Agārino vā panupāsakāse.
snp2.14:3.1	Tuvañhi lokassa sadevakassa,
snp2.14:3.2	Gatiṁ pajānāsi parāyaṇañca;
snp2.14:3.3	Na catthi tulyo nipuṇatthadassī,
snp2.14:3.4	Tuvañhi buddhaṁ pavaraṁ vadanti.
snp2.14:4.1	Sabbaṁ tuvaṁ ñāṇamavecca dhammaṁ,
snp2.14:4.2	Pakāsesi satte anukampamāno;
snp2.14:4.3	Vivaṭṭacchadosi samantacakkhu,
snp2.14:4.4	Virocasi vimalo sabbaloke.
snp2.14:5.1	Āgañchi te santike nāgarājā,
snp2.14:5.2	Erāvaṇo nāma jinoti sutvā;
snp2.14:5.3	Sopi tayā mantayitvājjhagamā,
snp2.14:5.4	Sādhūti sutvāna patītarūpo.
snp2.14:6.1	Rājāpi taṁ vessavaṇo kuvero,
snp2.14:6.2	Upeti dhammaṁ paripucchamāno;
snp2.14:6.3	Tassāpi tvaṁ pucchito brūsi dhīra,
snp2.14:6.4	So cāpi sutvāna patītarūpo.
snp2.14:7.1	Ye kecime titthiyā vādasīlā,
snp2.14:7.2	Ājīvakā vā yadi vā nigaṇṭhā;
snp2.14:7.3	Paññāya taṁ nātitaranti sabbe,
snp2.14:7.4	Ṭhito vajantaṁ viya sīghagāmiṁ.
snp2.14:8.1	Ye kecime brāhmaṇā vādasīlā,
snp2.14:8.2	Vuddhā cāpi brāhmaṇā santi keci;
snp2.14:8.3	Sabbe tayi atthabaddhā bhavanti,
snp2.14:8.4	Ye cāpi aññe vādino maññamānā.
snp2.14:9.1	Ayañhi dhammo nipuṇo sukho ca,
snp2.14:9.2	Yoyaṁ tayā bhagavā suppavutto;
snp2.14:9.3	Tameva sabbepi sussūsamānā,
snp2.14:9.4	Taṁ no vada pucchito buddhaseṭṭha.
snp2.14:10.1	Sabbepime bhikkhavo sannisinnā,
snp2.14:10.2	Upāsakā cāpi tatheva sotuṁ;
snp2.14:10.3	Suṇantu dhammaṁ vimalenānubuddhaṁ,
snp2.14:10.4	Subhāsitaṁ vāsavasseva devā”.
snp2.14:11.1	“Suṇātha me bhikkhavo sāvayāmi vo,
