snp3.2:6.2	bhagavā etadabravi;
snp3.2:6.3	“Pamattabandhu pāpima,
snp3.2:6.4	yenatthena idhāgato.
snp3.2:7.1	Aṇumattopi puññena,
snp3.2:7.2	Attho mayhaṁ na vijjati;
snp3.2:7.3	Yesañca attho puññena,
snp3.2:7.4	Te māro vattumarahati.
snp3.2:8.1	Atthi saddhā tathā viriyaṁ,
snp3.2:8.2	paññā ca mama vijjati;
snp3.2:8.3	Evaṁ maṁ pahitattampi,
snp3.2:8.4	kiṁ jīvamanupucchasi.
snp3.2:9.1	Nadīnamapi sotāni,
snp3.2:9.2	ayaṁ vāto visosaye;
snp3.2:9.3	Kiñca me pahitattassa,
snp3.2:9.4	lohitaṁ nupasussaye.
snp3.2:10.1	Lohite sussamānamhi,
snp3.2:10.2	Pittaṁ semhañca sussati;
snp3.2:10.3	Maṁsesu khīyamānesu,
snp3.2:10.4	Bhiyyo cittaṁ pasīdati;
snp3.2:10.5	Bhiyyo sati ca paññā ca,
snp3.2:10.6	Samādhi mama tiṭṭhati.
snp3.2:11.1	Tassa mevaṁ viharato,
snp3.2:11.2	Pattassuttamavedanaṁ;
snp3.2:11.3	Kāmesu nāpekkhate cittaṁ,
snp3.2:11.4	Passa sattassa suddhataṁ.
snp3.2:12.1	Kāmā te paṭhamā senā,
snp3.2:12.2	Dutiyā arati vuccati;
snp3.2:12.3	Tatiyā khuppipāsā te,
snp3.2:12.4	Catutthī taṇhā pavuccati.
snp3.2:13.1	Pañcamaṁ thinamiddhaṁ te,
snp3.2:13.2	Chaṭṭhā bhīrū pavuccati;
snp3.2:13.3	Sattamī vicikicchā te,
snp3.2:13.4	Makkho thambho te aṭṭhamo.
snp3.2:14.1	Lābho siloko sakkāro,
snp3.2:14.2	Micchāladdho ca yo yaso;
snp3.2:14.3	Yo cattānaṁ samukkaṁse,
snp3.2:14.4	Pare ca avajānati.
snp3.2:15.1	Esā namuci te senā,
snp3.2:15.2	Kaṇhassābhippahārinī;
snp3.2:15.3	Na naṁ asūro jināti,
snp3.2:15.4	Jetvā ca labhate sukhaṁ.
snp3.2:16.1	Esa muñjaṁ parihare,
snp3.2:16.2	Dhiratthu mama jīvitaṁ;
snp3.2:16.3	Saṅgāme me mataṁ seyyo,
snp3.2:16.4	Yañce jīve parājito.
snp3.2:17.1	Pagāḷhettha na dissanti,
snp3.2:17.2	Eke samaṇabrāhmaṇā;
snp3.2:17.3	Tañca maggaṁ na jānanti,
snp3.2:17.4	Yena gacchanti subbatā.
snp3.2:18.1	Samantā dhajiniṁ disvā,
snp3.2:18.2	Yuttaṁ māraṁ savāhanaṁ;
snp3.2:18.3	Yuddhāya paccuggacchāmi,
snp3.2:18.4	Mā maṁ ṭhānā acāvayi.
snp3.2:19.1	Yaṁ te taṁ nappasahati,
snp3.2:19.2	Senaṁ loko sadevako;
snp3.2:19.3	Taṁ te paññāya bhecchāmi,
snp3.2:19.4	Āmaṁ pattaṁva asmanā.
snp3.2:20.1	Vasīkaritvā saṅkappaṁ,
snp3.2:20.2	Satiñca sūpatiṭṭhitaṁ;
snp3.2:20.3	Raṭṭhā raṭṭhaṁ vicarissaṁ,
snp3.2:20.4	Sāvake vinayaṁ puthū.
snp3.2:21.1	Te appamattā pahitattā,
snp3.2:21.2	Mama sāsanakārakā;
snp3.2:21.3	Akāmassa te gamissanti,
snp3.2:21.4	Yattha gantvā na socare”.
snp3.2:22.1	“Satta vassāni bhagavantaṁ,
snp3.2:22.2	Anubandhiṁ padāpadaṁ;
snp3.2:22.3	Otāraṁ nādhigacchissaṁ,
snp3.2:22.4	Sambuddhassa satīmato.
snp3.2:23.1	Medavaṇṇaṁva pāsāṇaṁ,
snp3.2:23.2	Vāyaso anupariyagā;
snp3.2:23.3	Apettha muduṁ vindema,
snp3.2:23.4	Api assādanā siyā.
snp3.2:24.1	Aladdhā tattha assādaṁ,
snp3.2:24.2	Vāyasetto apakkami;
snp3.2:24.3	Kākova selamāsajja,
snp3.2:24.4	Nibbijjāpema gotamaṁ”.
snp3.2:25.1	Tassa sokaparetassa,
snp3.2:25.2	Vīṇā kacchā abhassatha;
snp3.2:25.3	Tato so dummano yakkho,
snp3.2:25.4	Tatthevantaradhāyathāti.
snp3.2:25.5	Padhānasuttaṁ dutiyaṁ.
snp3.3:0.1	Sutta Nipāta 3.3
snp3.3:0.2	Subhāsitasutta
snp3.3:1.1	Evaṁ me sutaṁ—
snp3.3:1.2	eka samayaṁ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṁ viharati jetavane anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme.
snp3.3:1.3	Tatra kho bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi:
snp3.3:1.4	“bhikkhavo”ti.
snp3.3:1.5	“Bhadante”ti te bhikkhū bhagavato paccassosuṁ.
snp3.3:1.6	Bhagavā etadavoca:
snp3.3:2.1	“Catūhi, bhikkhave, aṅgehi samannāgatā vācā subhāsitā hoti, na dubbhāsitā, anavajjā ca ananuvajjā ca viññūnaṁ.
snp3.3:2.2	Katamehi catūhi?
snp3.3:2.3	Idha, bhikkhave, bhikkhu subhāsitaṁyeva bhāsati no dubbhāsitaṁ, dhammaṁyeva bhāsati no adhammaṁ, piyaṁyeva bhāsati no appiyaṁ, saccaṁyeva bhāsati no alikaṁ.
snp3.3:2.4	Imehi kho, bhikkhave, catūhi aṅgehi samannāgatā vācā subhāsitā hoti, no dubbhāsitā, anavajjā ca ananuvajjā ca viññūnan”ti.
snp3.3:2.5	Idamavoca bhagavā.
snp3.3:2.6	Idaṁ vatvāna sugato, athāparaṁ etadavoca satthā:
snp3.3:3.1	“Subhāsitaṁ uttamamāhu santo,
snp3.3:3.2	Dhammaṁ bhaṇe nādhammaṁ taṁ dutiyaṁ;
snp3.3:3.3	Piyaṁ bhaṇe nāppiyaṁ taṁ tatiyaṁ,
snp3.3:3.4	Saccaṁ bhaṇe nālikaṁ taṁ catutthan”ti.
snp3.3:4.1	Atha kho āyasmā vaṅgīso uṭṭhāyāsanā ekaṁsaṁ cīvaraṁ katvā yena bhagavā tenañjaliṁ paṇāmetvā bhagavantaṁ etadavoca:
snp3.3:4.2	“paṭibhāti maṁ, bhagavā, paṭibhāti maṁ, sugatā”ti.
snp3.3:4.3	“Paṭibhātu taṁ, vaṅgīsā”ti bhagavā avoca.
snp3.3:4.4	Atha kho āyasmā vaṅgīso bhagavantaṁ sammukhā sāruppāhi gāthāhi abhitthavi:
snp3.3:5.1	“Tameva vācaṁ bhāseyya,
snp3.3:5.2	yāyattānaṁ na tāpaye;
snp3.3:5.3	Pare ca na vihiṁseyya,
snp3.3:5.4	sā ve vācā subhāsitā.
snp3.3:6.1	Piyavācameva bhāseyya,
snp3.3:6.2	yā vācā paṭinanditā;
snp3.3:6.3	Yaṁ anādāya pāpāni,
snp3.3:6.4	paresaṁ bhāsate piyaṁ.
snp3.3:7.1	Saccaṁ ve amatā vācā,
snp3.3:7.2	esa dhammo sanantano;
snp3.3:7.3	Sacce atthe ca dhamme ca,
snp3.3:7.4	āhu santo patiṭṭhitā.
snp3.3:8.1	Yaṁ buddho bhāsati vācaṁ,
snp3.3:8.2	Khemaṁ nibbānapattiyā;
snp3.3:8.3	Dukkhassantakiriyāya,
snp3.3:8.4	Sā ve vācānamuttamā”ti.
snp3.3:8.5	Subhāsitasuttaṁ tatiyaṁ.
snp3.4:0.1	Sutta Nipāta 3.4
snp3.4:0.2	Pūraḷāsa (sundarikabhāradvāja) sutta
snp3.4:1.1	Evaṁ me sutaṁ—
snp3.4:1.2	ekaṁ samayaṁ bhagavā kosalesu viharati sundarikāya nadiyā tīre.
snp3.4:1.3	Tena kho pana samayena sundarikabhāradvājo brāhmaṇo sundarikāya nadiyā tīre aggiṁ juhati, aggihuttaṁ paricarati.
snp3.4:1.4	Atha kho sundarikabhāradvājo brāhmaṇo aggiṁ juhitvā aggihuttaṁ paricaritvā uṭṭhāyāsanā samantā catuddisā anuvilokesi:
snp3.4:1.5	“ko nu kho imaṁ habyasesaṁ bhuñjeyyā”ti?
snp3.4:1.6	Addasā kho sundarikabhāradvājo brāhmaṇo bhagavantaṁ avidūre aññatarasmiṁ rukkhamūle sasīsaṁ pārutaṁ nisinnaṁ;
snp3.4:1.7	disvāna vāmena hatthena habyasesaṁ gahetvā dakkhiṇena hatthena kamaṇḍaluṁ gahetvā yena bhagavā tenupasaṅkami.
snp3.4:2.1	Atha kho bhagavā sundarikabhāradvājassa brāhmaṇassa padasaddena sīsaṁ vivari.
snp3.4:2.2	Atha kho sundarikabhāradvājo brāhmaṇo: “muṇḍo ayaṁ bhavaṁ, muṇḍako ayaṁ bhavan”ti tatova puna nivattitukāmo ahosi.
snp3.4:2.3	Atha kho sundarikabhāradvājassa brāhmaṇassa etadahosi:
snp3.4:2.4	“muṇḍāpi hi idhekacce brāhmaṇā bhavanti, yannūnāhaṁ upasaṅkamitvā jātiṁ puccheyyan”ti.
snp3.4:2.5	Atha kho sundarikabhāradvājo brāhmaṇo yena bhagavā tenupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṁ etadavoca:
snp3.4:2.6	“kiṁjacco bhavan”ti?
snp3.4:3.1	Atha kho bhagavā sundarikabhāradvājaṁ brāhmaṇaṁ gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi:
snp3.4:4.1	“Na brāhmaṇo nomhi na rājaputto,
snp3.4:4.2	Na vessāyano uda koci nomhi;
snp3.4:4.3	Gottaṁ pariññāya puthujjanānaṁ,
snp3.4:4.4	Akiñcano manta carāmi loke.
snp3.4:5.1	Saṅghāṭivāsī agaho carāmi,
snp3.4:5.2	Nivuttakeso abhinibbutatto;
snp3.4:5.3	Alippamāno idha māṇavehi,
snp3.4:5.4	Akallaṁ maṁ brāhmaṇa pucchasi gottapañhaṁ”.
snp3.4:6.1	“Pucchanti ve bho brāhmaṇā brāhmaṇebhi,
snp3.4:6.2	Saha brāhmaṇo no bhavan”ti.
snp3.4:7.1	“Brāhmaṇo hi ce tvaṁ brūsi,
snp3.4:7.2	Mañca brūsi abrāhmaṇaṁ;
snp3.4:7.3	Taṁ taṁ sāvittiṁ pucchāmi,
snp3.4:7.4	Tipadaṁ catuvīsatakkharaṁ”.
snp3.4:8.1	“Kiṁ nissitā isayo manujā,
snp3.4:8.2	Khattiyā brāhmaṇā devatānaṁ;
snp3.4:8.3	Yaññamakappayiṁsu puthū idha loke”.
snp3.4:9.1	“Yadantagū vedagū yaññakāle,
snp3.4:9.2	Yassāhutiṁ labhe tassijjheti brūmi”.
snp3.4:10.1	“Addhā hi tassa hutamijjhe,
snp3.4:10.2	(iti brāhmaṇo)
snp3.4:10.3	Yaṁ tādisaṁ vedagumaddasāma;
snp3.4:10.4	Tumhādisānañhi adassanena,
snp3.4:10.5	Añño jano bhuñjati pūraḷāsaṁ”.
snp3.4:11.1	“Tasmātiha tvaṁ brāhmaṇa atthena,
snp3.4:11.2	Atthiko upasaṅkamma puccha;
snp3.4:11.3	Santaṁ vidhūmaṁ anīghaṁ nirāsaṁ,
snp3.4:11.4	Appevidha abhivinde sumedhaṁ”.
snp3.4:12.1	“Yaññe ratohaṁ bho gotama,
snp3.4:12.2	Yaññaṁ yiṭṭhukāmo nāhaṁ pajānāmi;
snp3.4:12.3	Anusāsatu maṁ bhavaṁ,
snp3.4:12.4	Yattha hutaṁ ijjhate brūhi me taṁ”.
snp3.4:13.1	“Tena hi tvaṁ, brāhmaṇa, odahassu sotaṁ; dhammaṁ te desessāmi—
snp3.4:14.1	Mā jātiṁ pucchī caraṇañca puccha,
snp3.4:14.2	Kaṭṭhā have jāyati jātavedo;
snp3.4:14.3	Nīcākulīnopi munī dhitīmā,
snp3.4:14.4	Ājāniyo hoti hirīnisedho.
snp3.4:15.1	Saccena danto damasā upeto,
snp3.4:15.2	Vedantagū vūsitabrahmacariyo;
snp3.4:15.3	Kālena tamhi habyaṁ pavecche,
snp3.4:15.4	Yo brāhmaṇo puññapekkho yajetha.
snp3.4:16.1	Ye kāme hitvā agahā caranti,
snp3.4:16.2	Susaññatattā tasaraṁva ujjuṁ;
snp3.4:16.3	Kālena tesu habyaṁ pavecche,
snp3.4:16.4	Yo brāhmaṇo puññapekkho yajetha.
snp3.4:17.1	Ye vītarāgā susamāhitindriyā,
snp3.4:17.2	Candova rāhuggahaṇā pamuttā;
snp3.4:17.3	Kālena tesu habyaṁ pavecche,
snp3.4:17.4	Yo brāhmaṇo puññapekkho yajetha.
snp3.4:18.1	Asajjamānā vicaranti loke,
snp3.4:18.2	Sadā satā hitvā mamāyitāni;
snp3.4:18.3	Kālena tesu habyaṁ pavecche,
snp3.4:18.4	Yo brāhmaṇo puññapekkho yajetha.
snp3.4:19.1	Yo kāme hitvā abhibhuyyacārī,
snp3.4:19.2	Yo vedi jātīmaraṇassa antaṁ;
snp3.4:19.3	Parinibbuto udakarahadova sīto,
snp3.4:19.4	Tathāgato arahati pūraḷāsaṁ.
snp3.4:20.1	Samo samehi visamehi dūre,
snp3.4:20.2	Tathāgato hoti anantapañño;
snp3.4:20.3	Anūpalitto idha vā huraṁ vā,
snp3.4:20.4	Tathāgato arahati pūraḷāsaṁ.
snp3.4:21.1	Yamhi na māyā vasati na māno,
snp3.4:21.2	Yo vītalobho amamo nirāso;
snp3.4:21.3	Paṇunnakodho abhinibbutatto,
snp3.4:21.4	Yo brāhmaṇo sokamalaṁ ahāsi;
snp3.4:21.5	Tathāgato arahati pūraḷāsaṁ.
snp3.4:22.1	Nivesanaṁ yo manaso ahāsi,
snp3.4:22.2	Pariggahā yassa na santi keci;
snp3.4:22.3	Anupādiyāno idha vā huraṁ vā,
snp3.4:22.4	Tathāgato arahati pūraḷāsaṁ.
snp3.4:23.1	Samāhito yo udatāri oghaṁ,
snp3.4:23.2	Dhammaṁ caññāsi paramāya diṭṭhiyā;
snp3.4:23.3	Khīṇāsavo antimadehadhārī,
snp3.4:23.4	Tathāgato arahati pūraḷāsaṁ.
snp3.4:24.1	Bhavāsavā yassa vacī kharā ca,
snp3.4:24.2	Vidhūpitā atthagatā na santi;
snp3.4:24.3	Sa vedagū sabbadhi vippamutto,
snp3.4:24.4	Tathāgato arahati pūraḷāsaṁ.
snp3.4:25.1	Saṅgātigo yassa na santi saṅgā,
snp3.4:25.2	Yo mānasattesu amānasatto;
snp3.4:25.3	Dukkhaṁ pariññāya sakhettavatthuṁ,
snp3.4:25.4	Tathāgato arahati pūraḷāsaṁ.
snp3.4:26.1	Āsaṁ anissāya vivekadassī,
snp3.4:26.2	Paravediyaṁ diṭṭhimupātivatto;
snp3.4:26.3	Ārammaṇā yassa na santi keci,
snp3.4:26.4	Tathāgato arahati pūraḷāsaṁ.
snp3.4:27.1	Paroparā yassa samecca dhammā,
snp3.4:27.2	Vidhūpitā atthagatā na santi;
snp3.4:27.3	Santo upādānakhaye vimutto,
snp3.4:27.4	Tathāgato arahati pūraḷāsaṁ.
snp3.4:28.1	Saṁyojanaṁ jātikhayantadassī,
snp3.4:28.2	Yopānudi rāgapathaṁ asesaṁ;
snp3.4:28.3	Suddho nidoso vimalo akāco,
snp3.4:28.4	Tathāgato arahati pūraḷāsaṁ.
snp3.4:29.1	Yo attano attānaṁ nānupassati,
snp3.4:29.2	Samāhito ujjugato ṭhitatto;
snp3.4:29.3	Sa ve anejo akhilo akaṅkho,
snp3.4:29.4	Tathāgato arahati pūraḷāsaṁ.
snp3.4:30.1	Mohantarā yassa na santi keci,
snp3.4:30.2	Sabbesu dhammesu ca ñāṇadassī;
snp3.4:30.3	Sarīrañca antimaṁ dhāreti,
snp3.4:30.4	Patto ca sambodhimanuttaraṁ sivaṁ;
snp3.4:30.5	Ettāvatā yakkhassa suddhi,
snp3.4:30.6	Tathāgato arahati pūraḷāsaṁ”.
snp3.4:31.1	“Hutañca mayhaṁ hutamatthu saccaṁ,
snp3.4:31.2	Yaṁ tādisaṁ vedagunaṁ alatthaṁ;
snp3.4:31.3	Brahmā hi sakkhi paṭigaṇhātu me bhagavā,
snp3.4:31.4	Bhuñjatu me bhagavā pūraḷāsaṁ”.
snp3.4:32.1	“Gāthābhigītaṁ me abhojaneyyaṁ,
snp3.4:32.2	Sampassataṁ brāhmaṇa nesa dhammo;
snp3.4:32.3	Gāthābhigītaṁ panudanti buddhā,
snp3.4:32.4	Dhamme satī brāhmaṇa vuttiresā.
snp3.4:33.1	Aññena ca kevalinaṁ mahesiṁ,
snp3.4:33.2	Khīṇāsavaṁ kukkuccavūpasantaṁ;
snp3.4:33.3	Annena pānena upaṭṭhahassu,
snp3.4:33.4	Khettañhi taṁ puññapekkhassa hoti”.
snp3.4:34.1	“Sādhāhaṁ bhagavā tathā vijaññaṁ,
snp3.4:34.2	Yo dakkhiṇaṁ bhuñjeyya mādisassa;
snp3.4:34.3	Yaṁ yaññakāle pariyesamāno,
snp3.4:34.4	Pappuyya tava sāsanaṁ”.
snp3.4:35.1	“Sārambhā yassa vigatā,
snp3.4:35.2	Cittaṁ yassa anāvilaṁ;
snp3.4:35.3	Vippamutto ca kāmehi,
snp3.4:35.4	Thinaṁ yassa panūditaṁ.
snp3.4:36.1	Sīmantānaṁ vinetāraṁ,
snp3.4:36.2	Jātimaraṇakovidaṁ;
snp3.4:36.3	Muniṁ moneyyasampannaṁ,
snp3.4:36.4	Tādisaṁ yaññamāgataṁ.
snp3.4:37.1	Bhakuṭiṁ vinayitvāna,
snp3.4:37.2	Pañjalikā namassatha;
snp3.4:37.3	Pūjetha annapānena,
snp3.4:37.4	Evaṁ ijjhanti dakkhiṇā”.
snp3.4:38.1	“Buddho bhavaṁ arahati pūraḷāsaṁ,
snp3.4:38.2	Puññakhettamanuttaraṁ;
snp3.4:38.3	Āyāgo sabbalokassa,
snp3.4:38.4	Bhoto dinnaṁ mahapphalan”ti.
snp3.4:39.1	Atha kho sundarikabhāradvājo brāhmaṇo bhagavantaṁ etadavoca:
snp3.4:39.2	“abhikkantaṁ, bho gotama, abhikkantaṁ, bho gotama.
snp3.4:39.3	Seyyathāpi, bho gotama, nikkujjitaṁ vā ukkujjeyya, paṭicchannaṁ vā vivareyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṁ ācikkheyya, andhakāre vā telapajjotaṁ dhāreyya: ‘cakkhumanto rūpāni dakkhantī’ti; evamevaṁ bhotā gotamena anekapariyāyena dhammo pakāsito.
snp3.4:39.4	Esāhaṁ bhavantaṁ gotamaṁ saraṇaṁ gacchāmi dhammañca bhikkhusaṅghañca.
snp3.4:39.5	Labheyyāhaṁ bhoto gotamassa santike pabbajjaṁ, labheyyaṁ upasampadan”ti.
snp3.4:39.6	Alattha kho sundarikabhāradvājo brāhmaṇo …pe…
snp3.4:39.7	arahataṁ ahosīti.
snp3.4:39.8	Sundarikabhāradvājasuttaṁ catutthaṁ.
snp3.5:0.1	Sutta Nipāta 3.5
snp3.5:0.2	Māghasutta
snp3.5:1.1	Evaṁ me sutaṁ—
snp3.5:1.2	ekaṁ samayaṁ bhagavā rājagahe viharati gijjhakūṭe pabbate.
snp3.5:1.3	Atha kho māgho māṇavo yena bhagavā tenupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṁ sammodi.
snp3.5:1.4	Sammodanīyaṁ kathaṁ sāraṇīyaṁ vītisāretvā ekamantaṁ nisīdi.
snp3.5:1.5	Ekamantaṁ nisinno kho māgho māṇavo bhagavantaṁ etadavoca:
snp3.5:2.1	“Ahañhi, bho gotama, dāyako dānapati vadaññū yācayogo;
snp3.5:2.2	dhammena bhoge pariyesāmi;
snp3.5:2.3	dhammena bhoge pariyesitvā dhammaladdhehi bhogehi dhammādhigatehi ekassapi dadāmi dvinnampi tiṇṇampi catunnampi pañcannampi channampi sattannampi aṭṭhannampi navannampi dasannampi dadāmi, vīsāyapi tiṁsāyapi cattālīsāyapi paññāsāyapi dadāmi, satassapi dadāmi, bhiyyopi dadāmi.
snp3.5:2.4	Kaccāhaṁ, bho gotama, evaṁ dadanto evaṁ yajanto bahuṁ puññaṁ pasavāmī”ti?
snp3.5:3.1	“Taggha tvaṁ, māṇava, evaṁ dadanto evaṁ yajanto bahuṁ puññaṁ pasavasi.
snp3.5:3.2	Yo kho, māṇava, dāyako dānapati vadaññū yācayogo;
snp3.5:3.3	dhammena bhoge pariyesati;
snp3.5:3.4	dhammena bhoge pariyesitvā dhammaladdhehi bhogehi dhammādhigatehi ekassapi dadāti …pe… satassapi dadāti, bhiyyopi dadāti, bahuṁ so puññaṁ pasavatī”ti.
snp3.5:3.5	Atha kho māgho māṇavo bhagavantaṁ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi—
snp3.5:4.1	“Pucchāmahaṁ gotamaṁ vadaññuṁ,
snp3.5:4.2	(iti māgho māṇavo)
snp3.5:4.3	Kāsāyavāsiṁ agahaṁ carantaṁ;
snp3.5:4.4	Yo yācayogo dānapati gahaṭṭho,
snp3.5:4.5	Puññatthiko yajati puññapekkho;
snp3.5:4.6	Dadaṁ paresaṁ idha annapānaṁ,
snp3.5:4.7	Kathaṁ hutaṁ yajamānassa sujjhe”.
snp3.5:5.1	“Yo yācayogo dānapati gahaṭṭho,
snp3.5:5.2	(māghāti bhagavā)
snp3.5:5.3	Puññatthiko yajati puññapekkho;
snp3.5:5.4	Dadaṁ paresaṁ idha annapānaṁ,
snp3.5:5.5	Ārādhaye dakkhiṇeyyebhi tādi”.
snp3.5:6.1	“Yo yācayogo dānapati gahaṭṭho,
snp3.5:6.2	(iti māgho māṇavo)
snp3.5:6.3	Puññatthiko yajati puññapekkho;
snp3.5:6.4	Dadaṁ paresaṁ idha annapānaṁ,
snp3.5:6.5	Akkhāhi me bhagavā dakkhiṇeyye”.
snp3.5:7.1	“Ye ve asattā vicaranti loke,
snp3.5:7.2	Akiñcanā kevalino yatattā;
snp3.5:7.3	Kālena tesu habyaṁ pavecche,
snp3.5:7.4	Yo brāhmaṇo puññapekkho yajetha.
snp3.5:8.1	Ye sabbasaṁyojanabandhanacchidā,
snp3.5:8.2	Dantā vimuttā anīghā nirāsā;
snp3.5:8.3	Kālena tesu habyaṁ pavecche,
snp3.5:8.4	Yo brāhmaṇo puññapekkho yajetha.
snp3.5:9.1	Ye sabbasaṁyojanavippamuttā,
snp3.5:9.2	Dantā vimuttā anīghā nirāsā;
snp3.5:9.3	Kālena tesu habyaṁ pavecche,
snp3.5:9.4	Yo brāhmaṇo puññapekkho yajetha.
snp3.5:10.1	Rāgañca dosañca pahāya mohaṁ,
snp3.5:10.2	Khīṇāsavā vūsitabrahmacariyā;
snp3.5:10.3	Kālena tesu habyaṁ pavecche,
snp3.5:10.4	Yo brāhmaṇo puññapekkho yajetha.
snp3.5:11.1	Yesu na māyā vasati na māno,
snp3.5:11.2	Khīṇāsavā vūsitabrahmacariyā;
snp3.5:11.3	Kālena tesu habyaṁ pavecche,
snp3.5:11.4	Yo brāhmaṇo puññapekkho yajetha.
snp3.5:12.1	Ye vītalobhā amamā nirāsā,
snp3.5:12.2	Khīṇāsavā vūsitabrahmacariyā;
snp3.5:12.3	Kālena tesu habyaṁ pavecche,
snp3.5:12.4	Yo brāhmaṇo puññapekkho yajetha.
snp3.5:13.1	Ye ve na taṇhāsu upātipannā,
snp3.5:13.2	Vitareyya oghaṁ amamā caranti;
snp3.5:13.3	Kālena tesu habyaṁ pavecche,
snp3.5:13.4	Yo brāhmaṇo puññapekkho yajetha.
snp3.5:14.1	Yesaṁ taṇhā natthi kuhiñci loke,
snp3.5:14.2	Bhavābhavāya idha vā huraṁ vā;
snp3.5:14.3	Kālena tesu habyaṁ pavecche,
snp3.5:14.4	Yo brāhmaṇo puññapekkho yajetha.
snp3.5:15.1	Ye kāme hitvā agahā caranti,
snp3.5:15.2	Susaññatattā tasaraṁva ujjuṁ;
snp3.5:15.3	Kālena tesu habyaṁ pavecche,
snp3.5:15.4	Yo brāhmaṇo puññapekkho yajetha.
snp3.5:16.1	Ye vītarāgā susamāhitindriyā,
snp3.5:16.2	Candova rāhuggahaṇā pamuttā;
snp3.5:16.3	Kālena tesu habyaṁ pavecche,
snp3.5:16.4	Yo brāhmaṇo puññapekkho yajetha.
snp3.5:17.1	Samitāvino vītarāgā akopā,
snp3.5:17.2	Yesaṁ gatī natthidha vippahāya;
snp3.5:17.3	Kālena tesu habyaṁ pavecche,
snp3.5:17.4	Yo brāhmaṇo puññapekkho yajetha.
snp3.5:18.1	Jahitvā jātimaraṇaṁ asesaṁ,
snp3.5:18.2	Kathaṅkathiṁ sabbamupātivattā;
snp3.5:18.3	Kālena tesu habyaṁ pavecche,
snp3.5:18.4	Yo brāhmaṇo puññapekkho yajetha.
snp3.5:19.1	Ye attadīpā vicaranti loke,
snp3.5:19.2	Akiñcanā sabbadhi vippamuttā;
snp3.5:19.3	Kālena tesu habyaṁ pavecche,
snp3.5:19.4	Yo brāhmaṇo puññapekkho yajetha.
snp3.5:20.1	Ye hettha jānanti yathā tathā idaṁ,
snp3.5:20.2	Ayamantimā natthi punabbhavoti;
snp3.5:20.3	Kālena tesu habyaṁ pavecche,
snp3.5:20.4	Yo brāhmaṇo puññapekkho yajetha.
snp3.5:21.1	Yo vedagū jhānarato satīmā,
snp3.5:21.2	Sambodhipatto saraṇaṁ bahūnaṁ;
snp3.5:21.3	Kālena tamhi habyaṁ pavecche,
snp3.5:21.4	Yo brāhmaṇo puññapekkho yajetha”.
snp3.5:22.1	“Addhā amoghā mama pucchanā ahu,
snp3.5:22.2	(iti māgho māṇavo)
snp3.5:22.3	Akkhāsi me bhagavā dakkhiṇeyye;
snp3.5:22.4	Tvañhettha jānāsi yathā tathā idaṁ,
snp3.5:22.5	Tathā hi te vidito esa dhammo.
snp3.5:23.1	Yo yācayogo dānapati gahaṭṭho,
snp3.5:23.2	Puññatthiko yajati puññapekkho;
snp3.5:23.3	Dadaṁ paresaṁ idha annapānaṁ,
snp3.5:23.4	Akkhāhi me bhagavā yaññasampadaṁ”.
snp3.5:24.1	“Yajassu yajamāno,
snp3.5:24.2	(māghāti bhagavā)
snp3.5:24.3	Sabbattha ca vippasādehi cittaṁ;
snp3.5:24.4	Ārammaṇaṁ yajamānassa yañño,
snp3.5:24.5	Ettha patiṭṭhāya jahāti dosaṁ.
snp3.5:25.1	So vītarāgo pavineyya dosaṁ,
snp3.5:25.2	Mettaṁ cittaṁ bhāvayamappamāṇaṁ;
snp3.5:25.3	Rattindivaṁ satatamappamatto,
snp3.5:25.4	Sabbā disā pharati appamaññaṁ”.
snp3.5:26.1	“Ko sujjhati muccati bajjhatī ca,
snp3.5:26.2	Kenattanā gacchati brahmalokaṁ;
snp3.5:26.3	Ajānato me muni brūhi puṭṭho,
snp3.5:26.4	Bhagavā hi me sakkhi brahmajjadiṭṭho;
snp3.5:26.5	Tuvañhi no brahmasamosi saccaṁ,
snp3.5:26.6	Kathaṁ upapajjati brahmalokaṁ jutima”.
snp3.5:27.1	“Yo yajati tividhaṁ yaññasampadaṁ,
snp3.5:27.2	(māghāti bhagavā)
snp3.5:27.3	Ārādhaye dakkhiṇeyyebhi tādi;
snp3.5:27.4	Evaṁ yajitvā sammā yācayogo,
snp3.5:27.5	Upapajjati brahmalokanti brūmī”ti.
snp3.5:28.1	Evaṁ vutte, māgho māṇavo bhagavantaṁ etadavoca:
snp3.5:28.2	“abhikkantaṁ, bho gotama …pe…
snp3.5:28.3	ajjatagge pāṇupetaṁ saraṇaṁ gatan”ti.
snp3.5:28.4	Māghasuttaṁ pañcamaṁ.
snp3.6:0.1	Sutta Nipāta 3.6
snp3.6:0.2	Sabhiyasutta
snp3.6:1.1	Evaṁ me sutaṁ—
snp3.6:1.2	ekaṁ samayaṁ bhagavā rājagahe viharati veḷuvane kalandakanivāpe.
snp3.6:1.3	Tena kho pana samayena sabhiyassa paribbājakassa purāṇasālohitāya devatāya pañhā uddiṭṭhā honti:
snp3.6:1.4	“yo te, sabhiya, samaṇo vā brāhmaṇo vā ime pañhe puṭṭho byākaroti tassa santike brahmacariyaṁ careyyāsī”ti.
snp3.6:2.1	Atha kho sabhiyo paribbājako tassā devatāya santike te pañhe uggahetvā ye te samaṇabrāhmaṇā saṅghino gaṇino gaṇācariyā ñātā yasassino titthakarā sādhusammatā bahujanassa, seyyathidaṁ—
snp3.6:2.2	pūraṇo kassapo makkhaligosālo ajito kesakambalo pakudho kaccāno sañcayo belaṭṭhaputto nigaṇṭho nāṭaputto—
snp3.6:2.3	te upasaṅkamitvā te pañhe pucchati.
snp3.6:2.4	Te sabhiyena paribbājakena pañhe puṭṭhā na sampāyanti;
snp3.6:2.5	asampāyantā kopañca dosañca appaccayañca pātukaronti.
snp3.6:2.6	Api ca sabhiyaṁyeva paribbājakaṁ paṭipucchanti.
snp3.6:3.1	Atha kho sabhiyassa paribbājakassa etadahosi:
snp3.6:3.2	“ye kho te bhonto samaṇabrāhmaṇā saṅghino gaṇino gaṇācariyā ñātā yasassino titthakarā sādhusammatā bahujanassa, seyyathidaṁ—
snp3.6:3.3	pūraṇo kassapo …pe… nigaṇṭho nāṭaputto—
snp3.6:3.4	te mayā pañhe puṭṭhā na sampāyanti,
snp3.6:3.5	asampāyantā kopañca dosañca appaccayañca pātukaronti;
snp3.6:3.6	api ca maññevettha paṭipucchanti.
snp3.6:3.7	Yannūnāhaṁ hīnāyāvattitvā kāme paribhuñjeyyan”ti.
snp3.6:4.1	Atha kho sabhiyassa paribbājakassa etadahosi:
snp3.6:4.2	“ayampi kho samaṇo gotamo saṅghī ceva gaṇī ca gaṇācariyo ca ñāto yasassī titthakaro sādhusammato bahujanassa;
snp3.6:4.3	yannūnāhaṁ samaṇaṁ gotamaṁ upasaṅkamitvā ime pañhe puccheyyan”ti.
snp3.6:5.1	Atha kho sabhiyassa paribbājakassa etadahosi:
snp3.6:5.2	“yepi kho te bhonto samaṇabrāhmaṇā jiṇṇā vuḍḍhā mahallakā addhagatā vayoanuppattā therā rattaññū cirapabbajitā saṅghino gaṇino gaṇācariyā ñātā yasassino titthakarā sādhusammatā bahujanassa, seyyathidaṁ—
snp3.6:5.3	pūraṇo kassapo …pe… nigaṇṭho nāṭaputto—
snp3.6:5.4	tepi mayā pañhe puṭṭhā na sampāyanti,
snp3.6:5.5	asampāyantā kopañca dosañca appaccayañca pātukaronti, api ca maññevettha paṭipucchanti;
snp3.6:5.6	kiṁ pana me samaṇo gotamo ime pañhe puṭṭho byākarissati.
snp3.6:5.7	Samaṇo hi gotamo daharo ceva jātiyā, navo ca pabbajjāyā”ti.
snp3.6:6.1	Atha kho sabhiyassa paribbājakassa etadahosi:
snp3.6:6.2	“samaṇo kho daharoti na uññātabbo na paribhotabbo.
snp3.6:6.3	Daharopi cesa samaṇo gotamo mahiddhiko hoti mahānubhāvo, yannūnāhaṁ samaṇaṁ gotamaṁ upasaṅkamitvā ime pañhe puccheyyan”ti.
snp3.6:7.1	Atha kho sabhiyo paribbājako yena rājagahaṁ tena cārikaṁ pakkāmi.
snp3.6:7.2	Anupubbena cārikaṁ caramāno yena rājagahaṁ veḷuvanaṁ kalandakanivāpo, yena bhagavā tenupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṁ sammodi.
snp3.6:7.3	Sammodanīyaṁ kathaṁ sāraṇīyaṁ vītisāretvā ekamantaṁ nisīdi.
snp3.6:7.4	Ekamantaṁ nisinno kho sabhiyo paribbājako bhagavantaṁ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi—
snp3.6:8.1	“Kaṅkhī vecikicchī āgamaṁ,
snp3.6:8.2	(iti sabhiyo)
snp3.6:8.3	Pañhe pucchituṁ abhikaṅkhamāno;
snp3.6:8.4	Tesantakaro bhavāhi pañhe me puṭṭho,
snp3.6:8.5	Anupubbaṁ anudhammaṁ byākarohi me”.
snp3.6:9.1	“Dūrato āgatosi sabhiya,
snp3.6:9.2	(iti bhagavā)
snp3.6:9.3	Pañhe pucchituṁ abhikaṅkhamāno;
snp3.6:9.4	Tesantakaro bhavāmi pañhe te puṭṭho,
snp3.6:9.5	Anupubbaṁ anudhammaṁ byākaromi te.
snp3.6:10.1	Puccha maṁ sabhiya pañhaṁ,
snp3.6:10.2	Yaṁ kiñci manasicchasi;
snp3.6:10.3	Tassa tasseva pañhassa,
snp3.6:10.4	Ahaṁ antaṁ karomi te”ti.
snp3.6:11.1	Atha kho sabhiyassa paribbājakassa etadahosi:
snp3.6:11.2	“acchariyaṁ vata bho, abbhutaṁ vata bho.
snp3.6:11.3	Yaṁ vatāhaṁ aññesu samaṇabrāhmaṇesu okāsakammamattampi nālatthaṁ taṁ me idaṁ samaṇena gotamena okāsakammaṁ katan”ti.
snp3.6:11.4	Attamano pamudito udaggo pītisomanassajāto bhagavantaṁ pañhaṁ apucchi—
snp3.6:12.1	“Kiṁpattinamāhu bhikkhunaṁ,
snp3.6:12.2	(iti sabhiyo)
snp3.6:12.3	Sorataṁ kena kathañca dantamāhu;
snp3.6:12.4	Buddhoti kathaṁ pavuccati,
snp3.6:12.5	Puṭṭho me bhagavā byākarohi”.
snp3.6:13.1	“Pajjena katena attanā,
snp3.6:13.2	(sabhiyāti bhagavā)
snp3.6:13.3	Parinibbānagato vitiṇṇakaṅkho;
snp3.6:13.4	Vibhavañca bhavañca vippahāya,
snp3.6:13.5	Vusitavā khīṇapunabbhavo sa bhikkhu.
snp3.6:14.1	Sabbattha upekkhako satimā,
snp3.6:14.2	Na so hiṁsati kañci sabbaloke;
snp3.6:14.3	Tiṇṇo samaṇo anāvilo,
snp3.6:14.4	Ussadā yassa na santi sorato so.
snp3.6:15.1	Yassindriyāni bhāvitāni,
snp3.6:15.2	Ajjhattaṁ bahiddhā ca sabbaloke;
snp3.6:15.3	Nibbijjha imaṁ parañca lokaṁ,
snp3.6:15.4	Kālaṁ kaṅkhati bhāvito sa danto.
snp3.6:16.1	Kappāni viceyya kevalāni,
snp3.6:16.2	Saṁsāraṁ dubhayaṁ cutūpapātaṁ;
snp3.6:16.3	Vigatarajamanaṅgaṇaṁ visuddhaṁ,
snp3.6:16.4	Pattaṁ jātikhayaṁ tamāhu buddhan”ti.
snp3.6:17.1	Atha kho sabhiyo paribbājako bhagavato bhāsitaṁ abhinanditvā anumoditvā attamano pamudito udaggo pītisomanassajāto bhagavantaṁ uttariṁ pañhaṁ apucchi—
snp3.6:18.1	“Kiṁpattinamāhu brāhmaṇaṁ,
snp3.6:18.2	(iti sabhiyo)
snp3.6:18.3	Samaṇaṁ kena kathañca nhātakoti;
snp3.6:18.4	Nāgoti kathaṁ pavuccati,
snp3.6:18.5	Puṭṭho me bhagavā byākarohi”.
snp3.6:19.1	“Bāhitvā sabbapāpakāni,
snp3.6:19.2	(sabhiyāti bhagavā)
snp3.6:19.3	Vimalo sādhusamāhito ṭhitatto;
snp3.6:19.4	Saṁsāramaticca kevalī so,
snp3.6:19.5	Asito tādi pavuccate sa brahmā.
snp3.6:20.1	Samitāvi pahāya puññapāpaṁ,
snp3.6:20.2	Virajo ñatvā imaṁ parañca lokaṁ;
snp3.6:20.3	Jātimaraṇaṁ upātivatto,
snp3.6:20.4	Samaṇo tādi pavuccate tathattā.
snp3.6:21.1	Ninhāya sabbapāpakāni,
snp3.6:21.2	Ajjhattaṁ bahiddhā ca sabbaloke;
snp3.6:21.3	Devamanussesu kappiyesu,
snp3.6:21.4	Kappaṁ neti tamāhu nhātakoti.
snp3.6:22.1	Āguṁ na karoti kiñci loke,
snp3.6:22.2	Sabbasaṁyoge visajja bandhanāni;
snp3.6:22.3	Sabbattha na sajjatī vimutto,
snp3.6:22.4	Nāgo tādi pavuccate tathattā”ti.
snp3.6:23.1	Atha kho sabhiyo paribbājako …pe… bhagavantaṁ uttariṁ pañhaṁ apucchi—
snp3.6:24.1	“Kaṁ khettajinaṁ vadanti buddhā,
snp3.6:24.2	(iti sabhiyo)
snp3.6:24.3	Kusalaṁ kena kathañca paṇḍitoti;
snp3.6:24.4	Muni nāma kathaṁ pavuccati,
snp3.6:24.5	Puṭṭho me bhagavā byākarohi”.
snp3.6:25.1	“Khettāni viceyya kevalāni,
snp3.6:25.2	(sabhiyāti bhagavā)
snp3.6:25.3	Dibbaṁ mānusakañca brahmakhettaṁ;
snp3.6:25.4	Sabbakhettamūlabandhanā pamutto,
snp3.6:25.5	Khettajino tādi pavuccate tathattā.
snp3.6:26.1	Kosāni viceyya kevalāni,
snp3.6:26.2	Dibbaṁ mānusakañca brahmakosaṁ;
snp3.6:26.3	Sabbakosamūlabandhanā pamutto,
snp3.6:26.4	Kusalo tādi pavuccate tathattā.
snp3.6:27.1	Dubhayāni viceyya paṇḍarāni,
snp3.6:27.2	Ajjhattaṁ bahiddhā ca suddhipañño;
snp3.6:27.3	Kaṇhaṁ sukkaṁ upātivatto,
snp3.6:27.4	Paṇḍito tādi pavuccate tathattā.
snp3.6:28.1	Asatañca satañca ñatvā dhammaṁ,
snp3.6:28.2	Ajjhattaṁ bahiddhā ca sabbaloke;
snp3.6:28.3	Devamanussehi pūjanīyo,
snp3.6:28.4	Saṅgaṁ jālamaticca so munī”ti.
snp3.6:29.1	Atha kho sabhiyo paribbājako …pe… bhagavantaṁ uttariṁ pañhaṁ apucchi—
snp3.6:30.1	“Kiṁpattinamāhu vedaguṁ,
snp3.6:30.2	(iti sabhiyo)
snp3.6:30.3	Anuviditaṁ kena kathañca viriyavāti;
snp3.6:30.4	Ājāniyo kinti nāma hoti,
snp3.6:30.5	Puṭṭho me bhagavā byākarohi”.
snp3.6:31.1	“Vedāni viceyya kevalāni,
snp3.6:31.2	(sabhiyāti bhagavā)
snp3.6:31.3	Samaṇānaṁ yānidhatthi brāhmaṇānaṁ;
snp3.6:31.4	Sabbavedanāsu vītarāgo,
snp3.6:31.5	Sabbaṁ vedamaticca vedagū so.
snp3.6:32.1	Anuvicca papañcanāmarūpaṁ,
snp3.6:32.2	Ajjhattaṁ bahiddhā ca rogamūlaṁ;
snp3.6:32.3	Sabbarogamūlabandhanā pamutto,
snp3.6:32.4	Anuvidito tādi pavuccate tathattā.
snp3.6:33.1	Virato idha sabbapāpakehi,
snp3.6:33.2	Nirayadukkhaṁ aticca viriyavāso;
snp3.6:33.3	So vīriyavā padhānavā,
snp3.6:33.4	Dhīro tādi pavuccate tathattā.
snp3.6:34.1	Yassassu lunāni bandhanāni,
snp3.6:34.2	Ajjhattaṁ bahiddhā ca saṅgamūlaṁ;
snp3.6:34.3	Sabbasaṅgamūlabandhanā pamutto,
snp3.6:34.4	Ājāniyo tādi pavuccate tathattā”ti.
snp3.6:35.1	Atha kho sabhiyo paribbājako …pe… bhagavantaṁ uttariṁ pañhaṁ apucchi—
snp3.6:36.1	“Kiṁpattinamāhu sottiyaṁ,
snp3.6:36.2	(iti sabhiyo)
snp3.6:36.3	Ariyaṁ kena kathañca caraṇavāti;
snp3.6:36.4	Paribbājako kinti nāma hoti,
snp3.6:36.5	Puṭṭho me bhagavā byākarohi”.
snp3.6:37.1	“Sutvā sabbadhammaṁ abhiññāya loke,
snp3.6:37.2	(sabhiyāti bhagavā)
snp3.6:37.3	Sāvajjānavajjaṁ yadatthi kiñci;
snp3.6:37.4	Abhibhuṁ akathaṅkathiṁ vimuttaṁ,
snp3.6:37.5	Anighaṁ sabbadhimāhu sottiyoti.
snp3.6:38.1	Chetvā āsavāni ālayāni,
snp3.6:38.2	Vidvā so na upeti gabbhaseyyaṁ;
snp3.6:38.3	Saññaṁ tividhaṁ panujja paṅkaṁ,
snp3.6:38.4	Kappaṁ neti tamāhu ariyoti.
snp3.6:39.1	Yo idha caraṇesu pattipatto,
snp3.6:39.2	Kusalo sabbadā ājānāti dhammaṁ;
snp3.6:39.3	Sabbattha na sajjati vimuttacitto,
snp3.6:39.4	Paṭighā yassa na santi caraṇavā so.
snp3.6:40.1	Dukkhavepakkaṁ yadatthi kammaṁ,
snp3.6:40.2	Uddhamadho tiriyaṁ vāpi majjhe;
snp3.6:40.3	Paribbājayitvā pariññacārī,
snp3.6:40.4	Māyaṁ mānamathopi lobhakodhaṁ;
snp3.6:40.5	Pariyantamakāsi nāmarūpaṁ,
snp3.6:40.6	Taṁ paribbājakamāhu pattipattan”ti.
snp3.6:41.1	Atha kho sabhiyo paribbājako bhagavato bhāsitaṁ abhinanditvā anumoditvā attamano pamudito udaggo pītisomanassajāto uṭṭhāyāsanā ekaṁsaṁ uttarāsaṅgaṁ karitvā yena bhagavā tenañjaliṁ paṇāmetvā bhagavantaṁ sammukhā sāruppāhi gāthāhi abhitthavi:
snp3.6:42.1	“Yāni ca tīṇi yāni ca saṭṭhi,
snp3.6:42.2	Samaṇappavādasitāni bhūripañña;
snp3.6:42.3	Saññakkharasaññanissitāni,
snp3.6:42.4	Osaraṇāni vineyya oghatamagā.
snp3.6:43.1	Antagūsi pāragū dukkhassa,
snp3.6:43.2	Arahāsi sammāsambuddho khīṇāsavaṁ taṁ maññe;
snp3.6:43.3	Jutimā mutimā pahūtapañño,
snp3.6:43.4	Dukkhassantakara atāresi maṁ.
snp3.6:44.1	Yaṁ me kaṅkhitamaññāsi,
snp3.6:44.2	Vicikicchā maṁ tārayi namo te;
snp3.6:44.3	Muni monapathesu pattipatta,
snp3.6:44.4	Akhila ādiccabandhu soratosi.
snp3.6:45.1	Yā me kaṅkhā pure āsi,
snp3.6:45.2	Taṁ me byākāsi cakkhumā;
snp3.6:45.3	Addhā munīsi sambuddho,
snp3.6:45.4	Natthi nīvaraṇā tava.
snp3.6:46.1	Upāyāsā ca te sabbe,
snp3.6:46.2	Viddhastā vinaḷīkatā;
snp3.6:46.3	Sītibhūto damappatto,
snp3.6:46.4	Dhitimā saccanikkamo.
snp3.6:47.1	Tassa te nāganāgassa,
snp3.6:47.2	Mahāvīrassa bhāsato;
snp3.6:47.3	Sabbe devānumodanti,
snp3.6:47.4	Ubho nāradapabbatā.
snp3.6:48.1	Namo te purisājañña,
snp3.6:48.2	Namo te purisuttama;
snp3.6:48.3	Sadevakasmiṁ lokasmiṁ,
snp3.6:48.4	Natthi te paṭipuggalo.
snp3.6:49.1	Tuvaṁ buddho tuvaṁ satthā,
snp3.6:49.2	Tuvaṁ mārābhibhū muni;
snp3.6:49.3	Tuvaṁ anusaye chetvā,
snp3.6:49.4	Tiṇṇo tāresimaṁ pajaṁ.
snp3.6:50.1	Upadhī te samatikkantā,
snp3.6:50.2	Āsavā te padālitā;
snp3.6:50.3	Sīhosi anupādāno,
snp3.6:50.4	Pahīnabhayabheravo.
snp3.6:51.1	Puṇḍarīkaṁ yathā vaggu,
snp3.6:51.2	Toye na upalimpati;
snp3.6:51.3	Evaṁ puññe ca pāpe ca,
snp3.6:51.4	Ubhaye tvaṁ na limpasi;
snp3.6:51.5	Pāde vīra pasārehi,
snp3.6:51.6	Sabhiyo vandati satthuno”ti.
snp3.6:52.1	Atha kho sabhiyo paribbājako bhagavato pādesu sirasā nipatitvā bhagavantaṁ etadavoca:
snp3.6:52.2	“abhikkantaṁ, bhante …pe…
snp3.6:52.3	esāhaṁ bhagavantaṁ saraṇaṁ gacchāmi dhammañca bhikkhusaṅghañca;
snp3.6:52.4	labheyyāhaṁ, bhante, bhagavato santike pabbajjaṁ, labheyyaṁ upasampadan”ti.
snp3.6:53.1	“Yo kho, sabhiya, aññatitthiyapubbo imasmiṁ dhammavinaye ākaṅkhati pabbajjaṁ, ākaṅkhati upasampadaṁ, so cattāro māse parivasati; catunnaṁ māsānaṁ accayena āraddhacittā bhikkhū pabbājenti, upasampādenti bhikkhubhāvāya.
snp3.6:53.2	Api ca mettha puggalavemattatā viditā”ti.
snp3.6:54.1	“Sace, bhante, aññatitthiyapubbā imasmiṁ dhammavinaye ākaṅkhantā pabbajjaṁ, ākaṅkhantā upasampadaṁ cattāro māse parivasanti, catunnaṁ māsānaṁ accayena āraddhacittā bhikkhū pabbājenti, upasampādenti bhikkhubhāvāya, ahaṁ cattāri vassāni parivasissāmi; catunnaṁ vassānaṁ accayena āraddhacittā bhikkhū pabbājentu upasampādentu bhikkhubhāvāyā”ti.
snp3.6:54.2	Alattha kho sabhiyo paribbājako bhagavato santike pabbajjaṁ alattha upasampadaṁ …pe…
snp3.6:54.3	aññataro kho panāyasmā sabhiyo arahataṁ ahosīti.
snp3.6:54.4	Sabhiyasuttaṁ chaṭṭhaṁ.
snp3.7:0.1	Sutta Nipāta 3.7
snp3.7:0.2	Selasutta
snp3.7:1.1	Evaṁ me sutaṁ—
snp3.7:1.2	ekaṁ samayaṁ bhagavā aṅguttarāpesu cārikaṁ caramāno mahatā bhikkhusaṅghena saddhiṁ aḍḍhateḷasehi bhikkhusatehi yena āpaṇaṁ nāma aṅguttarāpānaṁ nigamo tadavasari.
snp3.7:1.3	Assosi kho keṇiyo jaṭilo:
snp3.7:1.4	“samaṇo khalu, bho, gotamo sakyaputto sakyakulā pabbajito aṅguttarāpesu cārikaṁ caramāno mahatā bhikkhusaṅghena saddhiṁ aḍḍhateḷasehi bhikkhusatehi āpaṇaṁ anuppatto.
snp3.7:1.5	Taṁ kho pana bhavantaṁ gotamaṁ evaṁ kalyāṇo kittisaddo abbhuggato:
snp3.7:1.6	‘itipi so bhagavā arahaṁ sammāsambuddho vijjācaraṇasampanno sugato lokavidū anuttaro purisadammasārathi satthā devamanussānaṁ buddho bhagavā’ti.
snp3.7:1.7	So imaṁ lokaṁ sadevakaṁ samārakaṁ sabrahmakaṁ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṁ pajaṁ sadevamanussaṁ sayaṁ abhiññā sacchikatvā pavedeti.
snp3.7:1.8	So dhammaṁ deseti ādikalyāṇaṁ majjhekalyāṇaṁ pariyosānakalyāṇaṁ sātthaṁ sabyañjanaṁ, kevalaparipuṇṇaṁ parisuddhaṁ brahmacariyaṁ pakāseti.
snp3.7:1.9	Sādhu kho pana tathārūpānaṁ arahataṁ dassanaṁ hotī”ti.
snp3.7:2.1	Atha kho keṇiyo jaṭilo yena bhagavā tenupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṁ sammodi.
snp3.7:2.2	Sammodanīyaṁ kathaṁ sāraṇīyaṁ vītisāretvā ekamantaṁ nisīdi.
snp3.7:2.3	Ekamantaṁ nisinnaṁ kho keṇiyaṁ jaṭilaṁ bhagavā dhammiyā kathāya sandassesi samādapesi samuttejesi sampahaṁsesi.
snp3.7:2.4	Atha kho keṇiyo jaṭilo bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassito samādapito samuttejito sampahaṁsito bhagavantaṁ etadavoca:
snp3.7:2.5	“adhivāsetu me bhavaṁ gotamo svātanāya bhattaṁ saddhiṁ bhikkhusaṅghenā”ti.
snp3.7:2.6	Evaṁ vutte, bhagavā keṇiyaṁ jaṭilaṁ etadavoca:
snp3.7:2.7	“mahā kho, keṇiya, bhikkhusaṅgho aḍḍhateḷasāni bhikkhusatāni; tvañca brāhmaṇesu abhippasanno”ti.
snp3.7:3.1	Dutiyampi kho keṇiyo jaṭilo bhagavantaṁ etadavoca:
snp3.7:3.2	“kiñcāpi, bho gotama, mahā bhikkhusaṅgho aḍḍhateḷasāni bhikkhusatāni, ahañca brāhmaṇesu abhippasanno;
snp3.7:3.3	adhivāsetu me bhavaṁ gotamo svātanāya bhattaṁ saddhiṁ bhikkhusaṅghenā”ti.
snp3.7:3.4	Dutiyampi kho bhagavā keṇiyaṁ jaṭilaṁ etadavoca:
snp3.7:3.5	“mahā kho, keṇiya, bhikkhusaṅgho aḍḍhateḷasāni bhikkhusatāni; tvañca brāhmaṇesu abhippasanno”ti.
snp3.7:4.1	Tatiyampi kho keṇiyo jaṭilo bhagavantaṁ etadavoca:
snp3.7:4.2	“kiñcāpi, bho gotama, mahā bhikkhusaṅgho aḍḍhateḷasāni bhikkhusatāni, ahañca brāhmaṇesu abhippasanno,
snp3.7:4.3	adhivāsetu me bhavaṁ gotamo svātanāya bhattaṁ saddhiṁ bhikkhusaṅghenā”ti.
snp3.7:4.4	Adhivāsesi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvena.
snp3.7:4.5	Atha kho keṇiyo jaṭilo bhagavato adhivāsanaṁ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā yena sako assamo tenupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā mittāmacce ñātisālohite āmantesi:
snp3.7:4.6	“suṇantu me bhavanto mittāmaccā ñātisālohitā,
snp3.7:4.7	samaṇo me gotamo nimantito svātanāya bhattaṁ saddhiṁ bhikkhusaṅghena,
snp3.7:4.8	yena me kāyaveyyāvaṭikaṁ kareyyāthā”ti.
snp3.7:4.9	“Evaṁ, bho”ti kho keṇiyassa jaṭilassa mittāmaccā ñātisālohitā keṇiyassa jaṭilassa paṭissutvā appekacce uddhanāni khaṇanti, appekacce kaṭṭhāni phālenti, appekacce bhājanāni dhovanti, appekacce udakamaṇikaṁ patiṭṭhāpenti, appekacce āsanāni paññāpenti.
snp3.7:4.10	Keṇiyo pana jaṭilo sāmaṁyeva maṇḍalamāḷaṁ paṭiyādeti.
snp3.7:5.1	Tena kho pana samayena selo brāhmaṇo āpaṇe paṭivasati, tiṇṇaṁ vedānaṁ pāragū sanighaṇḍukeṭubhānaṁ sākkharappabhedānaṁ itihāsapañcamānaṁ padako veyyākaraṇo lokāyatamahāpurisalakkhaṇesu anavayo, tīṇi ca māṇavakasatāni mante vāceti.
snp3.7:6.1	Tena kho pana samayena keṇiyo jaṭilo sele brāhmaṇe abhippasanno hoti.
snp3.7:6.2	Atha kho selo brāhmaṇo tīhi māṇavakasatehi parivuto jaṅghāvihāraṁ anucaṅkamamāno anuvicaramāno yena keṇiyassa jaṭilassa assamo tenupasaṅkami.
snp3.7:6.3	Addasā kho selo brāhmaṇo keṇiyassa jaṭilassa assame appekacce uddhanāni khaṇante …pe… appekacce āsanāni paññapente, keṇiyaṁ pana jaṭilaṁ sāmaṁyeva maṇḍalamāḷaṁ paṭiyādentaṁ.
snp3.7:6.4	Disvāna keṇiyaṁ jaṭilaṁ etadavoca:
snp3.7:6.5	“kiṁ nu kho bhoto keṇiyassa āvāho vā bhavissati, vivāho vā bhavissati, mahāyañño vā paccupaṭṭhito, rājā vā māgadho seniyo bimbisāro nimantito svātanāya saddhiṁ balakāyenā”ti?
snp3.7:7.1	“Na me, bho sela, āvāho vā bhavissati vivāho vā, nāpi rājā māgadho seniyo bimbisāro nimantito svātanāya saddhiṁ balakāyena;
snp3.7:7.2	api ca kho me mahāyañño paccupaṭṭhito.
snp3.7:7.3	Atthi samaṇo gotamo sakyaputto sakyakulā pabbajito aṅguttarāpesu cārikaṁ caramāno mahatā bhikkhusaṅghena saddhiṁ aḍḍhateḷasehi bhikkhusatehi āpaṇaṁ anuppatto.
snp3.7:7.4	Taṁ kho pana bhavantaṁ gotamaṁ …pe…
snp3.7:7.5	buddho bhagavāti.
snp3.7:7.6	So me nimantito svātanāya bhattaṁ saddhiṁ bhikkhusaṅghenā”ti.
snp3.7:7.7	“Buddhoti, bho keṇiya, vadesi”?
snp3.7:7.8	“Buddhoti, bho sela, vadāmi”.
snp3.7:7.9	“Buddhoti, bho keṇiya, vadesi”?
snp3.7:7.10	“Buddhoti, bho sela, vadāmī”ti.
snp3.7:8.1	Atha kho selassa brāhmaṇassa etadahosi:
snp3.7:8.2	“ghosopi kho eso dullabho lokasmiṁ, yadidaṁ buddhoti.
snp3.7:8.3	Āgatāni kho panamhākaṁ mantesu dvattiṁsamahāpurisalakkhaṇāni, yehi samannāgatassa mahāpurisassa dveva gatiyo bhavanti anaññā.
snp3.7:8.4	Sace agāraṁ ajjhāvasati rājā hoti cakkavattī dhammiko dhammarājā cāturanto vijitāvī janapadatthāvariyappatto sattaratanasamannāgato.
snp3.7:8.5	Tassimāni satta ratanāni bhavanti, seyyathidaṁ—
snp3.7:8.6	cakkaratanaṁ, hatthiratanaṁ, assaratanaṁ, maṇiratanaṁ, itthiratanaṁ, gahapatiratanaṁ, pariṇāyakaratanameva sattamaṁ.
snp3.7:8.7	Parosahassaṁ kho panassa puttā bhavanti sūrā vīraṅgarūpā parasenappamaddanā.
snp3.7:8.8	So imaṁ pathaviṁ sāgarapariyantaṁ adaṇḍena asatthena dhammena abhivijiya ajjhāvasati.
snp3.7:8.9	Sace kho pana agārasmā anagāriyaṁ pabbajati, arahaṁ hoti sammāsambuddho loke vivaṭṭacchado.
snp3.7:8.10	Kahaṁ pana, bho keṇiya, etarahi so bhavaṁ gotamo viharati arahaṁ sammāsambuddho”ti?
snp3.7:9.1	Evaṁ vutte, keṇiyo jaṭilo dakkhiṇaṁ bāhuṁ paggahetvā selaṁ brāhmaṇaṁ etadavoca:
snp3.7:9.2	“yenesā, bho sela, nīlavanarājī”ti.
snp3.7:9.3	Atha kho selo brāhmaṇo tīhi māṇavakasatehi saddhiṁ yena bhagavā tenupasaṅkami.
snp3.7:9.4	Atha kho selo brāhmaṇo te māṇavake āmantesi:
snp3.7:9.5	“appasaddā bhonto āgacchantu, pade padaṁ nikkhipantā.
snp3.7:9.6	Durāsadā hi te bhagavanto sīhāva ekacarā.
snp3.7:9.7	Yadā cāhaṁ, bho, samaṇena gotamena saddhiṁ manteyyaṁ, mā me bhonto antarantarā kathaṁ opātetha;
snp3.7:9.8	kathāpariyosānaṁ me bhavanto āgamentū”ti.
snp3.7:10.1	Atha kho selo brāhmaṇo yena bhagavā tenupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṁ sammodi.
snp3.7:10.2	Sammodanīyaṁ kathaṁ sāraṇīyaṁ vītisāretvā ekamantaṁ nisīdi.
snp3.7:10.3	Ekamantaṁ nisinno kho selo brāhmaṇo bhagavato kāye dvattiṁsamahāpurisalakkhaṇāni samannesi.
snp3.7:10.4	Addasā kho selo brāhmaṇo bhagavato kāye dvattiṁsamahāpurisalakkhaṇāni yebhuyyena ṭhapetvā dve.
snp3.7:10.5	Dvīsu mahāpurisalakkhaṇesu kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuccati na sampasīdati—
snp3.7:10.6	kosohite ca vatthaguyhe pahūtajivhatāya cāti.
snp3.7:11.1	Atha kho bhagavato etadahosi:
snp3.7:11.2	“passati kho me ayaṁ selo brāhmaṇo dvattiṁsamahāpurisalakkhaṇāni yebhuyyena ṭhapetvā dve.
snp3.7:11.3	Dvīsu mahāpurisalakkhaṇesu kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuccati na sampasīdati—
snp3.7:11.4	kosohite ca vatthaguyhe pahūtajivhatāya cā”ti.
snp3.7:11.5	Atha kho bhagavā tathārūpaṁ iddhābhisaṅkhāraṁ abhisaṅkhāsi, yathā addasa selo brāhmaṇo bhagavato kosohitaṁ vatthaguyhaṁ.
snp3.7:11.6	Atha kho bhagavā jivhaṁ ninnāmetvā ubhopi kaṇṇasotāni anumasi paṭimasi, ubhopi nāsikasotāni anumasi paṭimasi, kevalampi nalāṭamaṇḍalaṁ jivhāya chādesi.
snp3.7:12.1	Atha kho selassa brāhmaṇassa etadahosi:
snp3.7:12.2	“samannāgato kho samaṇo gotamo dvattiṁsamahāpurisalakkhaṇehi paripuṇṇehi, no aparipuṇṇehi.
snp3.7:12.3	No ca kho naṁ jānāmi buddho vā no vā.
snp3.7:12.4	Sutaṁ kho pana metaṁ brāhmaṇānaṁ vuḍḍhānaṁ mahallakānaṁ ācariyapācariyānaṁ bhāsamānānaṁ:
snp3.7:12.5	‘ye te bhavanti arahanto sammāsambuddhā, te sake vaṇṇe bhaññamāne attānaṁ pātukarontī’ti.
snp3.7:12.6	Yannūnāhaṁ samaṇaṁ gotamaṁ sammukhā sāruppāhi gāthāhi abhitthaveyyan”ti.
snp3.7:12.7	Atha kho selo brāhmaṇo bhagavantaṁ sammukhā sāruppāhi gāthāhi abhitthavi:
snp3.7:13.1	“Paripuṇṇakāyo suruci,
snp3.7:13.2	Sujāto cārudassano;
snp3.7:13.3	Suvaṇṇavaṇṇosi bhagavā,
snp3.7:13.4	Susukkadāṭhosi vīriyavā.
snp3.7:14.1	Narassa hi sujātassa,
snp3.7:14.2	ye bhavanti viyañjanā;
snp3.7:14.3	Sabbe te tava kāyasmiṁ,
snp3.7:14.4	mahāpurisalakkhaṇā.
snp3.7:15.1	Pasannanetto sumukho,
snp3.7:15.2	brahā uju patāpavā;
snp3.7:15.3	Majjhe samaṇasaṅghassa,
snp3.7:15.4	ādiccova virocasi.
snp3.7:16.1	Kalyāṇadassano bhikkhu,
snp3.7:16.2	kañcanasannibhattaco;
snp3.7:16.3	Kiṁ te samaṇabhāvena,
snp3.7:16.4	evaṁ uttamavaṇṇino.

snp3.7:17.2	cakkavattī rathesabho;

snp3.7:17.4	jambusaṇḍassa issaro.

snp3.7:19.3	Dhammarājā anuttaro;

snp3.7:19.5	Cakkaṁ appaṭivattiyaṁ”.

snp3.7:20.3	Dhammarājā anuttaro;
snp3.7:20.4	‘Dhammena cakkaṁ vattemi’,
snp3.7:20.5	Iti bhāsasi gotama.
snp3.7:21.1	Ko nu senāpati bhoto,
snp3.7:21.2	Sāvako satthuranvayo;
snp3.7:21.3	Ko te tamanuvatteti,
snp3.7:21.4	Dhammacakkaṁ pavattitaṁ”.
snp3.7:22.1	“Mayā pavattitaṁ cakkaṁ,
snp3.7:22.2	(selāti bhagavā)
snp3.7:22.3	Dhammacakkaṁ anuttaraṁ;
snp3.7:22.4	Sāriputto anuvatteti,
snp3.7:22.5	Anujāto tathāgataṁ.
snp3.7:23.1	Abhiññeyyaṁ abhiññātaṁ,
snp3.7:23.2	Bhāvetabbañca bhāvitaṁ;
snp3.7:23.3	Pahātabbaṁ pahīnaṁ me,
snp3.7:23.4	Tasmā buddhosmi brāhmaṇa.
snp3.7:24.1	Vinayassu mayi kaṅkhaṁ,
snp3.7:24.2	Adhimuccassu brāhmaṇa;
snp3.7:24.3	Dullabhaṁ dassanaṁ hoti,
snp3.7:24.4	Sambuddhānaṁ abhiṇhaso.
snp3.7:25.1	Yesaṁ ve dullabho loke,

snp3.7:25.4	Sallakatto anuttaro.
snp3.7:26.1	Brahmabhūto atitulo,
snp3.7:26.2	Mārasenappamaddano;

snp3.7:27.2	Yathā bhāsati cakkhumā;

snp3.7:28.1	Brahmabhūtaṁ atitulaṁ,
snp3.7:28.2	Mārasenappamaddanaṁ;

snp3.7:28.4	Api kaṇhābhijātiko.

snp3.7:29.2	Yo vā nicchati gacchatu;

snp3.7:29.4	Varapaññassa santike”.

snp3.7:30.2	Sammāsambuddhasāsane;

snp3.7:30.4	Varapaññassa santike”.

snp3.7:31.2	Yācanti pañjalīkatā;

snp3.7:31.4	Bhagavā tava santike”.
snp3.7:32.1	“Svākkhātaṁ brahmacariyaṁ,
snp3.7:32.2	(selāti bhagavā)
snp3.7:32.3	Sandiṭṭhikamakālikaṁ;
snp3.7:32.4	Yattha amoghā pabbajjā,
snp3.7:32.5	Appamattassa sikkhato”ti.
snp3.7:33.1	Alattha kho selo brāhmaṇo sapariso bhagavato santike pabbajjaṁ, alattha upasampadaṁ.
snp3.7:33.2	Atha kho keṇiyo jaṭilo tassā rattiyā accayena sake assame paṇītaṁ khādanīyaṁ bhojanīyaṁ paṭiyādāpetvā bhagavato kālaṁ ārocāpesi:
snp3.7:33.3	“kālo, bho gotama, niṭṭhitaṁ bhattan”ti.
snp3.7:33.4	Atha kho bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṁ nivāsetvā pattacīvaramādāya yena keṇiyassa jaṭilassa assamo tenupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā paññatte āsane nisīdi saddhiṁ bhikkhusaṅghena.
snp3.7:34.1	Atha kho keṇiyo jaṭilo buddhappamukhaṁ bhikkhusaṅghaṁ paṇītena khādanīyena bhojanīyena sahatthā santappesi sampavāresi.
snp3.7:34.2	Atha kho keṇiyo jaṭilo bhagavantaṁ bhuttāviṁ onītapattapāṇiṁ aññataraṁ nīcaṁ āsanaṁ gahetvā ekamantaṁ nisīdi.
snp3.7:34.3	Ekamantaṁ nisinnaṁ kho keṇiyaṁ jaṭilaṁ bhagavā imāhi gāthāhi anumodi:
snp3.7:35.1	“Aggihuttamukhā yaññā,
snp3.7:35.2	Sāvittī chandaso mukhaṁ;
snp3.7:35.3	Rājā mukhaṁ manussānaṁ,
snp3.7:35.4	Nadīnaṁ sāgaro mukhaṁ.
snp3.7:36.1	Nakkhattānaṁ mukhaṁ cando,
snp3.7:36.2	Ādicco tapataṁ mukhaṁ;
snp3.7:36.3	Puññaṁ ākaṅkhamānānaṁ,
snp3.7:36.4	Saṅgho ve yajataṁ mukhan”ti.
snp3.7:37.1	Atha kho bhagavā keṇiyaṁ jaṭilaṁ imāhi gāthāhi anumoditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pakkāmi.
snp3.7:37.2	Atha kho āyasmā selo sapariso eko vūpakaṭṭho appamatto ātāpī pahitatto viharanto nacirasseva …pe…
snp3.7:37.3	aññataro kho panāyasmā selo sapariso arahataṁ ahosi.
snp3.7:38.1	Atha kho āyasmā selo sapariso yena bhagavā tenupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā ekaṁsaṁ cīvaraṁ katvā yena bhagavā tenañjaliṁ paṇāmetvā bhagavantaṁ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi:
snp3.7:39.1	“Yaṁ taṁ saraṇamāgamha,
snp3.7:39.2	Ito aṭṭhami cakkhuma;
snp3.7:39.3	Sattarattena bhagavā,
snp3.7:39.4	Dantamha tava sāsane.
snp3.7:40.1	Tuvaṁ buddho tuvaṁ satthā,
snp3.7:40.2	Tuvaṁ mārābhibhū muni;
snp3.7:40.3	Tuvaṁ anusaye chetvā,
snp3.7:40.4	Tiṇṇo tāresimaṁ pajaṁ.
snp3.7:41.1	Upadhī te samatikkantā,

snp3.7:41.4	Pahīnabhayabheravo.

snp3.7:42.3	Pāde vīra pasārehi,
snp3.7:42.4	Nāgā vandantu satthuno”ti.

snp3.8:0.1	Sutta Nipāta 3.8
snp3.8:0.2	Sallasutta
snp3.8:1.1	Animittamanaññātaṁ,
snp3.8:1.2	maccānaṁ idha jīvitaṁ;
snp3.8:1.3	Kasirañca parittañca,
snp3.8:1.4	tañca dukkhena saṁyutaṁ.
snp3.8:2.1	Na hi so upakkamo atthi,
snp3.8:2.2	yena jātā na miyyare;
snp3.8:2.3	Jarampi patvā maraṇaṁ,
snp3.8:2.4	evaṁdhammā hi pāṇino.
snp3.8:3.1	Phalānamiva pakkānaṁ,
snp3.8:3.2	pāto patanato bhayaṁ;

snp3.8:3.4	niccaṁ maraṇato bhayaṁ.
snp3.8:4.1	Yathāpi kumbhakārassa,
snp3.8:4.2	katā mattikabhājanā;
snp3.8:4.3	Sabbe bhedanapariyantā,
snp3.8:4.4	evaṁ maccāna jīvitaṁ.

snp3.8:5.2	ye bālā ye ca paṇḍitā;

snp3.8:6.1	Tesaṁ maccuparetānaṁ,
snp3.8:6.2	gacchataṁ paralokato;
snp3.8:6.3	Na pitā tāyate puttaṁ,
snp3.8:6.4	ñātī vā pana ñātake.

snp3.8:7.2	passa lālapataṁ puthu;

snp3.8:8.2	Maccunā ca jarāya ca;

snp3.9:0.1	Sutta Nipāta 3.9
snp3.9:0.2	Vāseṭṭhasutta
snp3.9:1.1	Evaṁ me sutaṁ—
snp3.9:1.2	ekaṁ samayaṁ bhagavā icchānaṅgale viharati icchānaṅgalavanasaṇḍe.
snp3.9:1.3	Tena kho pana samayena sambahulā abhiññātā abhiññātā brāhmaṇamahāsālā icchānaṅgale paṭivasanti, seyyathidaṁ—
snp3.9:1.4	caṅkī brāhmaṇo, tārukkho brāhmaṇo, pokkharasāti brāhmaṇo, jāṇussoṇi brāhmaṇo, todeyyo brāhmaṇo, aññe ca abhiññātā abhiññātā brāhmaṇamahāsālā.
snp3.9:1.5	Atha kho vāseṭṭhabhāradvājānaṁ māṇavānaṁ jaṅghāvihāraṁ anucaṅkamantānaṁ anuvicarantānaṁ ayamantarākathā udapādi:
snp3.9:1.6	“kathaṁ, bho, brāhmaṇo hotī”ti?
snp3.9:2.1	Bhāradvājo māṇavo evamāha:
snp3.9:2.2	“yato kho, bho, ubhato sujāto hoti mātito ca pitito ca saṁsuddhagahaṇiko yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhitto anupakkuṭṭho jātivādena,
snp3.9:2.3	ettāvatā kho bho brāhmaṇo hotī”ti.
snp3.9:3.1	Vāseṭṭho māṇavo evamāha:
snp3.9:3.2	“yato kho, bho, sīlavā ca hoti vatasampanno ca,
snp3.9:3.3	ettāvatā kho, bho, brāhmaṇo hotī”ti.
snp3.9:3.4	Neva kho asakkhi bhāradvājo māṇavo vāseṭṭhaṁ māṇavaṁ saññāpetuṁ, na pana asakkhi vāseṭṭho māṇavo bhāradvājaṁ māṇavaṁ saññāpetuṁ.
snp3.9:4.1	Atha kho vāseṭṭho māṇavo bhāradvājaṁ māṇavaṁ āmantesi:
snp3.9:4.2	“ayaṁ kho, bho bhāradvāja, samaṇo gotamo sakyaputto sakyakulā pabbajito icchānaṅgale viharati icchānaṅgalavanasaṇḍe;
snp3.9:4.3	taṁ kho pana bhavantaṁ gotamaṁ evaṁ kalyāṇo kittisaddo abbhuggato:
snp3.9:4.4	‘itipi …pe… buddho bhagavā’ti.
snp3.9:4.5	Āyāma, bho bhāradvāja, yena samaṇo gotamo tenupasaṅkamissāma; upasaṅkamitvā samaṇaṁ gotamaṁ etamatthaṁ pucchissāma.
snp3.9:4.6	Yathā no samaṇo gotamo byākarissati tathā naṁ dhāressāmā”ti.
snp3.9:4.7	“Evaṁ, bho”ti kho bhāradvājo māṇavo vāseṭṭhassa māṇavassa paccassosi.
snp3.9:5.1	Atha kho vāseṭṭhabhāradvājā māṇavā yena bhagavā tenupasaṅkamiṁsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṁ sammodiṁsu.
snp3.9:5.2	Sammodanīyaṁ kathaṁ sāraṇīyaṁ vītisāretvā ekamantaṁ nisīdiṁsu.
snp3.9:5.3	Ekamantaṁ nisinno kho vāseṭṭho māṇavo bhagavantaṁ gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi:
snp3.9:6.1	“Anuññātapaṭiññātā,
snp3.9:6.2	tevijjā mayamasmubho;
snp3.9:6.3	Ahaṁ pokkharasātissa,
snp3.9:6.4	tārukkhassāyaṁ māṇavo.
snp3.9:7.1	Tevijjānaṁ yadakkhātaṁ,
snp3.9:7.2	Tatra kevalinosmase;
snp3.9:7.3	Padakasma veyyākaraṇā,
snp3.9:7.4	Jappe ācariyasādisā.
snp3.9:8.1	Tesaṁ no jātivādasmiṁ,
snp3.9:8.2	vivādo atthi gotama;
snp3.9:8.3	Jātiyā brāhmaṇo hoti,
snp3.9:8.4	bhāradvājo iti bhāsati;
snp3.9:8.5	Ahañca kammunā brūmi,
snp3.9:8.6	evaṁ jānāhi cakkhuma.
snp3.9:9.1	Te na sakkoma saññāpetuṁ,
snp3.9:9.2	aññamaññaṁ mayaṁ ubho;
snp3.9:9.3	Bhavantaṁ puṭṭhumāgamhā,
snp3.9:9.4	sambuddhaṁ iti vissutaṁ.
snp3.9:10.1	Candaṁ yathā khayātītaṁ,
snp3.9:10.2	pecca pañjalikā janā;
snp3.9:10.3	Vandamānā namassanti,
snp3.9:10.4	evaṁ lokasmi gotamaṁ.
snp3.9:11.1	Cakkhuṁ loke samuppannaṁ,
snp3.9:11.2	mayaṁ pucchāma gotamaṁ;
snp3.9:11.3	Jātiyā brāhmaṇo hoti,
snp3.9:11.4	udāhu bhavati kammunā;
snp3.9:11.5	Ajānataṁ no pabrūhi,
snp3.9:11.6	yathā jānemu brāhmaṇaṁ”.
snp3.9:12.1	“Tesaṁ vo ahaṁ byakkhissaṁ,
snp3.9:12.2	(vāseṭṭhāti bhagavā)
snp3.9:12.3	Anupubbaṁ yathātathaṁ;
snp3.9:12.4	Jātivibhaṅgaṁ pāṇānaṁ,

snp3.10:0.1	Sutta Nipāta 3.10
snp3.10:0.2	Kokālikasutta
snp3.10:1.1	Evaṁ me sutaṁ—
snp3.10:1.2	ekaṁ samayaṁ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṁ viharati jetavane anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme.
snp3.10:1.3	Atha kho kokāliko bhikkhu yena bhagavā tenupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṁ abhivādetvā ekamantaṁ nisīdi. Ekamantaṁ nisinno kho kokāliko bhikkhu bhagavantaṁ etadavoca:
snp3.10:1.4	“pāpicchā, bhante, sāriputtamoggallānā, pāpikānaṁ icchānaṁ vasaṁ gatā”ti.
snp3.10:2.1	Evaṁ vutte, bhagavā kokālikaṁ bhikkhuṁ etadavoca:
snp3.10:2.2	“mā hevaṁ, kokālika, mā hevaṁ, kokālika.
snp3.10:2.3	Pasādehi, kokālika, sāriputtamoggallānesu cittaṁ.
snp3.10:2.4	Pesalā sāriputtamoggallānā”ti.
snp3.10:3.1	Dutiyampi kho …pe…
snp3.10:3.2	tatiyampi kho kokāliko bhikkhu bhagavantaṁ etadavoca:
snp3.10:3.3	“kiñcāpi me, bhante, bhagavā saddhāyiko paccayiko, atha kho pāpicchāva sāriputtamoggallānā, pāpikānaṁ icchānaṁ vasaṁ gatā”ti.
snp3.10:3.4	Tatiyampi kho bhagavā kokālikaṁ bhikkhuṁ etadavoca:
snp3.10:3.5	“mā hevaṁ, kokālika, mā hevaṁ, kokālika.
snp3.10:3.6	Pasādehi, kokālika, sāriputtamoggallānesu cittaṁ.
snp3.10:3.7	Pesalā sāriputtamoggallānā”ti.
snp3.10:4.1	Atha kho kokāliko bhikkhu uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṁ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṁ katvā pakkāmi.
snp3.10:4.2	Acirappakkantassa ca kokālikassa bhikkhuno sāsapamattīhi piḷakāhi sabbo kāyo phuṭo ahosi;
snp3.10:4.3	sāsapamattiyo hutvā muggamattiyo ahesuṁ; muggamattiyo hutvā kaḷāyamattiyo ahesuṁ; kaḷāyamattiyo hutvā kolaṭṭhimattiyo ahesuṁ; kolaṭṭhimattiyo hutvā kolamattiyo ahesuṁ; kolamattiyo hutvā āmalakamattiyo ahesuṁ; āmalakamattiyo hutvā beḷuvasalāṭukamattiyo ahesuṁ; beḷuvasalāṭukamattiyo hutvā billamattiyo ahesuṁ; billamattiyo hutvā pabhijjiṁsu; pubbañca lohitañca pagghariṁsu.
snp3.10:4.4	Atha kho kokāliko bhikkhu tenevābādhena kālamakāsi.
snp3.10:4.5	Kālaṅkato ca kokāliko bhikkhu padumaṁ nirayaṁ upapajji sāriputtamoggallānesu cittaṁ āghātetvā.
snp3.10:5.1	Atha kho brahmā sahampati abhikkantāya rattiyā abhikkantavaṇṇo kevalakappaṁ jetavanaṁ obhāsetvā yena bhagavā tenupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṁ abhivādetvā ekamantaṁ aṭṭhāsi. Ekamantaṁ ṭhito kho brahmā sahampati bhagavantaṁ etadavoca:
snp3.10:5.2	“kokāliko, bhante, bhikkhu kālaṅkato;
snp3.10:5.3	kālaṅkato ca, bhante, kokāliko bhikkhu padumaṁ nirayaṁ upapanno sāriputtamoggallānesu cittaṁ āghātetvā”ti.
snp3.10:5.4	Idamavoca brahmā sahampati;
snp3.10:5.5	idaṁ vatvā bhagavantaṁ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṁ katvā tatthevantaradhāyi.
snp3.10:6.1	Atha kho bhagavā tassā rattiyā accayena bhikkhū āmantesi:
snp3.10:6.2	“imaṁ, bhikkhave, rattiṁ brahmā sahampati abhikkantāya rattiyā …pe…
snp3.10:6.3	idamavoca, bhikkhave, brahmā sahampati, idaṁ vatvā maṁ padakkhiṇaṁ katvā tatthevantaradhāyī”ti.
snp3.10:7.1	Evaṁ vutte, aññataro bhikkhu bhagavantaṁ etadavoca:
snp3.10:7.2	“kīvadīghaṁ nu kho, bhante, padume niraye āyuppamāṇan”ti?
snp3.10:7.3	“Dīghaṁ kho, bhikkhu, padume niraye āyuppamāṇaṁ;
snp3.10:7.4	taṁ na sukaraṁ saṅkhātuṁ ettakāni vassāni iti vā ettakāni vassasatāni iti vā ettakāni vassasahassāni iti vā ettakāni vassasatasahassāni iti vā”ti.
snp3.10:7.5	“Sakkā pana, bhante, upamā kātun”ti?
snp3.10:7.6	“Sakkā, bhikkhū”ti bhagavā avoca:
snp3.10:8.1	“Seyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsatikhāriko kosalako tilavāho; tato puriso vassasatassa vassasatassa accayena ekamekaṁ tilaṁ uddhareyya.
snp3.10:8.2	Khippataraṁ kho so bhikkhu vīsatikhāriko kosalako tilavāho iminā upakkamena parikkhayaṁ pariyādānaṁ gaccheyya, na tveva eko abbudo nirayo.
snp3.10:8.3	Seyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati abbudā nirayā evameko nirabbudo nirayo.
snp3.10:8.4	Seyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati nirabbudā nirayā evameko ababo nirayo.
snp3.10:8.5	Seyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati ababā nirayā evameko aṭaṭo nirayo.
snp3.10:8.6	Seyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati aṭaṭā nirayā evameko ahaho nirayo.
snp3.10:8.7	Seyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati ahahā nirayā evameko kumudo nirayo.
snp3.10:8.8	Seyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati kumudā nirayā evameko sogandhiko nirayo.
snp3.10:8.9	Seyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati sogandhikā nirayā evameko uppalako nirayo.
snp3.10:8.10	Seyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati uppalakā nirayā evameko puṇḍarīko nirayo.
snp3.10:8.11	Seyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati puṇḍarīkā nirayā evameko padumo nirayo.
snp3.10:8.12	Padumaṁ kho pana bhikkhu nirayaṁ kokāliko bhikkhu upapanno sāriputtamoggallānesu cittaṁ āghātetvā”ti.
snp3.10:8.13	Idamavoca bhagavā.
snp3.10:8.14	Idaṁ vatvāna sugato athāparaṁ etadavoca satthā:

snp3.10:10.1	Yo nindiyaṁ pasaṁsati,

snp3.10:10.4	Kalinā tena sukhaṁ na vindati.
snp3.10:11.1	Appamatto ayaṁ kali,
snp3.10:11.2	Yo akkhesu dhanaparājayo;

snp3.10:11.4	Ayameva mahattaro kali;
snp3.10:11.5	Yo sugatesu manaṁ padosaye.
snp3.10:12.1	Sataṁ sahassānaṁ nirabbudānaṁ,

snp3.11:3.1	Yadāpi āsī asurehi saṅgamo,
snp3.11:3.2	Jayo surānaṁ asurā parājitā;

snp3.11:4.1	Seḷenti gāyanti ca vādayanti ca,
snp3.11:4.2	Bhujāni phoṭenti ca naccayanti ca;
snp3.11:4.3	Pucchāmi vohaṁ merumuddhavāsine,
snp3.11:4.4	Dhunātha me saṁsayaṁ khippa mārisā”.
snp3.11:5.1	“So bodhisatto ratanavaro atulyo,
snp3.11:5.2	Manussaloke hitasukhatthāya jāto;
snp3.11:5.3	Sakyāna gāme janapade lumbineyye,
snp3.11:5.4	Tenamha tuṭṭhā atiriva kalyarūpā.
snp3.11:6.1	So sabbasattuttamo aggapuggalo,
snp3.11:6.2	Narāsabho sabbapajānamuttamo;
snp3.11:6.3	Vattessati cakkamisivhaye vane,
snp3.11:6.4	Nadaṁva sīho balavā migābhibhū”.
snp3.11:7.1	Taṁ saddaṁ sutvā turitamavasarī so,
snp3.11:7.2	Suddhodanassa tada bhavanaṁ upāvisi;
snp3.11:7.3	Nisajja tattha idamavocāsi sakye,
snp3.11:7.4	“Kuhiṁ kumāro ahamapi daṭṭhukāmo”.
snp3.11:8.1	Tato kumāraṁ jalitamiva suvaṇṇaṁ,
snp3.11:8.2	Ukkāmukheva sukusalasampahaṭṭhaṁ;
snp3.11:8.3	Daddallamānaṁ siriyā anomavaṇṇaṁ,
snp3.11:8.4	Dassesu puttaṁ asitavhayassa sakyā.
snp3.11:9.1	Disvā kumāraṁ sikhimiva pajjalantaṁ,

snp3.11:12.2	Jigīsako lakkhaṇamantapāragū;
snp3.11:12.3	Pasannacitto giramabbhudīrayi,

snp3.11:13.1	Athattano gamanamanussaranto,
snp3.11:13.2	Akalyarūpo gaḷayati assukāni;

snp3.12:0.1	Sutta Nipāta 3.12
snp3.12:0.2	Dvayatānupassanāsutta
snp3.12:1.1	Evaṁ me sutaṁ—
snp3.12:1.2	ekaṁ samayaṁ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṁ viharati pubbārāme migāramātupāsāde.
snp3.12:1.3	Tena kho pana samayena bhagavā tadahuposathe pannarase puṇṇāya puṇṇamāya rattiyā bhikkhusaṅghaparivuto abbhokāse nisinno hoti.
snp3.12:1.4	Atha kho bhagavā tuṇhībhūtaṁ tuṇhībhūtaṁ bhikkhusaṅghaṁ anuviloketvā bhikkhū āmantesi:
snp3.12:2.1	“‘Ye te, bhikkhave, kusalā dhammā ariyā niyyānikā sambodhagāmino, tesaṁ vo, bhikkhave, kusalānaṁ dhammānaṁ ariyānaṁ niyyānikānaṁ sambodhagāmīnaṁ kā upanisā savanāyā’ti, iti ce, bhikkhave, pucchitāro assu, te evamassu vacanīyā:
snp3.12:2.2	‘yāvadeva dvayatānaṁ dhammānaṁ yathābhūtaṁ ñāṇāyā’ti.

snp4.8:3.2	Pasaṁsamicchaṁ vinighāti hoti;
snp4.8:3.3	Apāhatasmiṁ pana maṅku hoti,
snp4.8:3.4	Nindāya so kuppati randhamesī.
snp4.8:4.1	Yamassa vādaṁ parihīnamāhu,
snp4.8:4.2	Apāhataṁ pañhavimaṁsakāse;
snp4.8:4.3	Paridevati socati hīnavādo,
snp4.8:4.4	Upaccagā manti anutthunāti.
snp4.8:5.1	Ete vivādā samaṇesu jātā,
snp4.8:5.2	Etesu ugghātinighāti hoti;
snp4.8:5.3	Etampi disvā virame kathojjaṁ,
snp4.8:5.4	Na haññadatthatthi pasaṁsalābhā.
snp4.8:6.1	Pasaṁsito vā pana tattha hoti,
snp4.8:6.2	Akkhāya vādaṁ parisāya majjhe;
snp4.8:6.3	So hassatī unnamatī ca tena,
snp4.8:6.4	Pappuyya tamatthaṁ yathā mano ahu.
snp4.8:7.1	Yā unnatī sāssa vighātabhūmi,
snp4.8:7.2	Mānātimānaṁ vadate paneso;
snp4.8:7.3	Etampi disvā na vivādayetha,
snp4.8:7.4	Na hi tena suddhiṁ kusalā vadanti.
snp4.8:8.1	Sūro yathā rājakhādāya puṭṭho,
snp4.8:8.2	Abhigajjameti paṭisūramicchaṁ;
snp4.8:8.3	Yeneva so tena palehi sūra,
snp4.8:8.4	Pubbeva natthi yadidaṁ yudhāya.
snp4.8:9.1	Ye diṭṭhimuggayha vivādayanti,
snp4.8:9.2	Idameva saccanti ca vādayanti;
snp4.8:9.3	Te tvaṁ vadassū na hi tedha atthi,
snp4.8:9.4	Vādamhi jāte paṭisenikattā.
snp4.8:10.1	Visenikatvā pana ye caranti,
snp4.8:10.2	Diṭṭhīhi diṭṭhiṁ avirujjhamānā;
snp4.8:10.3	Tesu tvaṁ kiṁ labhetho pasūra,
snp4.8:10.4	Yesīdha natthī paramuggahītaṁ.
snp4.8:11.1	Atha tvaṁ pavitakkamāgamā,
snp4.8:11.2	Manasā diṭṭhigatāni cintayanto;
snp4.8:11.3	Dhonena yugaṁ samāgamā,
snp4.8:11.4	Na hi tvaṁ sakkhasi sampayātaveti.
snp4.8:11.5	Pasūrasuttaṁ aṭṭhamaṁ.
snp4.9:0.1	Sutta Nipāta 4.9
snp4.9:0.2	Māgaṇḍiyasutta
snp4.9:1.1	“Disvāna taṇhaṁ aratiṁ ragañca,
snp4.9:1.2	Nāhosi chando api methunasmiṁ;
snp4.9:1.3	Kimevidaṁ muttakarīsapuṇṇaṁ,
snp4.9:1.4	Pādāpi naṁ samphusituṁ na icche”.
snp4.9:2.1	“Etādisañce ratanaṁ na icchasi,
snp4.9:2.2	Nāriṁ narindehi bahūhi patthitaṁ;
snp4.9:2.3	Diṭṭhigataṁ sīlavataṁ nu jīvitaṁ,
snp4.9:2.4	Bhavūpapattiñca vadesi kīdisaṁ”.
snp4.9:3.1	“Idaṁ vadāmīti na tassa hoti,
snp4.9:3.2	(māgaṇḍiyāti bhagavā)
snp4.9:3.3	Dhammesu niccheyya samuggahītaṁ;
snp4.9:3.4	Passañca diṭṭhīsu anuggahāya,
snp4.9:3.5	Ajjhattasantiṁ pacinaṁ adassaṁ”.
snp4.9:4.1	“Vinicchayā yāni pakappitāni,
snp4.9:4.2	(iti māgaṇḍiyo)
snp4.9:4.3	Te ve munī brūsi anuggahāya;
snp4.9:4.4	Ajjhattasantīti yametamatthaṁ,
snp4.9:4.5	Kathaṁ nu dhīrehi paveditaṁ taṁ”.
snp4.9:5.1	“Na diṭṭhiyā na sutiyā na ñāṇena,
snp4.9:5.2	(māgaṇḍiyāti bhagavā)
snp4.9:5.3	Sīlabbatenāpi na suddhimāha;
snp4.9:5.4	Adiṭṭhiyā assutiyā añāṇā,
snp4.9:5.5	Asīlatā abbatā nopi tena;
snp4.9:5.6	Ete ca nissajja anuggahāya,
snp4.9:5.7	Santo anissāya bhavaṁ na jappe”.
snp4.9:6.1	“No ce kira diṭṭhiyā na sutiyā na ñāṇena,
snp4.9:6.2	(iti māgaṇḍiyo)
snp4.9:6.3	Sīlabbatenāpi na suddhimāha;
snp4.9:6.4	Adiṭṭhiyā assutiyā añāṇā,
snp4.9:6.5	Asīlatā abbatā nopi tena;
snp4.9:6.6	Maññāmahaṁ momuhameva dhammaṁ,
snp4.9:6.7	Diṭṭhiyā eke paccenti suddhiṁ”.
snp4.9:7.1	“Diṭṭhañca nissāya anupucchamāno,
snp4.9:7.2	(māgaṇḍiyāti bhagavā)
snp4.9:7.3	Samuggahītesu pamohamāgā;
snp4.9:7.4	Ito ca nāddakkhi aṇumpi saññaṁ,
snp4.9:7.5	Tasmā tuvaṁ momuhato dahāsi.
snp4.9:8.1	Samo visesī uda vā nihīno,
snp4.9:8.2	Yo maññati so vivadetha tena;
snp4.9:8.3	Tīsu vidhāsu avikampamāno,
snp4.9:8.4	Samo visesīti na tassa hoti.
snp4.9:9.1	Saccanti so brāhmaṇo kiṁ vadeyya,
snp4.9:9.2	Musāti vā so vivadetha kena;
snp4.9:9.3	Yasmiṁ samaṁ visamaṁ vāpi natthi,
snp4.9:9.4	Sa kena vādaṁ paṭisaṁyujeyya.
snp4.9:10.1	Okaṁ pahāya aniketasārī,
snp4.9:10.2	Gāme akubbaṁ muni santhavāni;
snp4.9:10.3	Kāmehi ritto apurakkharāno,
snp4.9:10.4	Kathaṁ na viggayha janena kayirā.
snp4.9:11.1	Yehi vivitto vicareyya loke,
snp4.9:11.2	Na tāni uggayha vadeyya nāgo;
snp4.9:11.3	Jalambujaṁ kaṇḍakavārijaṁ yathā,
snp4.9:11.4	Jalena paṅkena canūpalittaṁ;
snp4.9:11.5	Evaṁ munī santivādo agiddho,
snp4.9:11.6	Kāme ca loke ca anūpalitto.
snp4.9:12.1	Na vedagū diṭṭhiyāyako na mutiyā,
snp4.9:12.2	Sa mānameti na hi tammayo so;
snp4.9:12.3	Na kammunā nopi sutena neyyo,
snp4.9:12.4	Anūpanīto sa nivesanesu.
snp4.9:13.1	Saññāvirattassa na santi ganthā,
snp4.9:13.2	Paññāvimuttassa na santi mohā;
snp4.9:13.3	Saññañca diṭṭhiñca ye aggahesuṁ,
snp4.9:13.4	Te ghaṭṭayantā vicaranti loke”ti.
snp4.9:13.5	Māgaṇḍiyasuttaṁ navamaṁ.
snp4.10:0.1	Sutta Nipāta 4.10
snp4.10:0.2	Purābhedasutta
snp4.10:1.1	“Kathaṁdassī kathaṁsīlo,
snp4.10:1.2	upasantoti vuccati;
snp4.10:1.3	Taṁ me gotama pabrūhi,
snp4.10:1.4	pucchito uttamaṁ naraṁ”.
snp4.10:2.1	“Vītataṇho purā bhedā,
snp4.10:2.2	(iti bhagavā)
snp4.10:2.3	Pubbamantamanissito;
snp4.10:2.4	Vemajjhe nupasaṅkheyyo,
snp4.10:2.5	Tassa natthi purakkhataṁ.
snp4.10:3.1	Akkodhano asantāsī,
snp4.10:3.2	avikatthī akukkuco;
snp4.10:3.3	Mantabhāṇī anuddhato,
snp4.10:3.4	sa ve vācāyato muni.
snp4.10:4.1	Nirāsatti anāgate,
snp4.10:4.2	atītaṁ nānusocati;
snp4.10:4.3	Vivekadassī phassesu,
snp4.10:4.4	diṭṭhīsu ca na nīyati.
snp4.10:5.1	Patilīno akuhako,
snp4.10:5.2	apihālu amaccharī;
snp4.10:5.3	Appagabbho ajeguccho,
snp4.10:5.4	pesuṇeyye ca no yuto.
snp4.10:6.1	Sātiyesu anassāvī,
snp4.10:6.2	atimāne ca no yuto;
snp4.10:6.3	Saṇho ca paṭibhānavā,
snp4.10:6.4	na saddho na virajjati.
snp4.10:7.1	Lābhakamyā na sikkhati,
snp4.10:7.2	alābhe ca na kuppati;
snp4.10:7.3	Aviruddho ca taṇhāya,
snp4.10:7.4	rasesu nānugijjhati.
snp4.10:8.1	Upekkhako sadā sato,
snp4.10:8.2	na loke maññate samaṁ;
snp4.10:8.3	Na visesī na nīceyyo,
snp4.10:8.4	tassa no santi ussadā.
snp4.10:9.1	Yassa nissayanā natthi,
snp4.10:9.2	ñatvā dhammaṁ anissito;
snp4.10:9.3	Bhavāya vibhavāya vā,
snp4.10:9.4	taṇhā yassa na vijjati.
snp4.10:10.1	Taṁ brūmi upasantoti,
snp4.10:10.2	Kāmesu anapekkhinaṁ;
snp4.10:10.3	Ganthā tassa na vijjanti,
snp4.10:10.4	Atarī so visattikaṁ.
snp4.10:11.1	Na tassa puttā pasavo,
snp4.10:11.2	Khettaṁ vatthuñca vijjati;
snp4.10:11.3	Attā vāpi nirattā vā,
snp4.10:11.4	Na tasmiṁ upalabbhati.
snp4.10:12.1	Yena naṁ vajjuṁ puthujjanā,
snp4.10:12.2	Atho samaṇabrāhmaṇā;
snp4.10:12.3	Taṁ tassa apurakkhataṁ,
snp4.10:12.4	Tasmā vādesu nejati.
snp4.10:13.1	Vītagedho amaccharī,
snp4.10:13.2	Na ussesu vadate muni;
snp4.10:13.3	Na samesu na omesu,
snp4.10:13.4	Kappaṁ neti akappiyo.
snp4.10:14.1	Yassa loke sakaṁ natthi,
snp4.10:14.2	Asatā ca na socati;
snp4.10:14.3	Dhammesu ca na gacchati,
snp4.10:14.4	Sa ve santoti vuccatī”ti.
snp4.10:14.5	Purābhedasuttaṁ dasamaṁ.

snp4.11:0.2	Kalahavivādasutta
snp4.11:1.1	“Kutopahūtā kalahā vivādā,
snp4.11:1.2	Paridevasokā sahamaccharā ca;
snp4.11:1.3	Mānātimānā sahapesuṇā ca,
snp4.11:1.4	Kutopahūtā te tadiṅgha brūhi”.
snp4.11:2.1	“Piyappahūtā kalahā vivādā,
snp4.11:2.2	Paridevasokā sahamaccharā ca;

snp4.11:2.4	Maccherayuttā kalahā vivādā;
snp4.11:2.5	Vivādajātesu ca pesuṇāni”.
snp4.11:3.1	“Piyā su lokasmiṁ kutonidānā,

snp4.11:4.2	Ye cāpi lobhā vicaranti loke;
snp4.11:4.3	Āsā ca niṭṭhā ca itonidānā,
snp4.11:4.4	Ye samparāyāya narassa honti”.
snp4.11:5.1	“Chando nu lokasmiṁ kutonidāno,
snp4.11:5.2	Vinicchayā cāpi kutopahūtā;

snp4.11:5.4	Ye vāpi dhammā samaṇena vuttā”.
snp4.11:6.1	“Sātaṁ asātanti yamāhu loke,
snp4.11:6.2	Tamūpanissāya pahoti chando;
snp4.11:6.3	Rūpesu disvā vibhavaṁ bhavañca,
snp4.11:6.4	Vinicchayaṁ kubbati jantu loke.
snp4.11:7.1	Kodho mosavajjañca kathaṅkathā ca,
snp4.11:7.2	Etepi dhammā dvayameva sante;
snp4.11:7.3	Kathaṅkathī ñāṇapathāya sikkhe,
snp4.11:7.4	Ñatvā pavuttā samaṇena dhammā”.
snp4.11:8.1	“Sātaṁ asātañca kutonidānā,
snp4.11:8.2	Kismiṁ asante na bhavanti hete;
snp4.11:8.3	Vibhavaṁ bhavañcāpi yametamatthaṁ,
snp4.11:8.4	Etaṁ me pabrūhi yatonidānaṁ”.
snp4.11:9.1	“Phassanidānaṁ sātaṁ asātaṁ,
snp4.11:9.2	Phasse asante na bhavanti hete;
snp4.11:9.3	Vibhavaṁ bhavañcāpi yametamatthaṁ,
snp4.11:9.4	Etaṁ te pabrūmi itonidānaṁ”.
snp4.11:10.1	“Phasso nu lokasmi kutonidāno,
snp4.11:10.2	Pariggahā cāpi kutopahūtā;
snp4.11:10.3	Kismiṁ asante na mamattamatthi,
snp4.11:10.4	Kismiṁ vibhūte na phusanti phassā”.
snp4.11:11.1	“Nāmañca rūpañca paṭicca phasso,
snp4.11:11.2	Icchānidānāni pariggahāni;
snp4.11:11.3	Icchāyasantyā na mamattamatthi,
snp4.11:11.4	Rūpe vibhūte na phusanti phassā”.
snp4.11:12.1	“Kathaṁ sametassa vibhoti rūpaṁ,
snp4.11:12.2	Sukhaṁ dukhañcāpi kathaṁ vibhoti;
snp4.11:12.3	Etaṁ me pabrūhi yathā vibhoti,
snp4.11:12.4	Taṁ jāniyāmāti me mano ahu”.
snp4.11:13.1	“Na saññasaññī na visaññasaññī,
snp4.11:13.2	Nopi asaññī na vibhūtasaññī;
snp4.11:13.3	Evaṁ sametassa vibhoti rūpaṁ,
snp4.11:13.4	Saññānidānā hi papañcasaṅkhā”.
snp4.11:14.1	“Yaṁ taṁ apucchimha akittayī no,
snp4.11:14.2	Aññaṁ taṁ pucchāma tadiṅgha brūhi;
snp4.11:14.3	Ettāvataggaṁ nu vadanti heke,
snp4.11:14.4	Yakkhassa suddhiṁ idha paṇḍitāse;
snp4.11:14.5	Udāhu aññampi vadanti etto”.
snp4.11:15.1	“Ettāvataggampi vadanti heke,
snp4.11:15.2	Yakkhassa suddhiṁ idha paṇḍitāse;
snp4.11:15.3	Tesaṁ paneke samayaṁ vadanti,
snp4.11:15.4	Anupādisese kusalā vadānā.
snp4.11:16.1	Ete ca ñatvā upanissitāti,
snp4.11:16.2	Ñatvā munī nissaye so vimaṁsī;
snp4.11:16.3	Ñatvā vimutto na vivādameti,
snp4.11:16.4	Bhavābhavāya na sameti dhīro”ti.
snp4.11:16.5	Kalahavivādasuttaṁ ekādasamaṁ.
snp4.12:0.1	Sutta Nipāta 4.12
snp4.12:0.2	Cūḷabyūhasutta
snp4.12:1.1	“Sakaṁsakaṁdiṭṭhiparibbasānā,
snp4.12:1.2	Viggayha nānā kusalā vadanti;
snp4.12:1.3	Yo evaṁ jānāti sa vedi dhammaṁ,
snp4.12:1.4	Idaṁ paṭikkosamakevalī so.
snp4.12:2.1	Evampi viggayha vivādayanti,
snp4.12:2.2	Bālo paro akkusaloti cāhu;
snp4.12:2.3	Sacco nu vādo katamo imesaṁ,
snp4.12:2.4	Sabbeva hīme kusalāvadānā”.
snp4.12:3.1	“Parassa ce dhammamanānujānaṁ,
snp4.12:3.2	Bālomako hoti nihīnapañño;
snp4.12:3.3	Sabbeva bālā sunihīnapaññā,
snp4.12:3.4	Sabbevime diṭṭhiparibbasānā.
snp4.12:4.1	Sandiṭṭhiyā ceva na vīvadātā,
snp4.12:4.2	Saṁsuddhapaññā kusalā mutīmā;
snp4.12:4.3	Na tesaṁ koci parihīnapañño,
snp4.12:4.4	Diṭṭhī hi tesampi tathā samattā.
snp4.12:5.1	Na vāhametaṁ tathiyanti brūmi,
snp4.12:5.2	Yamāhu bālā mithu aññamaññaṁ;
snp4.12:5.3	Sakaṁ sakaṁ diṭṭhimakaṁsu saccaṁ,
snp4.12:5.4	Tasmā hi bāloti paraṁ dahanti”.
snp4.12:6.1	“Yamāhu saccaṁ tathiyanti eke,
snp4.12:6.2	Tamāhu aññe tucchaṁ musāti;
snp4.12:6.3	Evampi vigayha vivādayanti,
snp4.12:6.4	Kasmā na ekaṁ samaṇā vadanti”.
snp4.12:7.1	“Ekañhi saccaṁ na dutīyamatthi,
snp4.12:7.2	Yasmiṁ pajā no vivade pajānaṁ;
snp4.12:7.3	Nānā te saccāni sayaṁ thunanti,
snp4.12:7.4	Tasmā na ekaṁ samaṇā vadanti”.
snp4.12:8.1	“Kasmā nu saccāni vadanti nānā,
snp4.12:8.2	Pavādiyāse kusalāvadānā;
snp4.12:8.3	Saccāni sutāni bahūni nānā,
snp4.12:8.4	Udāhu te takkamanussaranti”.
snp4.12:9.1	“Na heva saccāni bahūni nānā,
snp4.12:9.2	Aññatra saññāya niccāni loke;
snp4.12:9.3	Takkañca diṭṭhīsu pakappayitvā,
snp4.12:9.4	Saccaṁ musāti dvayadhammamāhu.
snp4.12:10.1	Diṭṭhe sute sīlavate mute vā,
snp4.12:10.2	Ete ca nissāya vimānadassī;
snp4.12:10.3	Vinicchaye ṭhatvā pahassamāno,
snp4.12:10.4	Bālo paro akkusaloti cāha.
snp4.12:11.1	Yeneva bāloti paraṁ dahāti,
snp4.12:11.2	Tenātumānaṁ kusaloti cāha;
snp4.12:11.3	Sayamattanā so kusalāvadāno,
snp4.12:11.4	Aññaṁ vimāneti tadeva pāva.
snp4.12:12.1	Atisāradiṭṭhiyā so samatto,
snp4.12:12.2	Mānena matto paripuṇṇamānī;
snp4.12:12.3	Sayameva sāmaṁ manasābhisitto,
snp4.12:12.4	Diṭṭhī hi sā tassa tathā samattā.
snp4.12:13.1	Parassa ce hi vacasā nihīno,
snp4.12:13.2	Tumo sahā hoti nihīnapañño;
snp4.12:13.3	Atha ce sayaṁ vedagū hoti dhīro,
snp4.12:13.4	Na koci bālo samaṇesu atthi.
snp4.12:14.1	Aññaṁ ito yābhivadanti dhammaṁ,
snp4.12:14.2	Aparaddhā suddhimakevalī te;
snp4.12:14.3	Evampi titthyā puthuso vadanti,
snp4.12:14.4	Sandiṭṭhirāgena hi tebhirattā.
snp4.12:15.1	Idheva suddhiṁ iti vādayanti,
snp4.12:15.2	Nāññesu dhammesu visuddhimāhu;
snp4.12:15.3	Evampi titthyā puthuso niviṭṭhā,
snp4.12:15.4	Sakāyane tattha daḷhaṁ vadānā.
snp4.12:16.1	Sakāyane vāpi daḷhaṁ vadāno,
snp4.12:16.2	Kamettha bāloti paraṁ daheyya;
snp4.12:16.3	Sayaṁva so medhagamāvaheyya,
snp4.12:16.4	Paraṁ vadaṁ bālamasuddhidhammaṁ.
snp4.12:17.1	Vinicchaye ṭhatvā sayaṁ pamāya,
snp4.12:17.2	Uddhaṁsa lokasmiṁ vivādameti;
snp4.12:17.3	Hitvāna sabbāni vinicchayāni,
snp4.12:17.4	Na medhagaṁ kubbati jantu loke”ti.
snp4.12:17.5	Cūḷabyūhasuttaṁ dvādasamaṁ.
snp4.13:0.1	Sutta Nipāta 4.13
snp4.13:0.2	Mahābyūhasutta
snp4.13:1.1	“Ye kecime diṭṭhiparibbasānā,
snp4.13:1.2	Idameva saccanti vivādayanti;
snp4.13:1.3	Sabbeva te nindamanvānayanti,
snp4.13:1.4	Atho pasaṁsampi labhanti tattha”.
snp4.13:2.1	“Appañhi etaṁ na alaṁ samāya,
snp4.13:2.2	Duve vivādassa phalāni brūmi;
snp4.13:2.3	Etampi disvā na vivādayetha,
snp4.13:2.4	Khemābhipassaṁ avivādabhūmiṁ”.
snp4.13:3.1	“Yā kācimā sammutiyo puthujjā,
snp4.13:3.2	Sabbāva etā na upeti vidvā;
snp4.13:3.3	Anūpayo so upayaṁ kimeyya,
snp4.13:3.4	Diṭṭhe sute khantimakubbamāno”.
snp4.13:4.1	“Sīluttamā saññamenāhu suddhiṁ,
snp4.13:4.2	Vataṁ samādāya upaṭṭhitāse;
snp4.13:4.3	Idheva sikkhema athassa suddhiṁ,
snp4.13:4.4	Bhavūpanītā kusalāvadānā.
snp4.13:5.1	Sace cuto sīlavatato hoti,
snp4.13:5.2	Pavedhatī kamma virādhayitvā;
snp4.13:5.3	Pajappatī patthayatī ca suddhiṁ,
snp4.13:5.4	Satthāva hīno pavasaṁ gharamhā.
snp4.13:6.1	Sīlabbataṁ vāpi pahāya sabbaṁ,
snp4.13:6.2	Kammañca sāvajjanavajjametaṁ;
snp4.13:6.3	Suddhiṁ asuddhinti apatthayāno,
snp4.13:6.4	Virato care santimanuggahāya.
snp4.13:7.1	Tamūpanissāya jigucchitaṁ vā,
snp4.13:7.2	Atha vāpi diṭṭhaṁ va sutaṁ mutaṁ vā;
snp4.13:7.3	Uddhaṁsarā suddhimanutthunanti,
snp4.13:7.4	Avītataṇhāse bhavābhavesu.
snp4.13:8.1	Patthayamānassa hi jappitāni,
snp4.13:8.2	Pavedhitaṁ vāpi pakappitesu;
snp4.13:8.3	Cutūpapāto idha yassa natthi,
snp4.13:8.4	Sa kena vedheyya kuhiṁ va jappe”.
snp4.13:9.1	“Yamāhu dhammaṁ paramanti eke,
snp4.13:9.2	Tameva hīnanti panāhu aññe;
snp4.13:9.3	Sacco nu vādo katamo imesaṁ,
snp4.13:9.4	Sabbeva hīme kusalāvadānā”.
snp4.13:10.1	“Sakañhi dhammaṁ paripuṇṇamāhu,
snp4.13:10.2	Aññassa dhammaṁ pana hīnamāhu;
snp4.13:10.3	Evampi viggayha vivādayanti,
snp4.13:10.4	Sakaṁ sakaṁ sammutimāhu saccaṁ.
snp4.13:11.1	Parassa ce vambhayitena hīno,
snp4.13:11.2	Na koci dhammesu visesi assa;
snp4.13:11.3	Puthū hi aññassa vadanti dhammaṁ,
snp4.13:11.4	Nihīnato samhi daḷhaṁ vadānā.
snp4.13:12.1	Saddhammapūjāpi nesaṁ tatheva,
snp4.13:12.2	Yathā pasaṁsanti sakāyanāni;
snp4.13:12.3	Sabbeva vādā tathiyā bhaveyyuṁ,
snp4.13:12.4	Suddhī hi nesaṁ paccattameva.
snp4.13:13.1	Na brāhmaṇassa paraneyyamatthi,
snp4.13:13.2	Dhammesu niccheyya samuggahītaṁ;
snp4.13:13.3	Tasmā vivādāni upātivatto,
snp4.13:13.4	Na hi seṭṭhato passati dhammamaññaṁ.
snp4.13:14.1	Jānāmi passāmi tatheva etaṁ,
snp4.13:14.2	Diṭṭhiyā eke paccenti suddhiṁ;
snp4.13:14.3	Addakkhi ce kiñhi tumassa tena,
snp4.13:14.4	Atisitvā aññena vadanti suddhiṁ.
snp4.13:15.1	Passaṁ naro dakkhati nāmarūpaṁ,
snp4.13:15.2	Disvāna vā ñassati tānimeva;
snp4.13:15.3	Kāmaṁ bahuṁ passatu appakaṁ vā,
snp4.13:15.4	Na hi tena suddhiṁ kusalā vadanti.
snp4.13:16.1	Nivissavādī na hi subbināyo,
snp4.13:16.2	Pakappitaṁ diṭṭhi purakkharāno;
snp4.13:16.3	Yaṁ nissito tattha subhaṁ vadāno,
snp4.13:16.4	Suddhiṁ vado tattha tathaddasā so.
snp4.13:17.1	Na brāhmaṇo kappamupeti saṅkhā,
snp4.13:17.2	Na diṭṭhisārī napi ñāṇabandhu;
snp4.13:17.3	Ñatvā ca so sammutiyo puthujjā,
snp4.13:17.4	Upekkhatī uggahaṇanti maññe.
snp4.13:18.1	Vissajja ganthāni munīdha loke,
snp4.13:18.2	Vivādajātesu na vaggasārī;
snp4.13:18.3	Santo asantesu upekkhako so,
snp4.13:18.4	Anuggaho uggahaṇanti maññe.
snp4.13:19.1	Pubbāsave hitvā nave akubbaṁ,
snp4.13:19.2	Na chandagū nopi nivissavādī;
snp4.13:19.3	Sa vippamutto diṭṭhigatehi dhīro,
snp4.13:19.4	Na lippati loke anattagarahī.
snp4.13:20.1	Sa sabbadhammesu visenibhūto,
snp4.13:20.2	Yaṁ kiñci diṭṭhaṁ va sutaṁ mutaṁ vā;
snp4.13:20.3	Sa pannabhāro muni vippamutto,
snp4.13:20.4	Na kappiyo nūparato na patthiyo”ti.
snp4.13:20.5	Mahābyūhasuttaṁ terasamaṁ.
snp4.14:0.1	Sutta Nipāta 4.14
snp4.14:0.2	Tuvaṭakasutta
snp4.14:1.1	“Pucchāmi taṁ ādiccabandhu,
snp4.14:1.2	Vivekaṁ santipadañca mahesi;
snp4.14:1.3	Kathaṁ disvā nibbāti bhikkhu,
snp4.14:1.4	Anupādiyāno lokasmiṁ kiñci”.
snp4.14:2.1	“Mūlaṁ papañcasaṅkhāya,
snp4.14:2.2	(iti bhagavā)
snp4.14:2.3	Mantā asmīti sabbamuparundhe;
snp4.14:2.4	Yā kāci taṇhā ajjhattaṁ,
snp4.14:2.5	Tāsaṁ vinayā sadā sato sikkhe.
snp4.14:3.1	Yaṁ kiñci dhammamabhijaññā,
snp4.14:3.2	Ajjhattaṁ atha vāpi bahiddhā;
snp4.14:3.3	Na tena thāmaṁ kubbetha,
snp4.14:3.4	Na hi sā nibbuti sataṁ vuttā.
snp4.14:4.1	Seyyo na tena maññeyya,
snp4.14:4.2	Nīceyyo atha vāpi sarikkho;
snp4.14:4.3	Phuṭṭho anekarūpehi,
snp4.14:4.4	Nātumānaṁ vikappayaṁ tiṭṭhe.
snp4.14:5.1	Ajjhattamevupasame,
snp4.14:5.2	Na aññato bhikkhu santimeseyya;
snp4.14:5.3	Ajjhattaṁ upasantassa,
snp4.14:5.4	Natthi attā kuto nirattā vā.
snp4.14:6.1	Majjhe yathā samuddassa,
snp4.14:6.2	Ūmi no jāyatī ṭhito hoti;
snp4.14:6.3	Evaṁ ṭhito anejassa,
snp4.14:6.4	Ussadaṁ bhikkhu na kareyya kuhiñci”.
snp4.14:7.1	“Akittayī vivaṭacakkhu,
snp4.14:7.2	Sakkhidhammaṁ parissayavinayaṁ;
snp4.14:7.3	Paṭipadaṁ vadehi bhaddante,
snp4.14:7.4	Pātimokkhaṁ atha vāpi samādhiṁ”.
snp4.14:8.1	“Cakkhūhi neva lolassa,
snp4.14:8.2	Gāmakathāya āvaraye sotaṁ;
snp4.14:8.3	Rase ca nānugijjheyya,
snp4.14:8.4	Na ca mamāyetha kiñci lokasmiṁ.
snp4.14:9.1	Phassena yadā phuṭṭhassa,
snp4.14:9.2	Paridevaṁ bhikkhu na kareyya kuhiñci;
snp4.14:9.3	Bhavañca nābhijappeyya,
snp4.14:9.4	Bheravesu ca na sampavedheyya.
snp4.14:10.1	Annānamatho pānānaṁ,
snp4.14:10.2	Khādanīyānaṁ athopi vatthānaṁ;
snp4.14:10.3	Laddhā na sannidhiṁ kayirā,
snp4.14:10.4	Na ca parittase tāni alabhamāno.
snp4.14:11.1	Jhāyī na pādalolassa,
snp4.14:11.2	Virame kukkuccā nappamajjeyya;
snp4.14:11.3	Athāsanesu sayanesu,
snp4.14:11.4	Appasaddesu bhikkhu vihareyya.
snp4.14:12.1	Niddaṁ na bahulīkareyya,
snp4.14:12.2	Jāgariyaṁ bhajeyya ātāpī;
snp4.14:12.3	Tandiṁ māyaṁ hassaṁ khiḍḍaṁ,
snp4.14:12.4	Methunaṁ vippajahe savibhūsaṁ.
snp4.14:13.1	Āthabbaṇaṁ supinaṁ lakkhaṇaṁ,
snp4.14:13.2	No vidahe athopi nakkhattaṁ;
snp4.14:13.3	Virutañca gabbhakaraṇaṁ,
snp4.14:13.4	Tikicchaṁ māmako na seveyya.
snp4.14:14.1	Nindāya nappavedheyya,
snp4.14:14.2	Na uṇṇameyya pasaṁsito bhikkhu;
snp4.14:14.3	Lobhaṁ saha macchariyena,
snp4.14:14.4	Kodhaṁ pesuṇiyañca panudeyya.
snp4.14:15.1	Kayavikkaye na tiṭṭheyya,
snp4.14:15.2	Upavādaṁ bhikkhu na kareyya kuhiñci;
snp4.14:15.3	Gāme ca nābhisajjeyya,
snp4.14:15.4	Lābhakamyā janaṁ na lapayeyya.
snp4.14:16.1	Na ca katthitā siyā bhikkhu,
snp4.14:16.2	Na ca vācaṁ payuttaṁ bhāseyya;
snp4.14:16.3	Pāgabbhiyaṁ na sikkheyya,
snp4.14:16.4	Kathaṁ viggāhikaṁ na kathayeyya.
snp4.14:17.1	Mosavajje na nīyetha,
snp4.14:17.2	Sampajāno saṭhāni na kayirā;
snp4.14:17.3	Atha jīvitena paññāya,
snp4.14:17.4	Sīlabbatena nāññamatimaññe.
snp4.14:18.1	Sutvā rusito bahuṁ vācaṁ,
snp4.14:18.2	Samaṇānaṁ vā puthujanānaṁ;
snp4.14:18.3	Pharusena ne na paṭivajjā,
snp4.14:18.4	Na hi santo paṭisenikaronti.
snp4.14:19.1	Etañca dhammamaññāya,
snp4.14:19.2	Vicinaṁ bhikkhu sadā sato sikkhe;
snp4.14:19.3	Santīti nibbutiṁ ñatvā,
snp4.14:19.4	Sāsane gotamassa na pamajjeyya.
snp4.14:20.1	Abhibhū hi so anabhibhūto,
snp4.14:20.2	Sakkhidhammamanītihamadassī;
snp4.14:20.3	Tasmā hi tassa bhagavato sāsane,
snp4.14:20.4	Appamatto sadā namassamanusikkhe”ti.
snp4.14:20.5	Tuvaṭakasuttaṁ cuddasamaṁ.
snp4.15:0.1	Sutta Nipāta 4.15
snp4.15:0.2	Attadaṇḍasutta
snp4.15:1.1	“Attadaṇḍā bhayaṁ jātaṁ,
snp4.15:1.2	janaṁ passatha medhagaṁ;
snp4.15:1.3	Saṁvegaṁ kittayissāmi,
snp4.15:1.4	yathā saṁvijitaṁ mayā.
snp4.15:2.1	Phandamānaṁ pajaṁ disvā,
snp4.15:2.2	macche appodake yathā;
snp4.15:2.3	Aññamaññehi byāruddhe,
snp4.15:2.4	disvā maṁ bhayamāvisi.
snp4.15:3.1	Samantamasāro loko,
snp4.15:3.2	disā sabbā sameritā;
snp4.15:3.3	Icchaṁ bhavanamattano,
snp4.15:3.4	nāddasāsiṁ anositaṁ.
snp4.15:4.1	Osāne tveva byāruddhe,
snp4.15:4.2	Disvā me aratī ahu;
snp4.15:4.3	Athettha sallamaddakkhiṁ,
snp4.15:4.4	Duddasaṁ hadayanissitaṁ.
snp4.15:5.1	Yena sallena otiṇṇo,
snp4.15:5.2	disā sabbā vidhāvati;
snp4.15:5.3	Tameva sallamabbuyha,
snp4.15:5.4	na dhāvati na sīdati.
snp4.15:6.1	Tattha sikkhānugīyanti,
snp4.15:6.2	yāni loke gadhitāni;
snp4.15:6.3	Na tesu pasuto siyā,
snp4.15:6.4	nibbijjha sabbaso kāme;
snp4.15:6.5	Sikkhe nibbānamattano.
snp4.15:7.1	Sacco siyā appagabbho,
snp4.15:7.2	amāyo rittapesuṇo;
snp4.15:7.3	Akkodhano lobhapāpaṁ,
snp4.15:7.4	vevicchaṁ vitare muni.
snp4.15:8.1	Niddaṁ tandiṁ sahe thīnaṁ,
snp4.15:8.2	pamādena na saṁvase;
snp4.15:8.3	Atimāne na tiṭṭheyya,
snp4.15:8.4	nibbānamanaso naro.
snp4.15:9.1	Mosavajje na nīyetha,
snp4.15:9.2	rūpe snehaṁ na kubbaye;
snp4.15:9.3	Mānañca parijāneyya,
snp4.15:9.4	sāhasā virato care.
snp4.15:10.1	Purāṇaṁ nābhinandeyya,
snp4.15:10.2	Nave khantiṁ na kubbaye;
snp4.15:10.3	Hiyyamāne na soceyya,
snp4.15:10.4	Ākāsaṁ na sito siyā.
snp4.15:11.1	Gedhaṁ brūmi mahoghoti,
snp4.15:11.2	Ājavaṁ brūmi jappanaṁ;
snp4.15:11.3	Ārammaṇaṁ pakappanaṁ,
snp4.15:11.4	Kāmapaṅko duraccayo.
snp4.15:12.1	Saccā avokkamma muni,
snp4.15:12.2	Thale tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇo;
snp4.15:12.3	Sabbaṁ so paṭinissajja,
snp4.15:12.4	Sa ve santoti vuccati.
snp4.15:13.1	Sa ve vidvā sa vedagū,
snp4.15:13.2	Ñatvā dhammaṁ anissito;
snp4.15:13.3	Sammā so loke iriyāno,
snp4.15:13.4	Na pihetīdha kassaci.
snp4.15:14.1	Yodha kāme accatari,
snp4.15:14.2	Saṅgaṁ loke duraccayaṁ;
snp4.15:14.3	Na so socati nājjheti,
snp4.15:14.4	Chinnasoto abandhano.
snp4.15:15.1	Yaṁ pubbe taṁ visosehi,
snp4.15:15.2	Pacchā te māhu kiñcanaṁ;
snp4.15:15.3	Majjhe ce no gahessasi,
snp4.15:15.4	Upasanto carissasi.
snp4.15:16.1	Sabbaso nāmarūpasmiṁ,
snp4.15:16.2	Yassa natthi mamāyitaṁ;
snp4.15:16.3	Asatā ca na socati,
snp4.15:16.4	Sa ve loke na jīyati.
snp4.15:17.1	Yassa natthi idaṁ meti,
snp4.15:17.2	Paresaṁ vāpi kiñcanaṁ;
snp4.15:17.3	Mamattaṁ so asaṁvindaṁ,
snp4.15:17.4	Natthi meti na socati.
snp4.15:18.1	Aniṭṭhurī ananugiddho,
snp4.15:18.2	Anejo sabbadhī samo;
snp4.15:18.3	Tamānisaṁsaṁ pabrūmi,
snp4.15:18.4	Pucchito avikampinaṁ.
snp4.15:19.1	Anejassa vijānato,
snp4.15:19.2	Natthi kāci nisaṅkhati;
snp4.15:19.3	Virato so viyārabbhā,
snp4.15:19.4	Khemaṁ passati sabbadhi.
snp4.15:20.1	Na samesu na omesu,
snp4.15:20.2	Na ussesu vadate muni;
snp4.15:20.3	Santo so vītamaccharo,
snp4.15:20.4	Nādeti na nirassatī”ti.
snp4.15:20.5	Attadaṇḍasuttaṁ pannarasamaṁ.
snp4.16:0.1	Sutta Nipāta 4.16
snp4.16:0.2	Sāriputtasutta
snp4.16:1.1	“Na me diṭṭho ito pubbe,
snp4.16:1.2	(iccāyasmā sāriputto)
snp4.16:1.3	Na suto uda kassaci;
snp4.16:1.4	Evaṁ vagguvado satthā,
snp4.16:1.5	Tusitā gaṇimāgato.
snp4.16:2.1	Sadevakassa lokassa,
snp4.16:2.2	yathā dissati cakkhumā;
snp4.16:2.3	Sabbaṁ tamaṁ vinodetvā,
snp4.16:2.4	ekova ratimajjhagā.
snp4.16:3.1	Taṁ buddhaṁ asitaṁ tādiṁ,
snp4.16:3.2	akuhaṁ gaṇimāgataṁ;
snp4.16:3.3	Bahūnamidha baddhānaṁ,
snp4.16:3.4	atthi pañhena āgamaṁ.
snp4.16:4.1	Bhikkhuno vijigucchato,
snp4.16:4.2	bhajato rittamāsanaṁ;
snp4.16:4.3	Rukkhamūlaṁ susānaṁ vā,
snp4.16:4.4	pabbatānaṁ guhāsu vā.
snp4.16:5.1	Uccāvacesu sayanesu,
snp4.16:5.2	kīvanto tattha bheravā;
snp4.16:5.3	Yehi bhikkhu na vedheyya,
snp4.16:5.4	nigghose sayanāsane.
snp4.16:6.1	Katī parissayā loke,
snp4.16:6.2	gacchato agataṁ disaṁ;
snp4.16:6.3	Ye bhikkhu abhisambhave,
snp4.16:6.4	pantamhi sayanāsane.
snp4.16:7.1	Kyāssa byappathayo assu,
snp4.16:7.2	kyāssassu idha gocarā;
snp4.16:7.3	Kāni sīlabbatānāssu,
snp4.16:7.4	pahitattassa bhikkhuno.
snp4.16:8.1	Kaṁ so sikkhaṁ samādāya,
snp4.16:8.2	ekodi nipako sato;
snp4.16:8.3	Kammāro rajatasseva,
snp4.16:8.4	niddhame malamattano”.
snp4.16:9.1	“Vijigucchamānassa yadidaṁ phāsu,
snp4.16:9.2	(sāriputtāti bhagavā)
snp4.16:9.3	Rittāsanaṁ sayanaṁ sevato ce;
snp4.16:9.4	Sambodhikāmassa yathānudhammaṁ,
snp4.16:9.5	Taṁ te pavakkhāmi yathā pajānaṁ.
snp4.16:10.1	Pañcannaṁ dhīro bhayānaṁ na bhāye,
snp4.16:10.2	Bhikkhu sato sapariyantacārī;
snp4.16:10.3	Ḍaṁsādhipātānaṁ sarīsapānaṁ,
snp4.16:10.4	Manussaphassānaṁ catuppadānaṁ.
snp4.16:11.1	Paradhammikānampi na santaseyya,
snp4.16:11.2	Disvāpi tesaṁ bahubheravāni;
snp4.16:11.3	Athāparāni abhisambhaveyya,
snp4.16:11.4	Parissayāni kusalānuesī.
snp4.16:12.1	Ātaṅkaphassena khudāya phuṭṭho,
snp4.16:12.2	Sītaṁ athuṇhaṁ adhivāsayeyya;
snp4.16:12.3	So tehi phuṭṭho bahudhā anoko,
snp4.16:12.4	Viriyaṁ parakkammadaḷhaṁ kareyya.
snp4.16:13.1	Theyyaṁ na kāre na musā bhaṇeyya,
snp4.16:13.2	Mettāya phasse tasathāvarāni;
snp4.16:13.3	Yadāvilattaṁ manaso vijaññā,
snp4.16:13.4	Kaṇhassa pakkhoti vinodayeyya.
snp4.16:14.1	Kodhātimānassa vasaṁ na gacche,
snp4.16:14.2	Mūlampi tesaṁ palikhañña tiṭṭhe;
snp4.16:14.3	Athappiyaṁ vā pana appiyaṁ vā,
snp4.16:14.4	Addhā bhavanto abhisambhaveyya.
snp4.16:15.1	Paññaṁ purakkhatvā kalyāṇapīti,
snp4.16:15.2	Vikkhambhaye tāni parissayāni;
snp4.16:15.3	Aratiṁ sahetha sayanamhi pante,
snp4.16:15.4	Caturo sahetha paridevadhamme.
snp4.16:16.1	Kiṁsū asissāmi kuva vā asissaṁ,

snp5.1:2.1	So assakassa visaye,

snp5.1:3.2	gāmo ca vipulo ahu;

snp5.1:11.4	Idaṁ vacanamabravi.

snp5.1:12.4	Ñāṇaṁ tassa na vijjati”.

snp5.1:13.4	Taṁ suṇoma vaco tava”.
snp5.1:14.1	“Ahampetaṁ na jānāmi,
snp5.1:14.2	Ñāṇamettha na vijjati;
snp5.1:14.3	Muddhani muddhādhipāte ca,
snp5.1:14.4	Jinānaṁ hettha dassanaṁ”.
snp5.1:15.1	“Atha ko carahi jānāti,
snp5.1:15.2	Asmiṁ pathavimaṇḍale;

snp5.1:15.4	Taṁ me akkhāhi devate”.
snp5.1:16.1	“Purā kapilavatthumhā,

snp5.19:28.4	Vasi bhagavā gaṇaseṭṭho.
